We aim to establish a novel ITS for Python programming that is highly adaptable, serves both as a teaching and research platform, provides interfaces to plug in hint mechanisms (e.g.\ via large language models), and works inside the particularly challenging regulatory environment of Germany, that is, conforming to the European data protection regulation, the European AI act, and ethical framework of the German Research Foundation. In this paper, we present the description of the current state of the ITS along with future development directions, as well as discuss the challenges and opportunities for improving the system.

</details>


### [40] [Univariate Channel Fusion for Multivariate Time Series Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16119)
*Fernando Moro,Vinicius M. A. Souza*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: UCF方法通过简单通道融合策略将多元时间序列转换为单变量表示，使原本为单变量设计的分类器可用于多元分类，在保持高性能的同时显著提升计算效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多元时间序列分类方法（特别是深度学习模型）通常需要高计算资源，不适合实时应用或部署在低成本硬件（如IoT设备和可穿戴系统）上。

Method: 提出Univariate Channel Fusion (UCF)方法，通过均值、中位数或动态时间规整重心等简单通道融合策略，将多元时间序列转换为单变量表示，从而可以使用任何为单变量时间序列设计的分类器。

Result: 在五个涵盖化学监测、脑机接口和人类活动分析等不同应用领域的案例研究中，UCF通常优于基线方法和为MTSC定制的最先进算法，同时在计算效率方面实现显著提升，特别适用于通道间相关性高的问题。

Conclusion: UCF提供了一种灵活且计算轻量的替代方案，能够有效处理多元时间序列分类问题，特别适合资源受限环境下的实时应用。

Abstract: Multivariate time series classification (MTSC) plays a crucial role in various domains, including biomedical signal analysis and motion monitoring. However, existing approaches, particularly deep learning models, often require high computational resources, making them unsuitable for real-time applications or deployment on low-cost hardware, such as IoT devices and wearable systems. In this paper, we propose the Univariate Channel Fusion (UCF) method to deal with MTSC efficiently. UCF transforms multivariate time series into a univariate representation through simple channel fusion strategies such as the mean, median, or dynamic time warping barycenter. This transformation enables the use of any classifier originally designed for univariate time series, providing a flexible and computationally lightweight alternative to complex models. We evaluate UCF in five case studies covering diverse application domains, including chemical monitoring, brain-computer interfaces, and human activity analysis. The results demonstrate that UCF often outperforms baseline methods and state-of-the-art algorithms tailored for MTSC, while achieving substantial gains in computational efficiency, being particularly effective in problems with high inter-channel correlation.

</details>


### [41] [Tabular foundation models for in-context prediction of molecular properties](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16123)
*Karim K. Ben Hicham,Jan G. Rittig,Martin Grohe,Alexander Mitsos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究评估了表格基础模型在分子性质预测中的应用，发现在小到中等数据量下，通过上下文学习的方式能够获得优异的预测性能，同时降低计算成本，为实际应用提供了高效替代方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分子性质预测在药物发现、催化和过程设计中至关重要，但实际应用常受限于小数据集。分子基础模型虽然提供了有前景的方向，但通常需要任务特定的微调、机器学习专业知识，且往往无法超越经典基线方法。

Method: 评估表格基础模型在低到中等数据量下的表现，使用标准制药基准和化学工程数据集。评估冻结的分子基础模型表示、经典描述符和指纹。特别关注TFMs与CheMeleon嵌入、RDKit2d和Mordred描述符的组合。

Result: TFMs在基准测试中表现出优异的预测性能，同时相比微调降低了计算成本。TFMs与CheMeleon嵌入组合在30个MoleculeACE任务中获得高达100%的胜率。分子表示是TFM性能的关键决定因素，分子基础模型嵌入和2D描述符集在许多任务上相比经典分子指纹都有显著提升。

Conclusion: 上下文学习与TFMs为实际应用中的性质预测提供了高度准确且成本效益高的替代方案，特别是在小到中等数据量情况下，能够有效解决传统方法面临的挑战。

Abstract: Accurate molecular property prediction is central to drug discovery, catalysis, and process design, yet real-world applications are often limited by small datasets. Molecular foundation models provide a promising direction by learning transferable molecular representations; however, they typically involve task-specific fine-tuning, require machine learning expertise, and often fail to outperform classical baselines. Tabular foundation models (TFMs) offer a fundamentally different paradigm: they perform predictions through in-context learning, enabling inference without task-specific training. Here, we evaluate TFMs in the low- to medium-data regime across both standardized pharmaceutical benchmarks and chemical engineering datasets. We evaluate both frozen molecular foundation model representations, as well as classical descriptors and fingerprints. Across the benchmarks, the approach shows excellent predictive performance while reducing computational cost, compared to fine-tuning, with these advantages also transferring to practical engineering data settings. In particular, combining TFMs with CheMeleon embeddings yields up to 100\% win rates on 30 MoleculeACE tasks, while compact RDKit2d and Mordred descriptors provide strong descriptor-based alternatives. Molecular representation emerges as a key determinant in TFM performance, with molecular foundation model embeddings and 2D descriptor sets both providing substantial gains over classic molecular fingerprints on many tasks. These results suggest that in-context learning with TFMs provides a highly accurate and cost-efficient alternative for property prediction in practical applications.

</details>


### [42] [Training Time Prediction for Mixed Precision-based Distributed Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16145)
*Minchul Kang,Changyong Shin,Jinwoo Jeong,Hyunho Lee,Younghun Go,Gyeongmin Kim,Gyeongsik Yang,Chuck Yoo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出精度感知的分布式深度学习训练时间预测器，解决现有方法因忽略浮点精度设置（包括混合精度）导致的预测误差问题


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式深度学习训练时间预测对资源分配、成本估算和作业调度至关重要。研究发现浮点精度设置是训练时间的关键决定因素，可导致训练时间变化达2.4倍，而现有预测方法基于静态计算图，无法捕捉精度变化（包括混合精度）

Method: 提出精度感知的分布式训练时间预测器，能够考虑不同精度设置（包括混合精度）对训练时间的影响，实现跨多种精度设置的鲁棒准确预测

Result: 实验表明，不考虑精度的训练时间预测会产生显著误差，MAPE最高达147.85%；而提出的精度感知预测器在包括混合精度在内的多种精度设置下实现鲁棒准确性，MAPE为9.8%

Conclusion: 浮点精度设置对分布式深度学习训练时间有重大影响，必须纳入预测模型；提出的精度感知预测器能有效解决这一问题，显著提升预测准确性

Abstract: Accurate prediction of training time in distributed deep learning is crucial for resource allocation, cost estimation, and job scheduling. We observe that the floating-point precision setting is a key determinant of training time, leading to training time variations of ~2.4x over its minimum. However, existing studies on distributed training time prediction rely on static model computation graphs that do not capture precision variations, including mixed precision. According to our experiments, training time prediction without considering precision results in significant prediction errors - reaching up to 147.85% in mean absolute percentage error (MAPE). To address this issue, we propose a precision-aware distributed training time predictor that achieves robust accuracy across diverse precision settings, including mixed precision, with 9.8% MAPE.

</details>


### [43] [JumpLoRA: Sparse Adapters for Continual Learning in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16171)
*Alexandra Dragomir,Ioana Pintilie,Antonio Barbalau,Marius Dragoi,Florin Brad,Cristian Daniel Paduraru,Alexandru Tifrea,Elena Burceanu,Radu Tudor Ionescu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: JumpLoRA：通过JumpReLU门控在LoRA块中自适应诱导稀疏性，实现动态参数隔离以防止任务干扰的持续学习框架


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于适配器的持续学习方法通过约束新适配器与先前适配器的关系来缓解灾难性遗忘，但存在子空间或坐标级干扰问题。需要更有效的参数隔离机制来防止任务干扰。

Method: 提出JumpLoRA框架，通过JumpReLU门控在Low-Rank Adaptation（LoRA）块中自适应诱导稀疏性，实现动态参数隔离。该方法具有高度模块化特性，可与LoRA-based持续学习方法兼容。

Result: 显著提升了IncLoRA的性能，并超越了当前最先进的持续学习方法ELLA

Conclusion: JumpLoRA通过自适应稀疏诱导机制有效防止任务干扰，为大型语言模型的持续学习提供了高效且模块化的解决方案

Abstract: Adapter-based methods have become a cost-effective approach to continual learning (CL) for Large Language Models (LLMs), by sequentially learning a low-rank update matrix for each task. To mitigate catastrophic forgetting, state-of-the-art approaches impose constraints on new adapters with respect to the previous ones, by targeting either subspace or coordinate-wise interference. In this paper, we propose JumpLoRA, a novel framework to adaptively induce sparsity in the Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) blocks through the use of JumpReLU gating. The method achieves dynamic parameter isolation, which helps prevent task interference. We demonstrate that our method is highly modular and compatible with LoRA-based CL approaches. Specifically, it significantly boosts the performance of IncLoRA and outperforms the leading state-of-the-art CL method, ELLA.

</details>


### [44] [Sketching the Readout of Large Language Models for Scalable Data Attribution and Valuation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16197)
*Yide Ran,Jianwen Xie,Minghui Wang,Wenjin Zheng,Denghui Zhang,Chuan Li,Zhaozhuo Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RISE是一种用于大语言模型数据归因和评估的轻量级方法，通过聚焦输出层的影响热点和双通道表示，显著降低了存储需求并实现了对32B参数模型的扩展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于梯度的数据归因方法在大语言模型上存在可扩展性挑战，无法处理大规模参数模型。受人类认知中决策依赖于相关记忆的聚焦读取而非重放所有路径的启发，需要开发一种更高效、可扩展的数据归因方法。

Method: RISE聚焦于输出层的影响热点，利用梯度可分解为外积形式的特点，构建双通道表示：词汇残差通道（RH）和语义投影误差通道（GH）。通过CountSketch投影对这些通道进行压缩，在保持准确归因的同时大幅减少存储需求。

Result: 在OLMo（1B-32B）和Pythia（14M-6.9B）模型系列上，RISE相比RapidIn将索引存储减少了高达112倍，并能扩展到32B参数模型，而基于梯度的基线方法（如RapidIn和ZO-Inf）在此规模下内存不可行。在三个任务上的评估表明RISE在回溯归因和前瞻评估方面均表现良好。

Conclusion: RISE为大语言模型中的影响分析和训练数据选择提供了一个实用且可扩展的基础方法，通过聚焦影响热点和高效压缩技术，解决了现有方法在大规模模型上的可扩展性限制。

Abstract: Data attribution and valuation are critical for understanding data-model synergy for Large Language Models (LLMs), yet existing gradient-based methods suffer from scalability challenges on LLMs. Inspired by human cognition, where decision making relies on a focused readout of relevant memories rather than replaying all pathways, we introduce RISE (Readout Influence Sketching Estimator). Instead of computing and indexing gradients across the entire LLM, RISE focuses on influence hotspots at the output layer, where influence signals concentrate, and the gradient admits a decomposed outer-product form. This enables a dual-channel representation combining a lexical residual channel (RH) and a semantic projected-error channel (GH). Applying CountSketch projections to these channels achieves strong compression while maintaining accurate attribution. Across the OLMo (1B-32B) and Pythia (14M-6.9B) families, RISE reduces index storage by up to 112$\times$ compared to RapidIn and scales to 32B parameters LLM, where gradient-based baselines such as RapidIn and ZO-Inf become memory-infeasible. We evaluate RISE on two paradigms: (1) retrospective attribution, retrieving influential training examples for specific predictions, and (2) prospective valuation, scoring candidate data utility zero-shot. We validate RISE on three tasks: Howdy backdoor data detection, Finance-Medical domain separation, and Brain Rot high-quality data selection. In a closed-loop Brain Rot study, continued pretraining on RISE-selected data yields consistent downstream improvements. Overall, RISE provides a practical and scalable primitive for influence analysis and training-data selection in modern large language models.

</details>


### [45] [OT on the Map: Quantifying Domain Shifts in Geographic Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16220)
*Haoran Zhang,Livia Betti,Konstantin Klemmer,Esther Rolf,David Alvarez-Melis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GeoSpOT：利用最优传输方法计算地理空间域间距离，以预测跨域迁移难度并指导模型部署决策


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地理数据在计算机视觉和机器学习中存在域外泛化挑战，由于全球数据覆盖不均和地理区域间的分布偏移。目前缺乏原则性方法来判断跨区域适应何时会成功，需要量化新目标域与训练域之间的差异来支持模型训练和部署决策。

Method: 提出GeoSpOT方法，结合地理信息与最优传输方法计算地理空间域间距离。利用预训练位置编码器从经纬度对中提取嵌入，这些嵌入提供与图像/文本嵌入相当的信息。

Result: GeoSpOT距离能有效预测跨域迁移难度。位置编码器嵌入仅使用经纬度对作为输入，却能提供与图像/文本嵌入相当的信息。这使得用户即使在下游任务未知或没有任务特定数据时，也能近似估计地理空间模型的域外性能。

Conclusion: GeoSpOT距离可以前瞻性地指导数据选择，并创建预测工具来分析模型可能表现不佳的区域。这为地理空间模型的跨域部署提供了量化评估框架。

Abstract: In computer vision and machine learning for geographic data, out-of-domain generalization is a pervasive challenge, arising from uneven global data coverage and distribution shifts across geographic regions. Though models are frequently trained in one region and deployed in another, there is no principled method for determining when this cross-region adaptation will be successful. A well-defined notion of distance between distributions can effectively quantify how different a new target domain is compared to the domains used for model training, which in turn could support model training and deployment decisions. In this paper, we propose a strategy for computing distances between geospatial domains that leverages geographic information with Optimal Transport methods (GeoSpOT). In our experiments, GeoSpOT distances emerge as effective predictors of cross-domain transfer difficulty. We further demonstrate that embeddings from pretrained location encoders provide information comparable to image/text embeddings, despite relying solely on longitude-latitude pairs as input. This allows users to get an approximation of out-of-domain performance for geospatial models, even when the exact downstream task is unknown, or no task-specific data is available. Building on these findings, we show that GeoSpOT distances can preemptively guide data selection and enable predictive tools to analyze regions where a model is likely to underperform.

</details>


### [46] [Neuro-Symbolic ODE Discovery with Latent Grammar Flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16232)
*Karin Yu,Eleni Chatzi,Georgios Kissas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LGF是一种神经符号生成框架，通过将方程嵌入基于语法的离散潜在空间，并使用行为损失使语义相似方程更接近，然后通过离散流模型递归生成拟合观测数据的候选方程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解自然和工程系统通常依赖于符号公式（如微分方程），这些公式提供了可解释性和可迁移性，超越了黑盒模型。需要从数据中发现常微分方程的方法。

Method: LGF将方程嵌入基于语法的离散潜在空间，使用行为损失使语义相似方程更接近。采用离散流模型指导采样过程，递归生成最佳拟合观测数据的候选方程。领域知识和约束可以嵌入规则或用作条件预测器。

Result: 论文介绍了LGF框架，但摘要中未提供具体实验结果。该方法能够从数据中发现常微分方程，并支持领域知识和约束的整合。

Conclusion: LGF是一个有前景的神经符号生成框架，用于从数据中发现常微分方程，结合了符号表示的可解释性和神经方法的灵活性。

Abstract: Understanding natural and engineered systems often relies on symbolic formulations, such as differential equations, which provide interpretability and transferability beyond black-box models.
  We introduce Latent Grammar Flow (LGF), a neuro-symbolic generative framework for discovering ordinary differential equations from data. LGF embeds equations as grammar-based representations into a discrete latent space and forces semantically similar equations to be positioned closer together with a behavioural loss. Then, a discrete flow model guides the sampling process to recursively generate candidate equations that best fit the observed data. Domain knowledge and constraints, such as stability, can be either embedded into the rules or used as conditional predictors.

</details>


### [47] [Enhancing AI and Dynamical Subseasonal Forecasts with Probabilistic Bias Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16238)
*Hannah Guan,Soukayna Mouatadid,Paulo Orenstein,Judah Cohen,Haiyu Dong,Zekun Ni,Jeremy Berman,Genevieve Flaspohler,Alex Lu,Jakob Schloer,Joshua Talib,Jonathan A. Weyn,Lester Mackey*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出概率偏差校正（PBC）机器学习框架，显著提升次季节（2-6周）天气预报准确性，在ECMWF实时预报竞赛中全面领先


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前天气预报在两周内精度较高，但在次季节时间尺度（2-6周）技能急剧下降，主要由于误差累积和系统性偏差，需要有效方法来提升这一关键时间尺度的预报准确性

Method: 提出概率偏差校正（PBC）机器学习框架，通过学习校正历史概率预报来减少系统性误差，可应用于动力模型和AI模型

Result: PBC使ECMWF的AI预报系统次季节技能翻倍，改善91%气压、92%温度、98%降水目标的业务去偏差动力模型技能；在2025年ECMWF实时预报竞赛中，PBC在所有天气变量和预报时效上均排名第一，超越6个业务预报中心的动力模型、国际多模式集合、ECMWF的AI预报系统以及全球34个团队的预报系统

Conclusion: PBC框架显著提升次季节天气预报技能，能够更准确地预测极端事件，有望改善农业规划、能源管理和脆弱社区的灾害准备

Abstract: Decision-makers rely on weather forecasts to plant crops, manage wildfires, allocate water and energy, and prepare for weather extremes. Today, such forecasts enjoy unprecedented accuracy out to two weeks thanks to steady advances in physics-based dynamical models and data-driven artificial intelligence (AI) models. However, model skill drops precipitously at subseasonal timescales (2 - 6 weeks ahead), due to compounding errors and persistent biases. To counter this degradation, we introduce probabilistic bias correction (PBC), a machine learning framework that substantially reduces systematic error by learning to correct historical probabilistic forecasts. When applied to the leading dynamical and AI models from the European Centre for Medium-Range Weather Forecasts (ECMWF), PBC doubles the subseasonal skill of the AI Forecasting System and improves the skill of the operationally-debiased dynamical model for 91% of pressure, 92% of temperature, and 98% of precipitation targets. We designed PBC for operational deployment, and, in ECMWF's 2025 real-time forecasting competition, its global forecasts placed first for all weather variables and lead times, outperforming the dynamical models from six operational forecasting centers, an international dynamical multi-model ensemble, ECMWF's AI Forecasting System, and the forecasting systems of 34 teams worldwide. These probabilistic skill gains translate into more accurate prediction of extreme events and have the potential to improve agricultural planning, energy management, and disaster preparedness in vulnerable communities.

</details>


### [48] [Detecting and Suppressing Reward Hacking with Gradient Fingerprints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16242)
*Songtao Wang,Quang Hieu Pham,Fangcong Yin,Xinpeng Wang,Jocelyn Qiaochu Chen,Greg Durrett,Xi Ye*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GRIFT方法通过分析模型内部计算的梯度指纹来检测强化学习中的奖励黑客行为，在数学、代码和逻辑推理基准测试中显著优于现有方法，并能通过拒绝微调减少奖励黑客行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统强化学习仅优化结果奖励而不约束中间推理过程，容易导致奖励黑客行为——模型利用奖励函数的漏洞获得高分但不解决实际任务。这些行为通常隐含在看似合理的思维链中，基于文本的监控方法难以有效检测。

Method: 提出GRIFT方法：给定提示和模型生成的思维链，计算思维链相对于提示的梯度，将梯度压缩为紧凑表示，然后用该表示评估思维链是否反映奖励黑客行为。

Result: 在数学、代码和逻辑推理的验证基准测试中，GRIFT显著优于CoT Monitor和TRACE等基线方法，在检测奖励黑客行为方面获得超过25%的相对改进。将GRIFT集成到拒绝微调流程中能减少奖励黑客并提升真实任务目标性能。

Conclusion: 利用梯度级表示评估思维链推理质量是一个有前景的方向。GRIFT为检测和缓解强化学习中的奖励黑客问题提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) typically optimizes for outcome rewards without imposing constraints on intermediate reasoning. This leaves training susceptible to reward hacking, where models exploit loopholes (e.g., spurious patterns in training data) in the reward function to achieve high scores without solving the intended task. These reward-hacking behaviors are often implicit, as the intermediate chain-of-thought (CoT) may appear plausible on the surface, limiting the effectiveness of purely text-based monitoring. We propose Gradient Fingerprint (GRIFT), a method for detecting reward hacking using models' internal computations. Given a prompt and a model-generated CoT, GRIFT computes gradients of the CoT conditioned on the prompt and compresses them into a compact representation, which is then used to assess whether the CoT reflects reward hacking behavior. Across verifiable reasoning benchmarks spanning math, code, and logical reasoning, GRIFT substantially outperforms strong baselines, including CoT Monitor and TRACE, achieving over 25% relative improvement in detecting reward hacking behavior. Moreover, integrating GRIFT into the rejection fine-tuning pipeline for reasoning tasks reduces reward hacking and improves performance on the true task objective. Our results highlight a promising direction of leveraging gradient level representations for assessing the quality of CoT reasoning traces. Our code is available at: https://github.com/songtao-x/reward_hack.

</details>


### [49] [Joint-Centric Dual Contrastive Alignment with Structure-Preserving and Information-Balanced Regularization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16247)
*Habibeh Naderi,Behrouz Haji Soleimani,Stan Matwin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: HILBERT是一个用于低资源环境下学习长序列文档级音频-文本表示的多模态框架，通过交叉注意力机制和互惠对比训练解决音频-文本维度不平衡问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在低资源数据设置中，从长序列分割的音频-文本对中学习文档级表示面临挑战，特别是音频和文本维度严重不平衡的问题，需要一种能够对齐模态同时保持模态特定结构的框架。

Method: 使用冻结的预训练语音和语言编码器提取片段级特征，通过交叉模态注意力和自注意力池化聚合形成模态特定文档表示和联合交叉注意力嵌入。引入互惠双重对比目标对齐音频-联合和文本-联合表示，而非直接对比音频和文本。使用中心核对齐损失保持模态与联合嵌入的结构一致性，以及互信息平衡损失防止单一模态主导。下游预测采用专家混合分类器处理异构标签机制。

Result: 在多个音频-文本骨干组合上的广泛评估表明，HILBERT能够学习语义上有意义的长序列表示，并在高度不平衡的多类设置中实现优越性能。

Conclusion: HILBERT通过层次化长序列平衡嵌入和互惠对比训练，有效解决了低资源环境下音频-文本维度不平衡问题，为长序列多模态表示学习提供了有效的框架。

Abstract: We propose HILBERT (HIerarchical Long-sequence Balanced Embedding with Reciprocal contrastive Training), a cross-attentive multimodal framework for learning document-level audio-text representations from long, segmented sequences in low-resource data settings. HILBERT leverages frozen pre-trained speech and language encoders to extract segment-level features, which are aggregated via cross-modal attention and self-attentive pooling to form modality-specific document representations and a joint cross-attentive embedding. To align modalities while preserving modality-specific structure under severe audio-text dimensional imbalance, we introduce a reciprocal dual contrastive objective that simultaneously aligns audio-to-joint and text-to-joint representations, rather than directly contrasting audio and text alone. Two auxiliary regularizers further stabilize long-sequence fusion: a Centered Kernel Alignment (CKA) loss that preserves structural consistency between each modality and the joint embedding, and a mutual information balancing loss that prevents dominance of a single modality by equalizing information flow from audio and text into the joint space. For downstream prediction, HILBERT employs a Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) classifier over concatenated audio, text, and joint representations to accommodate heterogeneous label regimes. Extensive evaluation across multiple audio-text backbone combinations demonstrates that HILBERT learns semantically meaningful long-sequence representations and achieves superior performance on highly imbalanced multi-class settings.

</details>


### [50] [Beyond Distribution Sharpening: The Importance of Task Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16259)
*Sarthak Mittal,Leo Gagnon,Guillaume Lajoie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究对比了分布锐化与基于任务奖励的强化学习在提升模型性能上的差异，发现分布锐化效果有限，而任务奖励信号能带来显著且稳定的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿模型通过整合基于任务奖励的强化学习展现出卓越能力，但学界对RL究竟是赋予模型新技能还是仅仅锐化现有分布以激发潜在能力存在争议。本研究旨在明确区分这两种机制并验证其实际效果。

Method: 使用RL作为工具同时实现分布锐化和任务奖励学习两种范式，从第一性原理分析分布锐化的局限性，并在Llama-3.2-3B-Instruct、Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct和Qwen3-4B-Instruct-2507模型上进行数学数据集实验验证。

Result: 理论分析显示分布锐化存在固有局限性，其最优解可能不利且方法本质上不稳定。实验结果表明分布锐化带来的增益有限，而基于任务奖励的信号能显著提升模型性能并确保学习过程的稳定性。

Conclusion: 基于任务奖励的强化学习确实能为模型带来实质性的新能力提升，而不仅仅是锐化现有分布。任务奖励信号对于实现稳健的性能改进和稳定学习至关重要。

Abstract: Frontier models have demonstrated exceptional capabilities following the integration of task-reward-based reinforcement learning (RL) into their training pipelines, enabling systems to evolve from pure reasoning models into sophisticated agents. However, debate persists regarding whether RL genuinely instills new skills within a base model or merely sharpens its existing distribution to elicit latent capabilities. To address this dichotomy, we present an explicit comparison between distribution sharpening and task-reward-based learning, utilizing RL as a tool to implement both paradigms. Our analysis reveals the inherent limitations of distribution sharpening, demonstrating from first principles how and why the optima can be unfavorable and the approach fundamentally unstable. Furthermore, our experiments using Llama-3.2-3B-Instruct, Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct and Qwen3-4B-Instruct-2507 on math datasets confirm that sharpening yields limited gains, whereas incorporating task-based reward signal can greatly help achieve robust performance improvements and stable learning.

</details>


### [51] [Evaluating the Progression of Large Language Model Capabilities for Small-Molecule Drug Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16279)
*Shriram Chennakesavalu,Kirill Shmilovich,Hayley Weir,Colin Grambow,John Bradshaw,Patricia Suriana,Chen Cheng,Kangway Chuang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文介绍了用于评估LLMs在药物设计中的化学基准测试套件，将其构建为RL环境，并展示了RL后训练能显著提升模型性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLMs在药物设计中有潜力，但缺乏反映真实场景的基准测试，其实用性仍不明确

Method: 引入化学基础任务套件（分子性质预测、分子表示转换、分子设计），将其构建为强化学习环境，并在三个模型家族上进行评估和RL后训练

Result: 前沿模型在化学任务上表现逐渐提升，但在低数据实验环境中仍有改进空间；RL后训练能显著提升性能，较小的后训练模型能与最先进的前沿模型竞争

Conclusion: 通过精心设计的评估任务和针对性后训练，可以阐明并缩小LLMs在药物发现中的关键能力差距，为实际应用提供可行路径

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have the potential to accelerate small molecule drug design due to their ability to reason about information from diverse sources and formats. However, their practical utility remains unclear due to the lack of benchmarks that reflect real-world scenarios. In this work, we introduce a suite of chemically-grounded tasks spanning molecular property prediction, molecular representation transformations, and molecular design. Importantly, we formulate these tasks as reinforcement learning (RL) environments, enabling a unified approach for evaluation and post-training. Across three model families, we find that frontier models are increasingly proficient at chemical tasks, but that there is significant room for improvement, especially in experimental settings with low data. Critically, we show that RL-based post-training can substantially improve performance. A smaller model post-trained on our environments becomes competitive with state-of-the-art frontier models, despite a significantly weaker base model. This suggests a practical route toward employing LLMs in drug discovery; by combining carefully-designed evaluation tasks with targeted post-training, we can both elucidate and close critical capability gaps.

</details>


### [52] [Geometric regularization of autoencoders via observed stochastic dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16282)
*Sean Hill,Felix X. -F. Ye*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种学习随机动力系统低维流形和潜在SDE的三阶段方法，通过协方差引导的正则化解决传统方法的指数标度问题和几何约束不足问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机动力系统在长时间尺度上在高维空间中演化于未知低维流形。现有方法存在显著问题：ATLAS等局部图方法存在指数级地标缩放和每步重投影问题，而自编码器方法则对切丛几何约束不足，误差会传播到学习的漂移和扩散项中。

Method: 提出三阶段管道：1) 图学习，2) 潜在漂移学习，3) 潜在扩散学习。关键创新是利用环境协方差Λ编码坐标不变的切空间信息，构造切丛惩罚和逆一致性惩罚。这些惩罚诱导出ρ-度量，严格弱于Sobolev H¹范数但达到相同的图质量泛化率。

Result: 在嵌入高达201维的四个曲面上，径向平均首次通过时间误差减少50-70%（旋转动力学），在大多数表面-跃迁对上获得最低的井间MFPT误差（Müller-Brown Langevin动力学），端到端环境系数误差相对于无正则化自编码器减少高达一个数量级。

Conclusion: 该方法通过协方差引导的正则化有效约束切丛几何，显著提高了流形学习和潜在SDE建模的精度，为从短时爆发环境集合构建降阶模拟器提供了理论保证和实际有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Stochastic dynamical systems with slow or metastable behavior evolve, on long time scales, on an unknown low-dimensional manifold in high-dimensional ambient space. Building a reduced simulator from short-burst ambient ensembles is a long-standing problem: local-chart methods like ATLAS suffer from exponential landmark scaling and per-step reprojection, while autoencoder alternatives leave tangent-bundle geometry poorly constrained, and the errors propagate into the learned drift and diffusion. We observe that the ambient covariance~$Λ$ already encodes coordinate-invariant tangent-space information, its range spanning the tangent bundle. Using this, we construct a tangent-bundle penalty and an inverse-consistency penalty for a three-stage pipeline (chart learning, latent drift, latent diffusion) that learns a single nonlinear chart and the latent SDE. The penalties induce a function-space metric, the $ρ$-metric, strictly weaker than the Sobolev $H^1$ norm yet achieving the same chart-quality generalization rate up to logarithmic factors. For the drift, we derive an encoder-pullback target via Itô's formula on the learned encoder and prove a bias decomposition showing the standard decoder-side formula carries systematic error for any imperfect chart. Under a $W^{2,\infty}$ chart-convergence assumption, chart-level error propagates controllably to weak convergence of the ambient dynamics and to convergence of radial mean first-passage times. Experiments on four surfaces embedded in up to $201$ ambient dimensions reduce radial MFPT error by $50$--$70\%$ under rotation dynamics and achieve the lowest inter-well MFPT error on most surface--transition pairs under metastable Müller--Brown Langevin dynamics, while reducing end-to-end ambient coefficient errors by up to an order of magnitude relative to an unregularized autoencoder.

</details>


<div id='cs.SE'></div>

# cs.SE [[Back]](#toc)

### [53] [Prompt-Driven Code Summarization: A Systematic Literature Review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15385)
*Afia Farjana,Zaiyu Cheng,Antonio Mastropaolo*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文对LLM在软件文档生成中的提示工程方法进行系统性文献综述，分析不同提示策略的有效性、适用条件及评估方法，为未来研究和实践提供指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件文档对程序理解、开发人员入职、代码审查和长期维护至关重要，但手动编写高质量文档耗时且结果往往不完整或不一致。LLM为自动生成代码的自然语言描述提供了有前景的解决方案，但其效果高度依赖于提示设计，而当前研究分散，缺乏对最佳提示策略、适用模型和条件的系统理解。

Method: 采用系统性文献综述方法，整合现有证据，对提示范式进行分类，分析其有效性，并识别研究空白。综述涵盖少样本提示、思维链推理、检索增强生成和零样本学习等多种提示工程方法。

Result: 当前研究呈现碎片化状态，对何种提示策略最适合特定模型和条件缺乏清晰理解。评估实践差异很大，大多数研究依赖基于重叠度的指标，这些指标可能无法捕捉语义质量。研究识别了现有文献中的关键空白和局限性。

Conclusion: 需要更系统化的研究来理解不同提示策略在软件文档任务中的相对有效性，开发更全面的评估方法，并建立最佳实践指南，以促进LLM在软件工程中的实际应用。

Abstract: Software documentation is essential for program comprehension, developer onboarding, code review, and long-term maintenance. Yet producing quality documentation manually is time-consuming and frequently yields incomplete or inconsistent results. Large language models (LLMs) offer a promising solution by automatically generating natural language descriptions from source code, helping developers understand code more efficiently, facilitating maintenance, and supporting downstream activities such as defect localization and commit message generation. However, the effectiveness of LLMs in documentation tasks critically depends on how they are prompted. Properly structured instructions can substantially improve model performance, making prompt engineering-the design of input prompts to guide model behavior-a foundational technique in LLM-based software engineering. Approaches such as few-shot prompting, chain-of-thought reasoning, retrieval-augmented generation, and zero-shot learning show promise for code summarization, yet current research remains fragmented. There is limited understanding of which prompting strategies work best, for which models, and under what conditions. Moreover, evaluation practices vary widely, with most studies relying on overlap-based metrics that may not capture semantic quality. This systematic literature review consolidates existing evidence, categorizes prompting paradigms, examines their effectiveness, and identifies gaps to guide future research and practical adoption.

</details>


### [54] [Analyzing Chain of Thought (CoT) Approaches in Control Flow Code Deobfuscation Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15390)
*Seyedreza Mohseni,Sarvesh Baskar,Edward Raff,Manas Gaur*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文探索使用思维链提示指导大语言模型进行代码反混淆，显著提升控制流图重建和语义保持性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统代码反混淆需要大量手动工作和复杂分析工具，耗时耗力。研究探索基于思维链提示的替代方法，利用大语言模型进行代码分析，旨在减少逆向工程的人工工作量

Method: 采用思维链提示方法，指导大语言模型进行逐步推理的代码分析。专注于控制流混淆（包括控制流平坦化、不透明谓词及其组合），使用五个最先进的大语言模型进行评估，在标准C基准测试集上验证

Result: 思维链提示相比简单提示显著提升反混淆质量。GPT5在应用CoT后表现最佳，控制流图重建平均提升约16%，语义保持平均提升约20.5%。模型性能受混淆级别、混淆器选择和原始控制流图内在复杂度影响

Conclusion: 思维链指导的大语言模型可作为有效的代码反混淆辅助工具，提供改进的代码可解释性、更准确的控制流图重建和更好的程序行为保持，同时可能减少逆向工程所需的人工努力

Abstract: Code deobfuscation is the task of recovering a readable version of a program while preserving its original behavior. In practice, this often requires days or even months of manual work with complex and expensive analysis tools. In this paper, we explore an alternative approach based on Chain-of-Thought (CoT) prompting, where a large language model is guided through explicit, step-by-step reasoning tailored for code analysis. We focus on control flow obfuscation, including Control Flow Flattening (CFF), Opaque Predicates, and their combination, and we measure both structural recovery of the control flow graph and preservation of program semantics. We evaluate five state-of-the-art large language models and show that CoT prompting significantly improves deobfuscation quality compared with simple prompting. We validate our approach on a diverse set of standard C benchmarks and report results using both structural metrics for control flow graphs and semantic metrics based on output similarity. Among the tested models and by applying CoT, GPT5 achieves the strongest overall performance, with an average gain of about 16% in control-flow graph reconstruction and about 20.5% in semantic preservation across our benchmarks compared to zero-shot prompting. Our results also show that model performance depends not only on the obfuscation level and the chosen obfuscator but also on the intrinsic complexity of the original control flow graph. Collectively, these findings suggest that CoT-guided large language models can serve as effective assistants for code deobfuscation, providing improved code explainability, more faithful control flow graph reconstruction, and better preservation of program behavior while potentially reducing the manual effort needed for reverse engineering.

</details>


### [55] [The Semi-Executable Stack: Agentic Software Engineering and the Expanding Scope of SE](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15468)
*Robert Feldt,Per Lenberg,Julian Frattini,Dhasarathy Parthasarathy*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出"半可执行栈"模型，认为AI时代软件工程的核心转变不是失去相关性，而是工程对象从可执行代码扩展到包含自然语言、工具、工作流等的半可执行工件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对AI（特别是LLM和智能体）对软件工程职业的威胁论，作者提出不同观点：真正的转变是工程对象范围的扩展，而非软件工程失去价值。需要新的概念框架来理解这一转变。

Method: 提出"半可执行栈"六环诊断参考模型，包含可执行工件、指令工件、编排执行、控制机制、操作逻辑、社会制度适配等层次。通过三个案例研究展开论证，将常见反对意见重构为工程目标而非拒绝转变的理由。

Result: 建立了理解AI时代软件工程转型的概念框架，提供了定位贡献、瓶颈和组织转型的分析工具。提出了"保留vs净化"启发式方法，用于决定哪些传统软件工程流程、控制和协调机制应保留或重新设计。

Conclusion: 软件工程在AI时代并未失去相关性，而是工程范围扩展到半可执行工件。半可执行栈模型为理解这一转型提供了诊断工具和议程设置框架，帮助从业者将挑战重构为工程目标。

Abstract: AI-based systems, currently driven largely by LLMs and tool-using agentic harnesses, are increasingly discussed as a possible threat to software engineering. Foundation models get stronger, agents can plan and act across multiple steps, and tasks such as scaffolding, routine test generation, straightforward bug fixing, and small integration work look more exposed than they did only a few years ago. The result is visible unease not only among students and junior developers, but also among experienced practitioners who worry that hard-won expertise may lose value. This paper argues for a different reading. The important shift is not that software engineering loses relevance. It is that the thing being engineered expands beyond executable code to semi-executable artifacts; combinations of natural language, tools, workflows, control mechanisms, and organizational routines whose enactment depends on human or probabilistic interpretation rather than deterministic execution. The Semi-Executable Stack is introduced as a six-ring diagnostic reference model for reasoning about that expansion, spanning executable artifacts, instructional artifacts, orchestrated execution, controls, operating logic, and societal and institutional fit. The model helps locate where a contribution, bottleneck, or organizational transition primarily sits, and which adjacent rings it depends on. The paper develops the argument through three worked cases, reframes familiar objections as engineering targets rather than reasons to dismiss the transition, and closes with a preserve-versus-purify heuristic for deciding which legacy software engineering processes, controls, and coordination routines should be kept and which should be simplified or redesigned. This paper is a conceptual keynote companion: diagnostic and agenda-setting rather than empirical.

</details>


### [56] [LLM4C2Rust: Large Language Models for Automated Memory-Safe Code Transpilation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15485)
*Sarah Bedell,Nazanin Siavash,Armin Moin*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出一个基于检索增强生成（RAG）的框架，结合LLM和SLM将C/C++代码转换为内存安全的Rust代码，通过分段处理和上下文检索提升代码正确性和安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: C/C++等内存不安全语言编写的遗留系统存在内存安全问题，手动转译耗时且易错，基于规则的方法不够灵活高效，而现代AI模型（如LLM）为自动化转译提供了新可能。

Method: 提出RAG辅助框架，集成LLM与SLM，采用分段策略将C/C++代码处理为平衡块，利用Rust文档和编译器错误参考作为检索上下文指导LLM进行转译。

Result: 使用三种OpenAI模型（GPT-4o、GPT-4-Turbo、o3-Mini）的实验表明，RAG增强管道普遍提升了C到Rust代码转译的正确性和安全性，多个Coreutils程序在最终Rust输出中完全消除了原始指针解引用（RPD）和不安全类型转换（UTC）。

Conclusion: LLM基础的转译方法在自动化软件现代化、修复以及内存安全代码生成方面具有潜力，RAG框架能有效提升转译质量。

Abstract: Memory safety has long been a critical challenge in software engineering, particularly for legacy systems written in memory-unsafe languages such as C and C++. Rust, one of the youngest modern programming languages, offers built-in memory-safety guarantees that make it a strong candidate for secure systems development. Consequently, transpiling C/C++ code into memory-safe Rust code has become a growing area of research. However, manual transpilation is often time-consuming and error-prone. Additionally, rule-based automated approaches are not as flexible or cost-effective as methods enabled by state-of-the-art AI models, techniques, and methods, such as those that deploy Large Language Models (LLMs), for example, Generative Pretrained Transformers (GPT). In this paper, we propose a Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG)-assisted framework that integrates an LLM with a Small Language Model (SLM) to perform C/C++-to-Rust transpilation with a focus on enhancing memory safety. The framework deploys a segmentation strategy that processes C/C++ code in balanced blocks, guiding the LLM with retrieved context from Rust documentation and compiler error references. Our experiments using three OpenAI models (GPT-4o, GPT-4-Turbo, and o3-Mini) demonstrate that the RAG-enhanced pipeline generally improves both code correctness and security for C-to-Rust code transpilation. Several Coreutils programs achieve complete elimination of Raw Pointer Dereferences (RPDs) and Unsafe Type Casts (UTCs) in the final Rust output, indicating the potential of LLM-based transpilation for advancing automated software modernization and repair, as well as memory-safe code generation.

</details>


### [57] [QMutBench: A Dataset of Quantum Circuit Mutants](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15870)
*Eñaut Mendiluze Usandizaga,Thomas Laurent,Paolo Arcaini,Shaukat Ali*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: QMutBench是一个包含70多万个量子电路突变体的数据集，用于评估量子软件测试技术的有效性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子软件测试需要评估测试用例的有效性，这需要包含故障的基准程序。但目前缺乏包含故障的基准数据集，阻碍了量子软件测试技术的发展。

Method: 创建QMutBench数据集，包含超过700,000个量子电路突变体，代表不同类型的故障。提供在线接口，用户可根据原始量子电路、期望的突变体存活率、故障量子门类型等标准筛选突变体。

Result: 开发了一个可访问的在线数据集，为量子软件开发者和测试者提供必要的突变体基准，用于评估测试用例质量或比较不同测试技术。

Conclusion: QMutBench填补了量子软件测试领域缺乏故障基准的空白，支持测试技术评估和新突变引导测试技术的开发。

Abstract: Quantum software testing has attracted interest in recent years, prompting the development of various techniques to automate the testing of quantum software. These techniques generate test cases that must be assessed for their effectiveness in detecting faults. Such an assessment requires benchmarks of faulty programs. However, there is a lack of benchmarks containing faults. In this data showcase, we propose QMutBench, a dataset that contains over 700,000 quantum circuit mutants representing different faults. The dataset is accessible via an online interface with selection criteria, such as the original quantum circuit(s) from which mutants are generated, the desired survival rate of the selected mutants, and other mutation characteristics (e.g., the type of faulty quantum gate). QMutBench provides quantum software developers and testers with an accessible online dataset to obtain benchmarks of mutants necessary to assess either the quality of the test cases generated by their testing technique or to compare different testing techniques. It also enables the development of new mutation-guided quantum software testing techniques.

</details>


### [58] [Empirical Investigation of Quantum Computing Toolchains and Algorithms : Mining Stack Overflow Repository](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15512)
*Maryam Tavassoli Sabzevari,Arif Ali Khan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 分析1,404个Stack Overflow量子计算相关帖子，识别主要讨论主题、流行工具和算法，并评估开发者问题的难度


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着量子计算向实际应用和工业采用过渡，需要了解开发者如何与量子技术互动，为研究人员、工具开发者和教育者提供改进量子软件工程可用性、文档和学习资源的见解

Method: 使用主题建模和定量分析方法，分析1,404个Stack Overflow量子计算帖子，识别主要讨论主题、流行工具和算法，并通过两个指标评估问题难度：(i) 无接受答案的问题百分比，(ii) 获得接受答案的中位时间

Result: 识别出七个主要主题，其中混合量子-经典计算和量子电路实现最为普遍；Qiskit和Q-sharp主导开发者讨论；Grover和Shor算法最常被引用；不同主题、工具和算法在参与度和难度上存在差异，表明成熟度和社区支持水平不同

Conclusion: 研究结果为研究人员、工具开发者和教育者提供了可操作的见解，支持改进量子软件工程的可用性、文档和学习资源；开源数据集已公开以支持透明度和可重复性

Abstract: Quantum computing (QC) is increasingly transitioning toward practical and industrial adoption, highlighting the need to understand how developers engage with quantum technologies. In this study, we analyze 1,404 Stack Overflow posts related to quantum computing topics, including quantum programming, tools, and algorithms, to investigate real-world developer discussions. Using topic modeling and quantitative analysis, we identify the main discussion topics, their popularity, and the tools, programming languages, and quantum algorithms referenced by practitioners. We further assess the difficulty of developer questions using two metrics: (i) the percentage of questions without accepted answers and (ii) the median time required to receive an accepted answer. Our findings reveal seven main topics, with hybrid quantum--classical computing and quantum circuit implementation emerging as the most prevalent. We observe that Qiskit and Q-sharp dominate developer discussions, while Grover's and Shor's algorithms are the most frequently referenced. Moreover, our analysis highlights differences in engagement and difficulty across topics, tools, and algorithms, indicating varying levels of maturity and community support. These findings provide actionable insights for researchers, tool developers, and educators, supporting improvements in usability, documentation, and learning resources in quantum software engineering. To support transparency and reproducibility, the open-source dataset used in this study is publicly available at Zenodo.

</details>


### [59] [CodeMMR: Bridging Natural Language, Code, and Image for Unified Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15663)
*Jiahui Geng,Qing Li,Fengyu Cai,Fakhri Karray*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: MMCoIR是首个多模态代码信息检索基准，涵盖5个视觉领域、8种编程语言和11个库；CodeMMR是统一检索模型，在共享语义空间中嵌入自然语言、代码和图像，在nDCG@10上平均提升10个点，并增强RAG的代码生成保真度和视觉基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代码信息检索模型主要基于文本，忽视了编程工件（如Web界面、数据可视化、SVG、示意图和UML）固有的视觉和结构特征，限制了代码发现、重用和LLM编码的可靠性。

Method: 提出CodeMMR统一检索模型，通过基于指令的多模态对齐，将自然语言、代码和图像共同嵌入到共享语义空间中，实现跨模态和跨语言的强泛化能力。

Result: CodeMMR在MMCoIR基准上显著优于竞争基线（如UniIR、GME、VLM2Vec），在nDCG@10指标上平均提升10个点；集成到RAG中能增强代码生成保真度和视觉基础能力。

Conclusion: 多模态检索是下一代智能编程系统的核心使能技术，CodeMMR通过统一的多模态嵌入展示了跨模态和跨语言的强泛化能力，为代码发现、重用和可靠LLM编码提供了新途径。

Abstract: Code search, framed as information retrieval (IR), underpins modern software engineering and increasingly powers retrieval-augmented generation (RAG), improving code discovery, reuse, and the reliability of LLM-based coding. Yet existing code IR models remain largely text-centric and often overlook the visual and structural aspects inherent in programming artifacts such as web interfaces, data visualizations, SVGs, schematic diagrams, and UML. To bridge this gap, we introduce MMCoIR, the first comprehensive benchmark for evaluating multimodal code IR across five visual domains, eight programming languages, eleven libraries, and show the challenge of the task through extensive evaluation. Therefore, we then propose CodeMMR, a unified retrieval model that jointly embeds natural language, code, and images into a shared semantic space through instruction-based multimodal alignment. CodeMMR achieves strong generalization across modalities and languages, outperforming competitive baselines (e.g., UniIR, GME, VLM2Vec) by an average of 10 points on nDCG@10. Moreover, integrating CodeMMR into RAG enhances code generation fidelity and visual grounding on unseen code generation tasks, underscoring the potential of multimodal retrieval as a core enabler for next-generation intelligent programming systems. Datasets are available at HuggingFace.

</details>


### [60] [Feature Toggle Dynamics in Large-Scale Systems: Prevalence, Growth, Lifespan, and Benchmarking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15872)
*Xhevahire Tërnava*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 对Kubernetes和GitLab中4000多个特性开关事件的纵向分析显示，开关移除滞后于添加，导致库存增长，且不同系统间开关寿命差异显著，部分开关成为事实上的永久开关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 特性开关虽然支持渐进式发布和实验，但往往超出预期生命周期而持续存在，积累为技术债务。先前研究关注开关交互和复杂性，但缺乏跨组织背景下开关随时间演化的量化纵向研究。

Method: 分析Kubernetes（1000万行代码，8.5年）和GitLab（500万行代码，5年）中超过4000个开关事件，比较开关添加与移除的时间模式，测量开关寿命分布，识别长期存在的开关。

Result: Kubernetes开关移除滞后添加约35%，GitLab滞后13%，导致开关库存增长。Kubernetes开关中位寿命734天，GitLab仅185天。部分开关（Kubernetes 1.33%，GitLab 0.73%）超过所有先前观察到的移除持续时间，成为事实上的永久开关。

Conclusion: 基于研究发现，提出了包含五个关键指标及其经验阈值区的基准框架，使实践者能够评估和比较不同项目的开关管理实践。所有脚本和数据已公开。

Abstract: Feature toggles enable gradual rollouts and experimentation in software systems, yet often persist beyond their intended lifecycle, accumulating as technical debt. Prior research has examined feature toggle interactions and complexity, but no longitudinal study has quantified how toggles evolve over time across different organizational contexts. We analyse over 4,000 toggle events in Kubernetes (10 MLoC, 8.5 years) and GitLab (5 MLoC, 5 years). We find that feature toggle removals lags behind additions in both systems (by roughly 35% and 13%, respectively), leading to growing toggle inventories. Their lifespan patterns also differ notably, with Kubernetes toggles lasting a median of 734 days versus 185 in GitLab. Then, some feature toggles (1.33% and 0.73%, respectively) exceed all previously observed removal durations, becoming de facto permanent. Building on these findings, we propose a benchmarking framework with five key metrics and their empirically derived threshold zones, enabling practitioners to assess and compare toggle management practices across projects. All scripts and data are publicly available.

</details>


### [61] [Small Yet Configurable: Unveiling Null Variability in Software](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15957)
*Xhevahire Tërnava,Georges Aaron Randrianaina,Luc Lesoil,Mathieu Acher*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 对GNU coreutils中108个小规模软件系统的可配置性进行首次实证研究，发现即使小型程序也表现出显著的编译时和运行时可变性，最多可达76个选项，且可变性随时间增加，减少不必要可变性可降低代码复杂性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然小规模软件系统在日常任务中广泛使用，但其可配置性尚未得到充分研究。同时，现代软件系统显示出可配置性增加的趋势，以及对构建不可变、专用和可重现软件的兴趣日益增长。本研究旨在填补小规模软件系统可配置性研究的空白。

Method: 对GNU coreutils中的108个程序进行实证分析，研究其编译时和运行时可变性。分析包括：1) 统计每个程序的选项数量；2) 探究运行时可变性与代码库大小的相关性；3) 对85个版本中的20个最小程序进行时间序列分析，观察可变性变化趋势。

Result: 1) 小型程序可表现出显著的可配置性，最多达76个选项/程序；2) 运行时可变性与代码库大小高度相关（相关系数0.78）；3) 随时间推移，可变性趋于增加，主要源于编译时可变性的增加；4) 首次提出"零可变性软件系统"概念（无强制性功能之外的可配置性）。

Conclusion: 高可配置性并非大型系统专属特征，小型软件同样存在。通过将选项在运行时和编译时之间转移、移除不必要的运行时可变性或早期解决编译时可变性，可降低代码库复杂性和大小。这有助于设计更轻量、更小、更易维护的软件，平衡可配置性与代码库大小。

Abstract: Many small-scale software systems, that is, with limited codebase or binary size, are widely used in everyday tasks, yet their configurability remains largely unexplored. At the same time, studies on modern software systems show a trend toward increasing configurability, alongside growing interest in building immutable, specialized, and reproducible software. In this paper, we present the first empirical study on the extent of configurability in small-scale software systems. By analyzing 108 programs from GNU coreutils, we show that even small programs can exhibit significant compile-time and run-time variability, with up to 76 options per program. Then, there is a high correlation (0.78) between run-time variability and codebase size. Furthermore, an analysis of the 20 smallest programs across 85 releases reveals that variability tends to increase over time, primarily due to the added compile-time variability. This suggests that shifting options between run-time and compile-time, removing unnecessary run-time variability, or resolving compile-time variability early, can help reduce codebase complexity and size. We also introduce, for the first time, the concept of null-variable software system, one with no configurability beyond mandatory features. Our findings show that high configurability is not exclusive to large-scale systems and that reducing unnecessary variability can lead to lightweight, smaller, and more maintainable software. We hope this effort contributes to designing new software by understanding how to balance its configurability with codebase size.

</details>


### [62] [Supporting the Comprehension of Data Analysis Scripts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15963)
*Florian Sihler,Oliver Gerstl,Lars Pfrenger,Julian Schubert,Matthias Tichy*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: flowR是一个用于R语言数据科学IDE的扩展工具，通过数据流和控制流分析提供脚本可视化、代码检查、值注释等功能，支持数据科学脚本的可理解性和可维护性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据科学脚本通常难以理解和维护，阻碍了研究的可重复性和重用性，同时缺乏处理这类脚本的工具支持。R语言作为数据科学常用语言，需要更好的工具来支持数据分析师。

Method: flowR作为Positron和VS Code的扩展，通过交织过程间数据流和控制流分析构建全面的数据流图，结合R语言的动态和探索性特性。提供静态后向程序切片器、交互式图形可视化、代码检查、内联值注释等功能，并支持插件系统集成更多分析功能。

Result: flowR在真实项目中平均仅需576ms即可计算完整数据流图，实现近实时反馈。工具已开源并提供Docker镜像，支持数据科学脚本的可视化分析和维护。

Conclusion: flowR通过提供全面的数据流分析和可视化工具，显著改善了R语言数据科学脚本的可理解性和可维护性，支持研究的可重复性和代码重用，为数据分析师提供了有效的工具支持。

Abstract: A lot of research relies on data analysis scripts to process, clean, and visualize data. However, recent studies show that these scripts are often hard to comprehend and maintain, hindering reproducibility and reuse, accompanied by a lack of tool support for handling such scripts. In this work, we focus on the R programming language, addressing this problem by presenting flowR as an extension for the common data analysis IDEs Positron and VS Code. Alongside a previously presented static backward program slicer, flowR provides an overview of data analysis scripts, interactive graph visualizations, linting, and inline value annotations to support data analysts. FlowR incrementally analyzes R projects by intertwining interprocedural data- and control-flow analyses to build a comprehensive dataflow graph, incorporating R's dynamic and explorative features. Additionally, flowR offers a plugin system and interfaces, allowing the integration of further analyses, such as new linting rules or custom visualizations. Requiring an average of 576ms to calculate the full dataflow graph of real-world projects, this enables near real-time feedback. The demonstration video is available at  https://youtu.be/hJzr-r-NmMg . For the full source code and extensive documentation, refer to https://github.com/flowr-analysis/flowr . To try the docker image, use `docker run --rm -it eagleoutice/flowr`.

</details>


### [63] [Neurosymbolic Repo-level Code Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16021)
*Xiufeng Xu,Xiufeng Wu,Zejun Zhang,Yi Li*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该论文揭示了现有代码定位方法存在关键词捷径偏差，提出了关键词无关的逻辑代码定位挑战，并开发了LogicLoc框架，结合LLM和Datalog逻辑推理实现精确的代码定位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代码定位方法在真实问题基准上表现优异，但存在严重偏差：这些基准中充斥着关键词引用（如文件路径、函数名），导致模型依赖表面词汇匹配而非真正的结构推理。作者将这种现象称为"关键词捷径"，需要解决这一根本问题。

Method: 提出了LogicLoc框架，结合大型语言模型和Datalog逻辑推理。框架从代码库中提取程序事实，利用LLM合成Datalog程序，通过解析器门控验证和基于突变的中间规则诊断反馈确保正确性和效率。验证后的程序由高性能推理引擎执行，实现全自动闭环工作流。

Result: 在KA-LogicQuery诊断基准上，现有最先进方法性能急剧下降，而LogicLoc显著优于这些方法。在流行的问题驱动基准上，LogicLoc保持竞争力。LogicLoc以显著更低的token消耗和更快的执行速度实现优越性能，通过将结构遍历卸载到确定性引擎，减少了迭代LLM推理的开销。

Conclusion: 关键词捷径是现有代码定位方法的重要偏差，需要结构推理能力。LogicLoc框架通过结合LLM和Datalog逻辑推理，实现了精确、可验证的代码定位，在保持传统基准性能的同时，显著提升了结构推理能力。

Abstract: Code localization is a cornerstone of autonomous software engineering. Recent advancements have achieved impressive performance on real-world issue benchmarks. However, we identify a critical yet overlooked bias: these benchmarks are saturated with keyword references (e.g. file paths, function names), encouraging models to rely on superficial lexical matching rather than genuine structural reasoning. We term this phenomenon the Keyword Shortcut. To address this, we formalize the challenge of Keyword-Agnostic Logical Code Localization (KA-LCL) and introduce KA-LogicQuery, a diagnostic benchmark requiring structural reasoning without any naming hints. Our evaluation reveals a catastrophic performance drop of state-of-the-art approaches on KA-LogicQuery, exposing their lack of deterministic reasoning capabilities. We propose LogicLoc, a novel agentic framework that combines large language models with the rigorous logical reasoning of Datalog for precise localization. LogicLoc extracts program facts from the codebase and leverages an LLM to synthesize Datalog programs, with parser-gated validation and mutation-based intermediate-rule diagnostic feedback to ensure correctness and efficiency. The validated programs are executed by a high-performance inference engine, enabling accurate and verifiable localization in a fully automated, closed-loop workflow. Experimental results demonstrate that LogicLoc significantly outperforms SOTA methods on KA-LogicQuery while maintaining competitive performance on popular issue-driven benchmarks. Notably, LogicLoc attains superior performance with significantly lower token consumption and faster execution by offloading structural traversal to a deterministic engine, reducing the overhead of iterative LLM inference.

</details>


### [64] [LLMSniffer: Detecting LLM-Generated Code via GraphCodeBERT and Supervised Contrastive Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16058)
*Mahir Labib Dihan,Abir Muhtasim*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: LLMSniffer：基于两阶段监督对比学习的AI生成代码检测框架，在GPTSniffer和Whodunit数据集上显著优于基线方法


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大型语言模型在软件开发中的快速普及，区分AI生成代码与人类编写代码成为关键挑战，涉及学术诚信、代码质量保证和软件安全等重要领域

Method: 提出LLMSniffer检测框架，采用两阶段监督对比学习管道微调GraphCodeBERT，结合注释移除预处理和多层感知机分类器

Result: 在两个基准数据集上取得显著改进：GPTSniffer准确率从70%提升至78%（F1从68%到78%），Whodunit准确率从91%提升至94.65%（F1从91%到94.64%）。t-SNE可视化证实对比学习微调产生分离良好、紧凑的嵌入表示

Conclusion: LLMSniffer框架有效提升了AI生成代码的检测性能，通过对比学习优化了代码表示学习，并开源了模型检查点、数据集、代码和交互式演示以促进进一步研究

Abstract: The rapid proliferation of Large Language Models (LLMs) in software development has made distinguishing AI-generated code from human-written code a critical challenge with implications for academic integrity, code quality assurance, and software security. We present LLMSniffer, a detection framework that fine-tunes GraphCodeBERT using a two-stage supervised contrastive learning pipeline augmented with comment removal preprocessing and an MLP classifier. Evaluated on two benchmark datasets - GPTSniffer and Whodunit - LLMSniffer achieves substantial improvements over prior baselines: accuracy increases from 70% to 78% on GPTSniffer (F1: 68% to 78%) and from 91% to 94.65% on Whodunit (F1: 91% to 94.64%). t-SNE visualizations confirm that contrastive fine-tuning yields well-separated, compact embeddings. We release our model checkpoints, datasets, codes and a live interactive demo to facilitate further research.

</details>


### [65] [Bridging the Gap between User Intent and LLM: A Requirement Alignment Approach for Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16198)
*Jia Li,Ruiqi Bai,Yangkang Luo,Yiran Zhang,Wentao Yang,Zeyu Sun,Tiankuo Zhao,Dongming Jin,Lei Li,Zhi Jin*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: REA-Coder：一种需求对齐方法，通过识别LLM未正确理解的需求内容并进行对齐，然后基于对齐后的需求生成代码并验证对齐性，迭代此过程直至生成正确代码或达到最大迭代次数，显著提升代码生成性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代码生成方法都假设LLM能正确理解给定需求，但这一假设并不总是成立。为了填补这一空白，需要开发能够识别和纠正LLM需求理解偏差的方法。

Method: REA-Coder采用需求对齐方法：1) 识别LLM未正确理解的需求内容并进行对齐；2) 基于对齐后的需求生成代码；3) 验证生成的代码是否与需求对齐；4) 迭代执行需求对齐和代码生成过程，直到生成正确代码或达到最大迭代次数。

Result: 在4个LLM和5个编程基准测试中，REA-Coder优于所有先进基线方法。在五个基准数据集上平均提升分别为7.93%、30.25%、26.75%、8.59%和8.64%，证明了需求对齐对提升LLM代码生成性能的有效性。

Conclusion: 需求对齐是提升LLM代码生成性能的关键因素，REA-Coder通过迭代的需求对齐和代码生成过程，有效解决了LLM需求理解不准确的问题，显著提高了代码生成的质量和正确性。

Abstract: Code generation refers to automatically producing executable programs from user requirements. Recently, researchers have explored approaches to enhance the correctness of generated code with advanced large language models. Although achieving improvements, existing approaches focus on designing reasoning strategies or post-refinement methods to enhance code generation performance. Despite their differences, all these methods share a common assumption: the LLM can correctly understand the given requirement. However, this assumption does not always hold. To fill this gap, we propose REA-Coder, a requirement alignment approach to enhance the code generation performance of LLMs. REA-Coder involves first identifying the requirement content that does not align with LLMs and aligning the requirements. Then, based on the aligned requirements, LLMs generate code and further verify whether the generated code aligns with the requirements, iterating this process of requirement alignment and code generation until generating correct code or achieving the maximum number of iterations. Experimental results show that REA-Coder outperforms all advanced baselines on four LLMs across five programming benchmarks. Concretely, REA-Coder achieves average improvements of 7.93%, 30.25%, 26.75%, 8.59%, and 8.64% on the five benchmark datasets, demonstrating the effectiveness of requirement alignment for improving the code generation performance of LLMs.

</details>


### [66] [From Papers to Progress: Rethinking Knowledge Accumulation in Software Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16208)
*Jason Cusati,Chris Brown*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该论文通过分析ICSE 2026调查数据，揭示软件工程研究领域存在知识积累与整合的结构性障碍，并提出重构研究产物的四项原则以支持累积性科学进步。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件工程研究虽然产出快速增长，但领域内知识积累、整合和重用的能力存在持续担忧。研究者希望通过分析社区自身的观点来理解这些挑战，特别是研究生产力提升与知识合成机制不足之间的张力。

Method: 分析ICSE 2026未来软件工程预调查的280份全球分布式高经验研究者的响应数据，识别社区对研究挑战的认知模式，并基于观察诊断结构性障碍。

Result: 识别出四个相互关联的结构性崩溃：论文作为孤立知识单元；上下文和来源在出版流程中丢失；主张演变缺乏系统追踪；激励机制偏向新颖性而非整合。这些障碍阻碍了知识的累积性发展。

Conclusion: 提出四项技术无关的研究产物设计原则：主张与证据的结构化表示；方法论决策的可检查与来源感知文档；超越出版的长期可重用基板；个体激励与集体知识构建目标协调的治理机制。建议FOSE作为实验替代性产物设计的平台。

Abstract: Software engineering research has experienced rapid growth in both output and participation over the past decades. Yet concerns persist about the field's ability to accumulate, integrate, and reuse knowledge in ways that support long-term progress. To better understand how the community itself perceives these challenges, we analyze responses from the ICSE 2026 Future of Software Engineering pre-survey, which captures perspectives from 280 globally distributed and highly experienced researchers. Our analysis reveals a tension between increasing research productivity and the limited mechanisms available for synthesizing results, tracking evolving claims, and supporting cumulative understanding over time.
  Building on these observations, we diagnose four interrelated structural breakdowns: papers function as isolated knowledge units with claims embedded in prose; context and provenance are lost as knowledge moves through the publication pipeline; claims evolve without systematic tracking; and incentive structures favor novelty over consolidation. We argue that addressing these barriers requires rethinking the fundamental properties of research artifacts.
  We articulate four technology-agnostic principles for future research artifacts: structured and interpretable representations of claims and evidence; inspectable and provenance-aware documentation of methodological decisions; long-lived and reusable substrates that evolve beyond publication; and governance mechanisms that align individual incentives with collective knowledge-building goals. We discuss implications for research practice, publication norms, and community infrastructure, positioning FOSE as a venue for experimenting with alternative artifact designs that support cumulative scientific progress.

</details>


<div id='cs.HC'></div>

# cs.HC [[Back]](#toc)

### [67] [NEFFY 2.0: A Breathing Companion Robot: User-Centered Design and Findings from a Study with Ukrainian Refugees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15325)
*Ilona Buchem,Jessica Kazubski,Charly Goerke*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: NEFFY 2.0是一款为压力缓解设计的触觉慢呼吸陪伴社交机器人，通过用户中心设计开发，在乌克兰难民群体中验证了其相比纯音频条件能更显著降低主观压力感知。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为经历长期焦虑的个体（特别是难民等脆弱群体）提供低门槛、易获取的慢呼吸引导工具，通过具身化和多感官交互增强压力缓解效果。

Method: 采用用户中心设计方法开发NEFFY 2.0机器人，通过混合方法研究设计：实验比较机器人辅助呼吸干预vs纯音频条件，测量主观评分和生理指标（HR、HRV-RMSSD、RR、GSR），结合访谈收集定性数据，使用k-means聚类识别呼吸模式。

Result: 定性结果显示NEFFY 2.0被感知为直观、镇静且支持性强；调查显示机器人条件相比纯音频条件在主观压力降低方面效果显著更大；生理数据结果混杂且个体差异大；识别出三种呼吸实践模式。

Conclusion: NEFFY 2.0作为有前景的低门槛压力缓解工具，在脆弱群体中验证了机器人辅助干预相比非机器人条件的优势，为HRI赋能社会的愿景提供了实证支持。

Abstract: This paper presents the design of NEFFY 2.0, a social robot designed as a haptic slow-paced breathing companion for stress reduction, and reports findings from a mixed-methods user study with 14 refugees from Ukraine. Developed through a user-centered design process, NEFFY 2.0 builds on NEFFY 1.0 and integrates embodiment and multi-sensory interaction to provide low-threshold, accessible guidance of slow-paced breathing for stress relief, which may be particularly valuable for individuals experiencing prolonged periods of anxiety. To evaluate effectiveness, an experimental comparison of a robot-assisted breathing intervention versus an audio-only condition was conducted. Measures included subjective ratings and physiological indicators, such as heart rate (HR), heart rate variability (HRV) using RMSSD parameter, respiratory rate (RR), and galvanic skin response (GSR), alongside qualitative data from interviews exploring user experience and perceived support. Qualitative findings showed that NEFFY 2.0 was perceived as intuitive, calming and supportive. Survey results showed a substantially larger effect in significant reduction of perceived stress in the NEFFY 2.0 condition compared to audio-only. Physiological data reveled mixed results combined with large inter-personal variability. Three patterns of breathing practice with NEFFY 2.0 were identified using k-means clustering. Despite the small sample size, this study makes a novel contribution by providing empirical evidence of stress reduction in a vulnerable population through a direct comparison of robot-assisted and non-robot conditions. The findings position NEFFY 2.0 as a promising low-threshold tool that supports stress relief and contributes to the vision of HRI empowering society.

</details>


### [68] [Struggle as Flow: Challenge, Design, and Experience in Soulslike Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15318)
*Zhehao Sun,Yuanyuan Xu,Chi Zhen,Yin-Shan Lin,Miles Thorogood,Patricia Lasserre,Aleksandra Dulic,Megan Smith*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 该论文挑战传统游戏设计的享乐主义范式，研究"失败悖论"的心理机制，提出"韧性心流"概念，并通过分析《艾尔登法环》等游戏玩家评论验证了当难度设计清晰公平时，挫折可转化为掌握与正念的深刻体验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统游戏设计强调无障碍体验，但魂类游戏却通过高难度和频繁失败取得商业成功，形成"失败悖论"。论文旨在挑战传统享乐主义游戏范式，探究玩家从挫折中获得满足感的心理机制。

Method: 整合Csikszentmihalyi的心流理论和Juul的游戏学框架，提出"韧性心流"概念。通过定性文本分析，研究《艾尔登法环》、《只狼：影逝二度》和《黑暗之魂III》在Steam社区的600条有用用户评论，避免实验室限制。

Result: 研究发现长期玩家在语言上将死亡重构为教学而非惩罚，使用与节奏同步和冥想专注相关的词汇。当难度设计具有清晰性和公平性时，能培养"注意力伦理"，将数字斗争转化为掌握和正念的深刻体验。

Conclusion: 论文得出结论：精心设计的难度（清晰、公平）能够将挫折有意义地框架化，维持玩家的沉浸状态，形成"韧性心流"，使数字斗争成为掌握和正念的深刻体验，挑战了传统游戏设计的享乐主义假设。

Abstract: While traditional game design prioritizes friction-free accessibility, the Soulslike subgenre has achieved commercial dominance through punishing difficulty and frequent failure. This paper challenges the conventional hedonistic paradigm of gaming to investigate the psychological mechanisms behind the Paradox of Failure. By integrating Csikszentmihalyi's Flow Theory with Juul's ludological framework, we propose the concept of Resilient Flow. We define this as a cognitive state wherein absorption is maintained not despite frustration but through the meaningful framing of it. To validate this model without invasive laboratory constraints, we conducted a qualitative text analysis of 600 helpful user reviews from Elden Ring, Sekiro: Shadows Die Twice, and Dark Souls III via the Steam Community platform. Findings reveal that long-term players linguistically reframe death as pedagogy rather than punishment and utilize vocabulary associated with rhythmic synchronization and meditative focus. We conclude that when difficulty is designed with clarity and fairness, it fosters an Ethics of Attention and transforms digital struggle into a profound experience of mastery and mindfulness.

</details>


### [69] [Towards Measuring Interactive Visualization Abilities: Connecting With Existing Literacies and Assessments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15320)
*Gabriela Molina León,Benjamin Bach,Matheus Valentim,Niklas Elmqvist*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出需要开发评估人们与数据可视化交互能力的正式方法，因为现有的可视化素养测试仅涉及静态可视化，无法评估交互能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的可视化素养测试（如VLAT及其衍生版本）仅评估静态可视化的理解能力，而缺乏评估人们与数据可视化进行有效交互能力的正式方法。随着可视化交互在数据分析中的重要性日益增加，需要开发能够评估交互能力的评估工具。

Method: 作为立场论文，本文通过讨论相关的素养概念和评估方法，提出并比较了不同的评估方法，以评估人们在交互式意义建构任务中使用可视化时所依赖的能力。

Result: 本文提出了评估可视化交互能力的需求，并比较了不同的评估方法，为开发正式的交互能力评估工具奠定了基础。

Conclusion: 需要开发正式的评估方法来衡量人们与数据可视化进行有效交互的能力，这对于理解和支持交互式数据分析至关重要。

Abstract: How do we assess people's abilities to interact with data visualizations? The current state-of-the-art visualization literacy tests -- such as VLAT and its derivatives -- only involve the use of static visualizations. Despite advances in investigating multiple visualization abilities, we do not yet have formal methods to assess the ability of a person to interact with a data visualization effectively. In this position paper, we discuss related literacy concepts and assessments to propose and compare different approaches for assessing the abilities that people leverage to use visualizations in interactive sensemaking tasks.

</details>


### [70] [Beyond Content Exposure: Systemic Factors Driving Moderators' Mental Health Crisis in Africa](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15321)
*Nuredin Ali Abdelkadir,Tianling Yang,Shivani Kapania,Kauna Ibrahim Malgwi,Fasica Berhane Gebrekidan,Adio-Adet Dinika,Elaine O. Nsoesie,Milagros Miceli,Stevie Chancellor*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 非洲内容审核员心理健康状况恶化，研究发现系统性劳动条件比内容本身更具破坏性，企业健康计划无效，需要结构性解决方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非洲内容审核员心理健康问题尤为严重，现有研究主要关注内容本身的影响，但需要探究除内容外导致心理健康恶化的其他因素，特别是系统性劳动条件的影响。

Method: 混合方法研究：对134名非洲内容审核员进行问卷调查评估心理健康状况，并对15名审核员进行深度访谈以理解其经历背景。

Result: 非洲审核员心理困扰程度高，幸福感低于其他地区同行；离职审核员心理困扰显著更高，显示长期影响；系统性劳动条件（而非仅内容本身）是主要致因；平台推广的企业健康计划无效且不足。

Conclusion: 需要所有相关方采取整体关注和结构性解决方案来改善审核员心理健康，单纯依赖企业健康计划不足以解决问题。

Abstract: Content moderators review disturbing content to protect social media users, often at significant cost to their mental health. Recent reports document the mental health conditions of African moderators as notably problematic. Beyond the content itself, what factors contribute to the deteriorating mental health of these workers? We surveyed 134 moderators across Africa to understand their mental health and interviewed 15 moderators to contextualize their experiences. We found that African moderators suffer from high psychological distress and lower well-being compared to moderators in other areas. Former moderators showed significantly higher distress levels, demonstrating long term impact that extends beyond their moderation work. Our interviews showed that systemic and structural labor conditions contribute to moderators' severe psychological distress and diminished mental well-being. Corporate wellness programs promoted by platforms were found ineffective and inadequate. We discuss how this requires holistic attention and structural solutions by all involved parties to improve moderators' mental health.

</details>


### [71] [Acoustic and Facial Markers of Perceived Conversational Success in Spontaneous Speech](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15322)
*Thanushi Withanage,Elizabeth Redcay,Carol Espy-Wilson*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 分析Zoom自然对话中多模态协调与感知对话质量的关系，发现自发语音中的协调现象与更高感知成功相关


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究自然非任务虚拟环境中对话协调现象，探索对话动态与感知互动质量的关系，填补任务导向对话研究的空白

Method: 分析大规模自发Zoom对话语料库，提取多模态特征（话轮转换、停顿、面部运动、音高和强度等声学特征），通过事后评分因子分析量化感知对话成功

Result: 在自发语音中可靠检测到协调现象，且与更高感知对话成功显著相关

Conclusion: 识别出对话质量的关键交互标记，为针对性干预促进更有效和吸引人的沟通提供机会

Abstract: Individuals often align their speaking patterns with their interlocutors, a phenomenon linked to engagement and rapport. While well documented in task-oriented dialogues, less is known about entrainment in naturalistic, non-task and virtual settings. In this study, we analyze a large corpus of spontaneous dyadic Zoom conversations to examine how conversational dynamics relate to perceived interaction quality. We extract multimodal features encompassing turn-taking, pauses, facial movements, and acoustic measures such as pitch and intensity. Perceived conversational success was quantified via factor analysis of post-conversation ratings. Results demonstrate that entrainment reliably detected in spontaneous speech and correlates with higher perceived success. These findings identify key interactional markers of conversational quality and highlight opportunities for targeted interventions to foster more effective and engaging communication.

</details>


### [72] [A Systematic Review of User Experiments Measuring the Effects of Dark Patterns](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15323)
*Brennan Schaffner,Luis Heysen,Marshini Chetty*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 该综述系统分析了欺骗性/操纵性模式(DMP)的实验研究，发现DMP显著改变用户行为但效果差异大，外部干预大多无效，且DMP对所有人群影响相似。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管DMP的伦理和法律影响受到广泛关注，但其实际效果的经验证据分散且缺乏系统梳理。本研究旨在综合最新的实验研究，量化DMP对用户的影响，为研究和政策制定提供实证基础。

Method: 采用系统性文献综述方法，聚焦于量化DMP影响用户的实验研究，同时汇总针对减少DMP效果的干预措施的研究发现。

Result: 1. DMP显著改变用户行为，但效果大小存在较大差异；2. 外部干预措施大多未能有效减轻DMP的影响；3. DMP效果与个人特征（如年龄或政治倾向）之间的显著相关性不常见，表明DMP对几乎所有测试人群都有相似影响。

Conclusion: 通过总结实验证据，澄清了DMP的效果，突出了现有实验文献中的空白和矛盾，为持续的研究和政策方向提供了信息。DMP具有普遍影响且难以通过外部干预缓解，这强调了需要更有效的监管和设计干预措施。

Abstract: Deceptive/Manipulative Patterns (DMP) are interface designs, also known as ``dark patterns,'' that manipulate user behavior. While considerable attention has been paid to their ethical and legal implications, empirical evidence about their real-world effects remains diffuse. This review synthesizes up-to-date experimental studies, focusing on works that quantify how (or whether) DMPs influence users. We also aggregate findings on interventions aimed at reducing DMP effects. Our synthesis highlights the experimental agreement that DMPs do significantly alter user behavior (with large variance in effect size) and that external interventions have been mostly unsuccessful in mitigating their effects. Lastly, we show that significant correlations between DMP effects and personal characteristics (e.g., age or political affiliation) are uncommon, indicating DMPs similarly affected nearly all populations tested. By summarizing the experimental evidence, we clarify the effects of DMPs, highlight gaps and tensions in the existing experimental literature, and help inform ongoing research and policy directions.

</details>


### [73] [Struggle Premium : How Human Effort and Imperfection Drive Perceived Value in the Age of AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15324)
*Nazneen Sultana,Mst Rafia Islam,Md. Tanvir Hossain,Azmine Toushik Wasi*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 研究探讨"奋斗溢价"现象，即感知到的人类努力如何为创意作品增加价值，通过分析努力线索对人类和AI生成作品的评价影响


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI进入创意实践，观众在判断作品真实性和价值时面临不确定性。需要理解人类努力线索如何影响对创意作品的评价，特别是在人机协作创作背景下

Method: 对70名大学生进行问卷调查，重点分析四种努力线索：过程视频、时间记录、书面解释和瑕疵。研究这些线索如何影响对人类和AI生成创意作品的真实性和价值判断

Result: 过程导向线索（特别是视频和时间记录）对真实性和价值判断影响最大，瑕疵影响有限。72.9%参与者愿意为人类作品支付更多。努力线索也能改善AI生成内容的感知，表明过程透明度可以部分弥补真实性差距

Conclusion: 研究将努力启发式扩展到算法创造力领域，为设计透明的人机创意系统提供参考。过程透明度有助于弥合人类与AI生成内容之间的真实性差距

Abstract: As AI enters creative practice, audiences face growing uncertainty in judging authenticity and value. This study examines the Struggle Premium, the added value attributed to perceived human effort, by analyzing how visible effort cues influence evaluations of human- and AI-generated creative works. We surveyed 70 university students, focusing on process videos, time documentation, written explanations, and imperfections. Process-oriented cues, especially videos and time spent, most strongly shaped authenticity and value judgments, while imperfections had limited impact. Participants showed a clear preference for human-made works, with 72.9% willing to pay more. Notably, effort cues also improved perceptions of AI-generated content, suggesting that process transparency can partially bridge authenticity gaps. These findings extend the effort heuristic to algorithmic creativity and inform the design of transparent human-AI creative systems.

</details>


### [74] [Analyzing the Presentation, Content, and Utilization of References in LLM-powered Conversational AI Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15326)
*Jianheng Ouyang,Arpit Narechania*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 该研究系统分析了9个对话AI系统的引用质量与呈现方式，发现不同系统在引用数量、质量和呈现方式上存在显著差异，用户很少与引用互动，需要改进界面设计以提升引用可信度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着对话AI系统在信息检索和问答中的普及，其引用的可靠性对于确保答案可信度至关重要。然而，此前没有研究系统分析这些引用的呈现方式或质量，因此需要填补这一空白。

Method: 研究分析了来自9个系统的30个问答对中的1,517个引用，重点关注：(1) 用户界面中的引用呈现方式；(2) 使用CRAAP标准评估引用质量。同时进行了初步用户研究，观察用户与引用的互动行为。

Result: 发现不同系统在引用呈现、质量和数量上存在显著差异：ChatGPT平均每个回答提供9.5个引用，CRAAP得分15.48/20（质量较高）；Hunyuan-TurboS平均提供4.0个引用，得分11.65/20（质量较低）。用户研究显示人们很少与这些引用互动，且行为因系统而异。

Conclusion: 研究结果强调了改进界面设计的必要性，以帮助用户更有效地参与引用并增强对引用的信任，从而提升对话AI系统的整体可信度。

Abstract: As conversational AI systems become popular for information retrieval and question-answering, the references they cite are key to ensuring their answers are reliable and trustworthy. Yet, no prior work systematically analyzes how these references are presented or their quality. We examine 1,517 references from 30 question-answer pairs across nine systems, focusing on their (1) presentation in the user interface and (2) quality using the CRAAP criteria. We find notable variations in the presentation, quality, and quantity of references across systems. For instance, ChatGPT provides more references (9.5 per response on average) with higher quality (15.48/20 CRAAP score), while Hunyuan-TurboS provides fewer references (4.0) and lower quality (11.65/20). Additionally, a preliminary user study shows that people rarely interact with these references and that their behavior differs across systems. These findings highlight the need for better interface designs that help users engage with and trust references more effectively.

</details>


### [75] [Interpupillary Distance Constraints in Pediatric VR: Implications for Psychology and Psychotherapy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15328)
*Grzegorz Pochwatko*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 消费级VR头显的瞳距限制是儿科心理学和心理治疗中未被充分认识的方法学和临床问题，可能影响儿童参与、体验和治疗效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VR在心理学研究和治疗中的应用日益增多，但大多数消费级头显主要针对成人人体测量学设计，包括成人瞳距范围。这种设计可能导致儿童被排除在参与之外，或获得系统性的感知体验降级，因为设备无法充分适应他们的视觉解剖结构。

Method: 使用已发表的发育瞳距数据、官方头显规格参数以及儿科和青少年VR研究案例，分析人体测量学不匹配问题。以Meta Quest 3为案例研究，论证头显兼容性应被视为心理学方法的一部分而非背景技术细节。

Result: 人体测量学不匹配可能不成比例地影响年幼儿童和瞳距分布较低的个体。头显适配问题可能影响视觉舒适度、深度感知、注意力负荷、晕动症、模拟停留意愿和临场感，进而影响参与度、情绪处理、退出率和治疗反应。

Conclusion: VR头显可能作为一种选择机制，影响研究参与者的纳入、干预耐受性和研究结果的普适性。儿科VR研究和治疗应将头显兼容性视为心理学方法的重要组成部分，而非单纯的技术细节。

Abstract: Virtual reality (VR) is increasingly used across psychology, from research and assessment to counseling, psychological treatment, and psychotherapy, with growing applications for children and adolescents. In these contexts, VR is often treated as a relatively neutral delivery medium. This assumption may be misleading. Most consumer head-mounted displays (HMDs) have been designed primarily for adult anthropometry, including adult interpupillary distance (IPD) ranges. As a result, some children may be excluded from participation or may receive a systematically degraded perceptual experience because the device cannot be adequately aligned to their visual anatomy. This paper argues that IPD constraints in consumer VR headsets represent an underrecognized methodological and clinical problem in pediatric psychology and psychotherapy. If headset fit affects visual comfort, depth perception, attentional load, cybersickness, willingness to remain in the simulation, and sense of presence, it may also influence engagement, emotional processing, dropout, and treatment response. The headset may therefore function as a selection mechanism, shaping who is included in studies, who can tolerate intervention, and to whom findings can be generalized. Using published developmental IPD data, official headset specifications, and examples from pediatric and youth-oriented VR studies, we show that anthropometric mismatch is likely to disproportionately affect younger children and those at the lower end of the IPD distribution. Using Meta Quest 3 as a case study, we argue that pediatric VR research and therapy should treat headset compatibility as part of psychological method rather than as background technical detail.

</details>


### [76] [Evaluating LLMs as Human Surrogates in Controlled Experiments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15329)
*Adnan Hoq,Tim Weninger*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 评估现成LLM生成数据能否作为人类行为研究的替代品，通过比较LLM与人类在准确性感知实验中的反应


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM越来越多地用于模拟人类行为研究中的反应，需要明确LLM生成数据在何种情况下能支持与人类数据相同的实验推断

Method: 将人类观察转换为结构化提示，让现成LLM生成0-10的结果变量，不进行任务特定训练，对LLM和人类响应应用相同的统计分析

Result: LLM能再现人类观察到的多个方向性效应，但效应大小和调节模式因模型而异；现成LLM在受控条件下能捕捉总体信念更新模式，但不能一致匹配人类尺度的效应

Conclusion: 现成LLM生成的数据可以充当行为替代品，但需谨慎使用，因为它们能捕捉总体模式但无法精确匹配人类效应大小和调节模式

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used to simulate human responses in behavioral research, yet it remains unclear when LLM-generated data support the same experimental inferences as human data. We evaluate this by directly comparing off-the-shelf LLM-generated responses with human responses from a canonical survey experiment on accuracy perception. Each human observation is converted into a structured prompt, and models generate a single 0--10 outcome variable without task-specific training; identical statistical analyses are applied to human and synthetic responses. We find that LLMs reproduce several directional effects observed in humans, but effect magnitudes and moderation patterns vary across models. Off-the-shelf LLMs therefore capture aggregate belief-updating patterns under controlled conditions but do not consistently match human-scale effects, clarifying when LLM-generated data can function as behavioral surrogates.

</details>


### [77] [Making It Work Is the Work: Engineering Maturity as Epistemic Work](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15330)
*Danny Leen,Stig Konings,Raf Ramakers,Kris Luyten*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 论文指出HCI与制造系统原型难以复用和转移的根本原因是缺乏工程成熟度所需的知识积累，提出了"Fab-ilities"六个维度框架来评估制造系统的可转移性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多HCI与制造系统作为原型很有吸引力，但难以在实际中复用、扩展或转移。传统解释认为这需要时间，但作者认为根本问题在于：系统在不同材料、机器和用户中的行为知识在发表时并不存在，因为产生这些知识的工作很少受到激励或奖励。

Method: 基于工程认识论和系统导向HCI的先前讨论，将工程成熟度重新定义为认识论工作：持续工程努力产生的知识，这些知识是原型制作无法揭示的。提出了六个维度（Fab-ilities）作为描述制造系统哪些方面已确立、哪些知识仍处于隐性状态的词汇表：(1)可构建性，(2)可执行性，(3)可靠性，(4)可维护性，(5)可转移性，(6)可扩展性。通过作者自己的五个项目（JigFab、StoryStick++、Silicone Devices、LamiFold和PaperPulse）进行案例研究，展示了不同传播尝试（商业化、衍生公司、市场探索）如何暴露已发表内容与实际转移需求之间的差距。

Result: 通过五个项目的实践分析，揭示了原型系统向实际应用转移过程中存在的知识差距。Fab-ilities框架提供了一个系统化的评估工具，帮助识别制造系统在不同维度上的成熟度水平，以及哪些方面需要进一步的知识积累才能实现真正的可转移性。

Conclusion: HCI与制造系统的可转移性问题不仅是时间问题，更是知识积累问题。需要将工程成熟度视为认识论工作，通过持续的工程努力填补原型制作无法揭示的知识空白。Fab-ilities框架为评估和推进制造系统的可转移性提供了概念工具，有助于促进HCI制造系统从原型向实际应用的转化。

Abstract: Many HCIxfabrication systems are compelling as prototypes but remain difficult to reuse, extend, or transfer beyond their original publication. A common explanation is that adoption simply takes time. We argue that the issue is more fundamental. The knowledge needed to make fabrication systems transferable, namely how they behave across different materials, machines, and users, usually does not exist at the time of publication because the work required to generate this knowledge is rarely incentivized or rewarded. Drawing on engineering epistemology and prior debates in systems-oriented HCI, we reframe engineering maturity as epistemic work: sustained engineering effort that produces knowledge which prototyping alone cannot reveal.
