We propose six dimensions, Fab-ilities, as a vocabulary to describe what aspects of fabrication artifacts have become established and what knowledge remains tacit: (1) buildability, (2) executability, (3) reliability, (4) maintainability, (5) transferability, and (6) scalability. We describe five of our own projects (JigFab, StoryStick++, Silicone Devices, LamiFold, and PaperPulse), where varied attempts at dissemination, such as commercialization, spin-offs, and market exploration, each exposed different gaps between what we published and what transfer actually required.

</details>


### [78] [How people use Copilot for Health](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15331)
*Beatriz Costa-Gomes,Pavel Tolmachev,Eloise Taysom,Viknesh Sounderajah,Hannah Richardson,Philipp Schoenegger,Xiaoxuan Liu,Matthew M Nour,Seth Spielman,Samuel F. Way,Yash Shah,Michael Bhaskar,Harsha Nori,Christopher Kelly,Peter Hames,Bay Gross,Mustafa Suleyman,Dominic King*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 研究分析了50万+微软Copilot健康对话，发现近20%涉及个人症状评估，40%为通用健康信息，14%为他人健康咨询，夜间个人健康查询显著增加，移动端侧重个人健康，桌面端侧重专业工作，大量查询聚焦医疗系统导航问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 了解用户如何通过对话式AI（微软Copilot）咨询健康问题，包括查询意图、主题分布、使用模式（设备、时间）、以及这些对话如何反映个人健康需求、照护角色和医疗系统摩擦，为健康AI的负责任开发提供数据支持。

Method: 收集2026年1月50万+脱敏健康对话；开发包含12个主要类别的分层意图分类法，使用隐私保护的LLM分类并经过专家人工标注验证；应用LLM驱动的主题聚类分析每个意图内的流行主题；分析查询意图、主题、对象（自己/他人）、设备（移动/桌面）和时间（白天/夜晚）的使用模式。

Result: 1) 近20%对话涉及个人症状评估或病情讨论；40%通用健康信息集中于具体治疗和病症，表明个人健康意图比例可能更高。2) 14%个人健康查询涉及他人（子女、父母、伴侣），显示AI可作为照护工具。3) 症状和情绪健康查询在夜间显著增加。4) 移动端集中于个人健康问题，桌面端以专业/学术工作为主。5) 大量查询聚焦医疗系统导航（找医生、理解保险），凸显现有医疗服务的摩擦。

Conclusion: 对话式AI在健康领域的使用模式揭示了重要的用户需求：个人健康管理、照护支持、非传统服务时间的健康咨询、以及医疗系统导航帮助。这些发现对平台特定设计、安全考量和健康AI的负责任开发具有直接意义，表明AI不仅是个体健康工具，也是照护支持和医疗系统导航的辅助手段。

Abstract: We analyze over 500,000 de-identified health-related conversations with Microsoft Copilot from January 2026 to characterize what people ask conversational AI about health. We develop a hierarchical intent taxonomy of 12 primary categories using privacy-preserving LLM-based classification validated against expert human annotation, and apply LLM-driven topic-clustering for prevalent themes within each intent. Using this taxonomy, we characterize the intents and topics behind health queries, identify who these queries are about, and analyze how usage varies by device and time of day. Five findings stand out. First, nearly one in five conversations involve personal symptom assessment or condition discussion, and even the dominant general information category (40%) is concentrated on specific treatments and conditions, suggesting that this is a lower bound on personal health intent. Second, one in seven of these personal health queries concern someone other than the user, such as a child, a parent, a partner, suggesting that conversational AI can be a caregiving tool, not just a personal one. Third, personal queries about symptoms and emotional health queries increase markedly in the evening and nighttime hours, when traditional healthcare is most limited. Fourth, usage diverges sharply by device: mobile concentrates on personal health concerns, while desktop is dominated by professional and academic work. Fifth, a substantial share of queries focuses on navigating healthcare systems such as finding providers, and understanding insurance, highlighting friction in the delivery of existing healthcare. These patterns have direct implications for platform-specific design, safety considerations, and the responsible development of health AI.

</details>


### [79] [Automating Crash Diagram Generation Using Vision-Language Models: A Case Study on Multi-Lane Roundabouts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15332)
*Xiao Lu,Hao Zhen,Jidong J. Yang*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本研究探索使用视觉语言模型(VLMs)自动从警方事故报告中生成事故示意图，以多车道环岛为测试案例，开发了三部分结构化提示框架和10指标评估系统，测试了GPT-4o、Gemini-1.5-Flash和Janus-4o三个模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统事故示意图的手动绘制耗时且存在人为差异，需要自动化解决方案来提高交通安全分析的效率和一致性。

Method: 开发了三部分结构化提示框架（解释、提取、视觉合成）来指导模型推理，设计了10指标评估系统评估语义准确性、空间保真度和视觉清晰度，在79份事故报告上测试了GPT-4o、Gemini-1.5-Flash和Janus-4o三个VLMs模型。

Result: GPT-4o获得最高平均性能（6.29/10），其次是Gemini-1.5-Flash（5.28）和Janus-4o（3.64）。GPT-4o在空间推理和提取数据与可视化数据对齐方面表现优异。

Conclusion: VLMs在工程可视化任务中显示出潜力但也存在局限，为将生成式AI集成到事故分析工作流程以提高效率、一致性和可解释性奠定了基础。

Abstract: Crash diagrams are essential tools in transportation safety analysis, yet their manual preparation remains time-consuming and prone to human variability. This study investigates the use of Vision-Language Models (VLMs) to automate crash diagram generation from police crash reports, focusing on multilane roundabouts as a challenging test case. A three-part structured prompt framework was developed to guide model reasoning through interpretation, extraction, and visual synthesis, while a 10-metric evaluation system was designed to assess diagram quality in terms of semantic accuracy, spatial fidelity, and visual clarity. Three popular models, including GPT-4o, Gemini-1.5-Flash, and Janus-4o, were tested on 79 crash reports. GPT-4o achieved the highest average performance (6.29 out of 10), followed by Gemini-1.5-Flash (5.28) and Janus-4o (3.64). The analysis revealed GPT-4o's superior spatial reasoning and alignment between extracted and visualized crash data. These results highlight both the promise and current limitations of VLMs in engineering visualization tasks. The study lays the groundwork for integrating generative AI into crash analysis workflows to improve efficiency, consistency, and interpretability.

</details>


### [80] [Technically Love: The Evolution of Human-AI Romance Discourse on Reddit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15333)
*Tyler Chang,Jina Huh-Yoo,Afsaneh Razi*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 分析Reddit上3383条自述AI浪漫伴侣帖子（2017-2025），发现讨论主题从积极亲密关系转向平台治理、技术问题和现实后果，揭示了人类-AI浪漫关系从私人体验到技术调解与监管的框架转变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类-AI浪漫关系日益普遍，但公众对此类关系的讨论如何随时间演变尚不清楚。先前研究关注用户体验和伦理问题，但缺乏对用户自发公共讨论的纵向分析。

Method: 使用高精度数据集（Reddit上3383条自述AI浪漫伴侣帖子，2017-2025年），采用主题建模和时间统计分析，识别主导主题及其随时间演变。

Result: 发现显著的主题漂移：讨论从积极的亲密关系转向平台治理、技术问题和现实后果。这些转变表明人类-AI浪漫关系的框架从私人体验转向技术调解与监管。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了人类-AI浪漫关系公共讨论的演变模式，对伴侣AI系统的设计和治理具有重要启示，强调需要关注从私人体验到技术调解与监管的框架转变。

Abstract: Human-AI romantic relationships are increasingly common, yet little is understood about how public discourse around them emerges and shifts over time. Prior research has examined user experiences and ethical concerns, but lacks longitudinal analyses of user-initiated public discussions. We address this gap by analyzing a high-precision dataset of 3,383 self-disclosed romantic companion AI posts from Reddit (2017-2025), using topic modeling and temporal statistical analysis to identify dominant themes and their evolution over time. We find significant topic drift, with discussions moving away from positive intimate relationships toward platform governance, technical issues, and real-world consequences. These shifts highlight a transition in how human-AI romance is framed-moving from private experiences to technical mediation and regulation-with implications for the design and governance of companion AI systems.

</details>


### [81] [Beyond Passive Viewing: A Pilot Study of a Hybrid Learning Platform Augmenting Video Lectures with Conversational AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15334)
*Mohammed Abraar,Raj Abhijit Dandekar,Rajat Dandekar,Sreedath Panat*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: AI增强视频平台通过集成对话式AI导师改善在线AI教育，相比传统视频教学显著提升学习效果和参与度


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线AI教育规模急剧扩大，但传统视频教学存在系统性缺陷，无法维持学习者参与度和促进深度概念掌握，需要探索更有效的教学方法

Method: 采用顺序组内设计，58名参与者先完成传统视频教学，再使用AI增强视频平台（集成对话式AI导师）。通过即时后测和延迟保留测试（2周后）评估学习效果，并分析行为数据

Result: AI增强教学在即时后测中显示大效应量（d=1.505），分数提高8.3分（91.8 vs 83.5/100，p<.001）；参与度持续时间提升71.1%；为对话式AI导师增强传统教育提供了初步证据

Conclusion: 对话式AI导师可能有效增强传统教育交付，为开发可扩展、适应性学习系统提供了潜在途径，但需要更大规模研究验证

Abstract: The exponential growth of AI education has brought millions of learners to online platforms, yet this massive scale has simultaneously exposed critical pedagogical shortcomings. Traditional video-based instruction, while cost-effective and scalable, demonstrates systematic failures in both sustaining learner engagement and facilitating the deep conceptual mastery essential for AI literacy. We present a pilot study evaluating a novel hybrid learning platform that integrates real-time conversational AI tutors with traditional video lectures. Our controlled experiment (N = 58, mean age M = 21.4, SD = 2.8) compared traditional video-based instruction with our AI-augmented video platform. This study employed a sequential within-subjects design where all participants first completed the traditional video condition followed by the AI-augmented condition, providing direct comparisons of learning outcomes. We measured learning effectiveness through immediate post-tests and delayed retention assessments (2-week delay). Results suggest improvements in learning performance: immediate post-test performance showed a large effect size (d = 1.505) with participants scoring 8.3 points higher after AI-augmented instruction (91.8 vs 83.5 out of 100, p < .001). Behavioral analytics revealed increased engagement duration (71.1% improvement with AI tutoring) in the experimental group. This pilot study provides preliminary evidence that conversational AI tutors may enhance traditional educational delivery, suggesting a potential avenue for developing scalable, adaptive learning systems.

</details>


### [82] [A Comparative Study on the Impact of Traditional Learning and Interactive Learning on Students' Academic Performance and Emotional Well-Being](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15335)
*Siva Raja Sindiramutty*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 研究比较了传统与互动学习方法对大学生学术表现、参与度、动机和情感健康的影响，发现互动学习组在测试成绩、行为与情感参与度方面显著优于传统组，但认知参与度下降提示可能存在认知负荷问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着互动学习工具在高等教育中的普及，需要实证研究比较传统与互动学习方法对学生表现和福祉的影响，以指导教学实践优化。

Method: 采用随机对照实验设计，将100名计算机入侵检测课程的大学生随机分为传统学习组（讲座和笔记）和互动学习组（使用Kahoot、Panopto、Slido、Quizizz、Padlet等工具）。通过前测、后测、期末考试和作业评估学术成就，使用经过验证的Likert量表问卷测量参与度和情感状态。

Result: 互动学习组在后测（67.48% vs 53.36%）和期末考试（80.8% vs 61.44%）中显著优于传统组。互动学习者的行为参与度（+67.01%）和情感参与度（+75.32%）更高，积极情绪增加（+66.67%），挫折感减少。但认知参与度显著下降（-39.8%），提示可能存在认知负荷。

Conclusion: 研究证实了互动学习的教学潜力，但强调了平衡刺激与认知水平的重要性。建议未来研究使用更大、更多样化的样本进行推广和结果最大化。

Abstract: The growing adoption of interactive learning tools in higher education offers new opportunities to enhance student performance and well-being. This study compares the effects of traditional and interactive learning methods on academic performance, engagement, motivation, and emotional well-being among 100 university students enrolled in a computer intrusion detection course. Participants were randomly assigned to either a traditional learning group (lectures and notes) or an interactive learning group utilising tools such as Kahoot, Panopto, Slido, Quizizz, Padlet, and educational videos. Academic achievement was measured through pre-tests, post-tests, final exams, and assignments, while engagement and emotional states were assessed using validated Likert-scale questionnaires. Results showed that students in the interactive group significantly outperformed their peers in both post-tests (67.48% vs. 53.36%) and final exams (80.8% vs. 61.44%). Interactive learners also demonstrated greater behavioural (+67.01%) and emotional engagement (+75.32%), along with enhanced emotional well-being marked by increased positive emotions (+66.67%) and reduced frustration. A significant drop in cognitive involvement (-39.8%) indicates possible cognitive overload. The pedagogical potential of interactive learning is reaffirmed by this result while reinforcing the need for balancing stimulation and cognitive level. Future research with larger, diverse samples is suggested for generalising and maximising outcomes.

</details>


### [83] [Uncertainty, Vagueness, and Ambiguity in Human-Robot Interaction: Why Conceptualization Matters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15339)
*Xiaowen Sun,Cornelius Weber,Matthias Kerzel,Josua Spisak,Stefan Wermter*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 该论文为HRI中的不确定性、模糊性和歧义性提出了统一的概念基础，澄清了这些常被混淆的概念的定义、区别和相互关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在HRI研究中，不确定性、模糊性和歧义性这些概念常被混淆，存在矛盾的定义和不一致的术语使用，这种概念混乱和术语不一致削弱了实证可比性，阻碍了理论积累。

Method: 首先查阅字典分析这三个术语的含义，然后在HRI背景下分析它们的区别和相互关系，通过示例说明这些特征，最后展示这一概念基础如何促进新方法的设计和现有方法的评估。

Result: 提出了一个一致的概念基础，澄清了不确定性、模糊性和歧义性在HRI中的定义、区别和相互关系，为相关方法的设计和评估提供了框架。

Conclusion: 建立一致的概念基础对于解决HRI中的不确定性、模糊性和歧义性挑战至关重要，这有助于提高实证可比性，促进理论积累，并为相关方法的设计和评估提供指导。

Abstract: Uncertainty, vagueness, and ambiguity are closely related and often confused concepts in human-robot interaction (HRI). In earlier studies, these concepts have been defined in contradictory ways and described using inconsistent terminology. This conceptual confusion and lack of terminological consistency undermine empirical comparability, thereby slowing the accumulation of theory. Consequently, consistent concepts that clarify these challenges, including their definitions, distinctions, and interrelationships, are needed in HRI. To address this lack of clarity, this paper proposes a consistent conceptual foundation for the challenges of uncertainty, vagueness, and ambiguity in HRI. First, we examine the meanings of these three terms in dictionaries. We then analyze the nature of their distinctions and interrelationships within the context of HRI. We further illustrate these characteristics through examples. Finally, we demonstrate how this consistent conceptual foundation facilitates the design of novel methods and the evaluation of existing methodologies for these phenomena.

</details>


### [84] [Radical Gender Neutrality: Agender Euphoria in Gaming and Play Experiences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15337)
*Katie Seaborn,Shano Liang,Rua M. Williams,Phoebe O. Toups Dugas*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 该研究探讨了"无性别愉悦"这一新概念，即从无性别体现、空间和活动中获得的幸福感，并探索了视频游戏如何为无性别及类似身份群体创造这种体验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然视频游戏可以为性别少数群体提供性别愉悦体验的安全空间，但无性别愉悦体验的可能性尚未被探索。本研究旨在填补这一空白，关注这一被忽视的自我实现框架。

Method: 采用关键事件技术(CIT)，对142名认同拥有或渴望无性别愉悦体验的人进行研究，分析游戏和游戏体验如何创造（或抑制）无性别愉悦。

Result: 揭示了无性别愉悦这一体验现象，并提供了基于实证的游戏设计标准，旨在为所有人（尤其是无性别及类似身份玩家）引发无性别愉悦体验。

Conclusion: 这项工作为游戏研究和人机交互领域中关于边缘化体验的批判性文献做出了贡献，强调了为无性别及类似身份群体设计包容性游戏体验的重要性。

Abstract: Agender euphoria is a new term representing the powerful feelings of happiness, joy, and contentment derived from experiences in gender-free embodiments, spaces, and activities. People with and without agender and adjacent identities (e.g., genderless, gender-free, non-binary, gender-apathetic) may have such experiences under the right circumstances. Video games can offer gender minorities a safe haven for gender euphoric experiences. However, the possibility of agender euphoric experiences was unexplored. We considered this overlooked frame of self-actualization with 142 people who identified as having or desiring agender euphoric experiences. Using the critical incident technique (CIT), we uncovered how games and play experiences create (and inhibit) agender euphoria. We surface this experiential phenomenon and provide empirically-grounded criteria for the design of games to elicit agender euphoric experiences for everyone, but especially agender and agender adjacent players. This work adds to the growing critical literatures on marginalized experiences in games research and human-computer interaction.

</details>


### [85] [Access Over Deception: Fighting Deceptive Patterns through Accessibility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15338)
*Tobias Pellkvist,Katie Seaborn,Miu Kojima*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 研究探讨了Web内容可访问性指南(WCAG)和相关立法能否作为对抗欺骗性设计模式的工具，通过启发式评估分析了欺骗性模式是否违反这些指南，识别出计时器、自动播放和隐藏信息三种模式受到WCAG影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 欺骗性设计模式（黑暗模式）被广泛用于操纵用户做出违背自身利益的行为，尤其影响教育程度较低、视力障碍和老年用户。虽然可访问性是用户体验、开发标准和法律的关键特征，但需要研究现有可访问性指南和立法能否作为对抗这些欺骗性模式的工具。

Method: 采用启发式评估方法，分析欺骗性设计模式是否违反或符合Web内容可访问性指南(WCAG)和相关立法（如欧洲可访问性法案EAA）。通过系统评估确定哪些欺骗性模式与可访问性要求存在冲突。

Result: 统计分析显示不同模式类型之间没有显著差异，但识别出三种受到WCAG指南影响的欺骗性模式：倒计时计时器、自动播放和隐藏信息。这些模式被发现与可访问性原则存在冲突。

Conclusion: WCAG和相关立法可以作为对抗UI欺骗的工具之一，支持包容性设计。该方法为打击基于用户界面的欺骗行为提供了实用框架，强调了可访问性标准在保护用户免受操纵性设计影响方面的潜在作用。

Abstract: Deceptive patterns, dark patterns, and manipulative user interfaces (UI) are a widely used design strategy that manipulates users to act against their own interests in pursuit of shareholder aims. These patterns may particularly affect people with less education, visual impairments, and older adults. Yet, access is a critical feature of the user experience (UX), development standards, and law. We considered whether and how the Web Content Accessibility Guidelines (WCAG) and related legislation, like the European Accessibility Act (EAA), could act as a tool against deceptive patterns. We used heuristic evaluation to analyze whether and how deceptive patterns violate or conform to these guidelines and legal statutes. Although statistical analysis revealed no significant differences by pattern type, we identified three patterns implicated by the WCAG guidelines: Countdown Timer, Auto-Play, and Hidden Information. We offer this approach as one tool in the fight against UI-based deception and in support of inclusive design.

</details>


### [86] [Restoration, Exploration and Transformation: How Youth Engage Character.AI Chatbots for Feels, Fun and Finding themselves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15340)
*Annabel Blake,Marcus Carter,Eduardo Velloso*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 青少年在Character.AI Discord社群中通过AI角色进行情感调节、创意探索和身份发展的研究


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前生成式AI研究主要关注成人设计的工具和实验，缺乏对青少年自主、游戏化使用方式的了解。本研究旨在探索青少年如何通过Character.AI平台进行自我导向的AI互动。

Method: 分析了Character.AI官方Discord中4,172名用户的讨论内容，识别出高度参与用户的人口统计特征（50%为13-17岁青少年，61.9%为女性或非二元性别，59%创建了自己的角色）。

Result: 发现青少年使用AI进行游戏化、情感化和创造性实践，提出了三种参与意图框架（恢复-情感调节、探索-创意实验、转变-身份发展）和七种青少年创建的角色原型分类。

Conclusion: 青少年为AI创造了新颖角色，揭示了当前AI体验与青少年使用之间的关键不匹配，为研究人员和从业者设计以青少年为中心的AI未来提供了框架。

Abstract: Young people are among the fastest adopters of generative AI, yet research emphasises adult-designed tools and experiments rather than playful, self-directed youth use. We analysed discourse from 4,172 users in Character.AI's official Discord, finding that the most engaged users were predominantly adolescents (50% aged 13-17), female or non-binary (61.9%), with most (59%) creating their own characters. We contribute (1) a descriptive account of how highly-engaged youth on Character.AI's Discord use AI for playful, emotional, and creative practices that push the platform limits; (2) a framework of three engagement intents -- Restoration (emotional regulation), Exploration (creative experimentation), and Transformation (identity development); and (3) a taxonomy of seven youth-created character archetypes. Together, these findings reveal how youth invent novel roles for AI, expose critical misalignments between youth use and current AI experiences, and provide frameworks for researchers and practitioners to design youth-centred AI futures.

</details>


### [87] [MRGEN: A Conceptual Framework for LLM-Powered Mixed Reality Authoring Tools for Education](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15341)
*Mohammed Oussama Seddini,Mohamed Ez-Zaouia,Ngoc Luyen Le,Iza Marfisi*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: MRGEN框架利用LLM帮助教师创建移动设备MR学习活动，减少36%任务时间，90%参与者认为AI辅助有帮助


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合现实为教育提供沉浸式多模态机会，但教师缺乏技术专业知识难以创作MR内容，需要降低创作门槛

Method: 提出MRGEN概念框架，包含学习目标、MR模态和GAI辅助三个维度；基于MIXAP平台实现原型，对24名参与者进行用户研究

Result: LLM辅助创作平均减少36%任务时间；超过90%参与者认为AI支持对头脑风暴、内容结构和学习目标对齐有帮助

Conclusion: MRGEN框架验证了AI辅助MR创作工具的可行性，为未来AI增强的MR教育工具提供了有前景的方向

Abstract: Mixed Reality (MR) offers immersive and multimodal opportunities for education but remains difficult for teachers to author without technical expertise. We propose MRGEN, a conceptual framework for LLM-powered authoring tools to support teachers in creating MR learning activities that work on mobile devices (tablets and smartphones). MRGEN articulates three axes: Learning Objectives, MR Modality, and GAI Assistance. To validate our framework, we implemented a prototype based on the open-source MIXAP authoring platform and conducted a user study with 24 participants. Results show that LLM-powered authoring reduced task duration by 36% on average, and that over 90% of participants found the AI support helpful for brainstorming, structuring, and aligning content with their learning goals. These findings yielded very promising results for future AI-assisted MR authoring tools.

</details>


### [88] [SuperProvenanceWidgets: Tracking and Visualizing Analytic Provenance Across UI Control Elements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15342)
*Antariksh Verma,Kaustubh Odak,Arpit Narechania*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: SuperProvenanceWidgets扩展了ProvenanceWidgets库，新增了跨多个UI控件追踪和可视化交互来源的SuperWidget，支持审计分析工作流、减少探索偏见和促进UI设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ProvenanceWidgets只能追踪单个UI控件的交互来源，缺乏对跨控件交互关系的理解。需要扩展功能以支持用户理解不同UI控件之间的使用关联、时序和依赖关系。

Method: 开发SuperProvenanceWidgets作为ProvenanceWidgets的扩展，引入新的SuperWidget组件，能够追踪和可视化多个UI控件之间的交互来源。通过三个使用场景展示功能，并使用认知维度符号框架进行技术自评估。

Result: SuperWidget成功实现了跨控件来源追踪，在三个应用场景中证明其有效性：(1)审计和共享分析工作流，(2)发现和减轻探索偏见，(3)促进用户界面设计和个性化。技术评估显示该库对开发者具有良好可用性。

Conclusion: SuperProvenanceWidgets扩展了来源追踪能力，从单个控件扩展到跨控件关系，为开发者构建高级来源应用提供了强大工具。该开源库已集成到ProvenanceWidgets中，支持工作流审计、偏见缓解和UI设计优化。

Abstract: ProvenanceWidgets is an existing JavaScript library that tracks the recency and frequency of user interactions with individual UI controls (e.g., range sliders and dropdowns) and dynamically overlays this provenance onto them. In this work, we introduce SuperProvenanceWidgets, an extension to ProvenanceWidgets featuring a new SuperWidget that similarly tracks and visualizes provenance but across multiple UI controls, enabling users to understand how, when, and whether different UI controls were used. Through three example usage scenarios, we demonstrate how this cross-control SuperWidget helps (a) audit and share analysis workflows, (b) surface and mitigate exploration biases, and (c) facilitate user interface design and personalization. We also perform a technical self-assessment using the Cognitive Dimensions of Notations to evaluate the library's usability for developers. SuperProvenanceWidgets is integrated into the ProvenanceWidgets library and is available as open-source software at ProvenanceWidgets.github.io, empowering developers to build advanced provenance applications.

</details>


### [89] [Improving Recycling Accuracy across UK Local Authorities: A Prototype for Citizen Engagement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15345)
*Chloé Greenstreet,Anastasia Vayona,Jane Henriksen-Bulmer*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本研究通过混合方法识别英国家庭回收障碍，开发交互式应用原型，使回收准确率提升60%，证明以用户为中心的技术方案能有效改善回收行为，但需与系统性改进互补。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管公众有回收意愿，但英国分散的地方当局废物管理系统导致公民困惑和"愿望回收"（将不可回收物品放入回收箱）。新的Simpler Recycling政策增加了识别、分类和清洁要求，需要公民指导以确保理解这些变化对其回收实践的影响。

Method: 采用混合方法研究：问卷调查（n=50）、专家访谈和设计活动。使用价值主张画布识别公民痛点：标识混乱、物流限制和当地要求信息缺口。然后开发交互式原型应用，提供位置特定指导、可视化分类辅助和材料特定信息。

Result: 焦点小组评估显示原型将回收准确率提高了60%，包装评估有明显改善。以用户为中心设计为基础的技术解决方案可显著改善回收行为并减少污染。

Conclusion: 技术解决方案在补充（而非替代）地方当局沟通和服务设计的系统性改进时最为有效。用户中心设计方法能有效解决回收障碍，但需要与政策实施和基础设施改进相结合。

Abstract: Despite public motivation to recycle, significant barriers hinder effective household recycling in the UK. Decentralised local authority waste management creates citizen confusion and "wishcycling" (disposing of non-recyclable items in recycling bins). The recent Simpler Recycling Policy further complicates this landscape by mandating new identification, sorting, and cleaning requirements that will require citizen guidance to ensure they understand how these will impact their recycling practices.
  This mixed methods study (surveys n=50, expert interviews, design activities) used the Value Proposition Canvas to identify citizen pain points: confusion about logos, logistical constraints, and information gaps about local requirements. We then developed an interactive prototype application providing location-specific guidance, visual sorting aids, and material-specific information to address these painpoints.
  Focus group evaluation showed the prototype improved recycling accuracy by 60 percent, with marked improvements in packaging assessment. Technology-enabled solutions grounded in user-centred design can measurably improve recycling behaviours and reduce contamination. However, such solutions are most effective when complementing (rather than substituting for) systemic improvements in local authority communication and service design.

</details>


### [90] [GazeSync: A Mobile Eye-Tracking Tool for Analyzing Visual Attention on Dynamically Manipulated Content](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15348)
*Yaxiong Lei,Rishab Talwar,Shijing He,Xinya Gong,Yuheng Wang,Xudong Cai,Zhongliang Guo,Juan Ye*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: GazeSync：一种移动眼动追踪系统，通过同步设备眼动估计与实时图像变换矩阵，解决动态内容中静态坐标失去语义意义的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统移动眼动追踪将注视映射到静态屏幕坐标，当用户进行捏合、缩放、旋转等操作时，静态坐标相对于底层视觉内容失去语义意义，无法准确捕捉用户在动态内容上的注意力。

Method: 提出GazeSync系统，同步设备眼动估计与实时图像变换矩阵（缩放、旋转和平移），通过记录注视坐标与精确UI状态，实现图像相对注意力模式的准确重建。

Result: 通过引导操作、阅读和视觉搜索任务的实证研究验证，GazeSync能够恢复变换内容上的真实注视位置，明显优于静态基线方法，同时揭示了校准漂移和复合操作下重建脆弱性的关键边界。

Conclusion: GazeSync提供了一种可重用的移动系统，能够准确重建图像相对注意力模式，将视觉注意力与设备交互解耦，为动态内容中的眼动追踪研究提供了有效工具。

Abstract: Conventional mobile eye-tracking maps gaze to static screen coordinates, failing to capture user attention when content is dynamic. As users pinch, zoom, and rotate images, static coordinates lose their semantic meaning relative to the underlying visual content. To address this methodological gap, we present \textit{GazeSync}, a reusable mobile system that synchronizes on-device gaze estimation with real-time image transformation matrices (scale, rotation, and translation). By logging gaze coordinates alongside precise UI states, GazeSync enables the accurate reconstruction of \textit{image-relative} attention patterns, decoupling visual attention from device interaction. We validate our end-to-end toolchain through a formative study involving guided manipulation, reading, and visual search tasks. Our results demonstrate GazeSync's ability to recover ground-truth gaze locations on transforming content, explicitly showing how it outperforms static baselines, while also surfacing critical boundaries regarding calibration drift and reconstruction fragility under compound manipulations.

</details>


### [91] [The Crutch or the Ceiling? How Different Generations of LLMs Shape EFL Student Writings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15460)
*Hengky Susanto,David James Woo,Chingyi Yeung,Stephanie Wing Yan Lo-Philip,Chi Ho Yeung*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本研究探讨大型语言模型在辅助中学生英语写作中的作用，分析ChatGPT发布前后学生作文变化，发现高级LLM能提升低水平学习者的评分和词汇多样性，但过度依赖会降低专家评分，需将AI功能与学习过程对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要关注LLM输出质量，而本研究旨在探索LLM发展演变及其对EFL学生写作的真实影响，评估更智能的模型是作为真正的脚手架还是仅仅作为补偿性依赖工具。

Method: 分析ChatGPT发布前后LLM辅助的学生作文，采用专家定性评分和定量指标（可读性测试、皮尔逊相关系数、MTLD等）进行综合评估。

Result: 高级LLM能提升低水平学习者的评估分数和词汇多样性，可能掩盖其真实能力；LLM辅助程度与人类专家评分呈负相关，表明表面流畅但缺乏深层连贯性。

Conclusion: 要将AI辅助实践转化为真正的学习，教学法需从关注输出质量转向验证学习过程。教育者应将AI功能（特别是区分概念脚手架与文本生成）与学习者的最近发展区对齐。

Abstract: The rapid evolution of Large Language Models (LLMs) has made them powerful tools for enhancing student writing. This study explores the extent and limitations of LLMs in assisting secondary-level English as a Foreign Language (EFL) students with their writing tasks. While existing studies focus on output quality, our research examines the developmental shift in LLMs and their impact on EFL students, assessing whether smarter models act as true scaffolds or mere compensatory crutches. To achieve this, we analyse student compositions assisted by LLMs before and after ChatGPT's release, using both expert qualitative scoring and quantitative metrics (readability tests, Pearson's correlation coefficient, MTLD, and others). Our results indicate that advanced LLMs boost assessment scores and lexical diversity for lower-proficiency learners, potentially masking their true ability. Crucially, increased LLM assistance correlated negatively with human expert ratings, suggesting surface fluency without deep coherence. To transform AI-assisted practice into genuine learning, pedagogy must shift from focusing on output quality to verifying the learning process. Educators should align AI functions, specifically differentiating ideational scaffolding from textual production, within the learner's Zone of Proximal Development.

</details>


### [92] [Designing More Engaging Serious Games to Support Students' Mental Health: A Pilot Study Based on A CBT-Informed Design Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15662)
*Ting-Chen Hsu,Zheyuan Zhang,Ziyi Chen,Yuwen Liu,Yanjia Liu*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本研究针对当前数字心理健康教育和严肃游戏存在的枯燥、依从性低、吸引力不足等问题，提出了以体验为中心的新型严肃游戏设计框架TPR-MMF，并基于该框架开发了横版卷轴严肃游戏原型"World + You - World"。通过小样本随机对照试验验证了该框架游戏相比传统显性教育严肃游戏在内在动机方面的优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对当前面向学生和青少年的数字心理健康教育和严肃游戏存在的三个主要问题：1) 内容枯燥乏味；2) 用户依从性低；3) 缺乏吸引力。这些问题限制了心理健康教育的效果和用户参与度。

Method: 提出了治疗性程序修辞与机制映射框架(TPR-MMF)，这是一个以体验为中心的严肃游戏设计框架。基于该框架开发了横版卷轴严肃游戏原型"World + You - World"。通过小样本随机对照试验(N=28)比较TPR-MMF框架游戏与传统显性教育严肃游戏的效果，使用内在动机量表(IMI)进行量化评估，并结合定性调查分析。

Result: 实验组(玩"World + You - World")在内在动机量表的四个方面显著优于对照组。定性调查结果显示，玩家能够感知游戏机制中的心理隐喻，并自发地与现实生活经验产生共鸣。

Conclusion: TPR-MMF框架为面向学生和青少年的游戏化心理健康教育提供了一种高度吸引人的新型开发范式，能够有效提升用户参与度和教育效果。

Abstract: Addressing the issues of dullness, low compliance, and lack of appeal in current digital mental health education and serious games for students and adolescents, this study proposes a novel, experience-centered framework for serious game design: the Therapeutic Procedural Rhetoric and Mechanism Mapping Framework (TPR-MMF). Based on this framework, a side-scrolling serious game prototype, "World + You - World," was developed. This study compared the effectiveness of TPR-MMF-based games with traditional explicit educational serious games through a small-sample randomized controlled trial (N=28). The results of the Intrinsic Motivation Inventory (IMI) showed that the experimental group (playing "World + You - World") significantly outperformed the control group in four aspects. Furthermore, qualitative survey results indicated that players could perceive the psychological metaphors within the game mechanics and spontaneously resonated with real-life experiences. This study provides a highly engaging new development paradigm for gamified mental health education for students and adolescents.

</details>


### [93] [ReVis: Towards Reusable Image-Based Visualizations with MLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15781)
*Xiaolin Wen,Changlin Li,Manusha Karunathilaka,Can Liu,Fangzhuo Jin,Yong Wang*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: ReVis：一种人机协作方法，通过领域特定语言和MLLM解析，实现基于图像的可视化灵活重用和定制


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线分享的可视化通常只是位图图像，难以重新设计或适应新数据。现有方法依赖结构化格式，支持的可视化类型有限，定制灵活性不足。

Method: 1. 提出通用领域特定语言（DSL）建模复杂可视化；2. 使用MLLM管道将图像可视化解析为DSL；3. 提供交互界面供用户上传、检查、更新数据和自定义视觉编码。

Result: 40个可视化图库展示了DSL的表达能力，定量研究评估了ReVis在这些示例上的复制质量。两个使用场景和16位可视化从业者的用户访谈证明了ReVis的有效性。

Conclusion: ReVis通过人机协作方法解决了图像可视化重用难题，提供灵活的重用和定制能力，为可视化实践者提供了实用工具。

Abstract: Many expressive visualizations are shared online only as bitmap images, making them difficult to redesign or adapt to new data. Reusing such image-based visualizations requires substantial expertise and is often time-consuming, even for experienced visualization practitioners. Existing work on reproducing visualizations often relies on structured SVG or specifications, supports limited visualization types, and offers limited flexibility for customization. To address these challenges, we present ReVis, a human-AI collaboration approach that enables flexible reuse of image-based visualizations. First, a generic Domain-Specific language (DSL) is proposed to model complex visualizations and support both visualization decomposition and reproduction. Then, ReVis employs an MLLM-based pipeline to parse an image-based visualization into the DSL, delineating its core visual structures and data-to-encoding mappings, and further reproduces the visualization from the DSL. Finally, ReVis includes an interactive interface to allow users to upload visualization images, inspect reproduced results, update the underlying data, and customize visual encodings. A gallery of 40 visualizations demonstrates the expressiveness of the DSL, and a quantitative study evaluates the reproduction quality of ReVis on these examples. Two usage scenarios and user interviews with 16 visualization practitioners demonstrate the effectiveness of ReVis.

</details>


### [94] [Filter Babel: The Challenge of Synthetic Media to Authenticity and Common Ground in AI-Mediated Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15786)
*Advait Sarkar*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 论文探讨了"过滤巴别塔"思想实验：AI为每个人生成个性化内容可能导致私人语言产生，挑战共同理解和沟通，但也可能促进身份认同发展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨AI个性化过滤可能导致的"过滤巴别塔"现象，分析其对人类沟通和身份认同的潜在影响，为AI中介沟通研究提供方向。

Method: 采用思想实验方法，结合维特根斯坦私人语言哲学和Lanier的身份理论，分析AI中介沟通的挑战与机遇。

Result: 识别出AI个性化过滤可能导致私人语言产生，挑战共同基础；同时私人体验对身份认同至关重要，需要平衡个性化与共享现实。

Conclusion: "过滤巴别塔"现象对沟通和身份认同构成挑战，需要研究AI中介沟通的建构性方向，平衡个性化体验与共享现实。

Abstract: Filter Babel is a thought experiment about a near future in which everything we read, watch, and even whom we "meet" is privately generated for each of us. If we each recede into a world of purely private experience, we may each develop a Wittgensteinian private language that remains intelligible to others only because an AI translator sits in the middle. This intermediation challenges the integrity of common ground and therefore of communication. On the other hand, private experience is an essential engine of identity and selfhood: as Lanier warns, one must be somebody before one can share oneself. This paper opens a discussion of the challenges and opportunities that Filter Babel might present to human communication and identity, and what constructive directions for research in AI-mediated communication might ensue.

</details>


### [95] [From Intention to Text: AI-Supported Goal Setting in Academic Writing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15800)
*Yueling Fan,Richard Lee Davis,Olga Viberg*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: WriteFlow是一款基于语音的AI写作助手，通过目标导向的对话支持反思性学术写作，帮助作者进行目标表达、监控和协商，增强元认知调节和写作自主性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学术写作需要迭代反思和动态目标调节，但作者常常难以表达和管理变化的目标。现有AI写作工具强调效率，但对元认知和作者自主性的支持有限。先前研究和17名参与者的形成性研究证实了这一需求缺口。

Method: WriteFlow将AI交互构建为基于作者意图的持续目标表达、监控和协商的对话空间。通过12名专家用户的Wizard-of-Oz研究评估系统效果，分析其如何支持元认知调节和行动中反思。

Result: 研究发现WriteFlow能够通过支持迭代目标细化、在起草过程中保持目标与文本对齐、以及提示目标实现评估，有效支持元认知调节和行动中反思。专家用户验证了该系统的有效性。

Conclusion: 研究为AI写作系统设计提供了重要启示：应优先考虑反思性对话、灵活的目标结构和多视角反馈，以支持有意图和自主性的写作过程，而不仅仅是追求效率。

Abstract: This study presents WriteFlow, an AI voice-based writing assistant designed to support reflective academic writing through goal-oriented interaction. Academic writing involves iterative reflection and evolving goal regulation, yet prior research and a formative study with 17 participants show that writers often struggle to articulate and manage changing goals. While commonly used AI writing tools emphasize efficiency, they offer limited support for metacognition and writer agency. WriteFlow frames AI interaction as a dialogic space for ongoing goal articulation, monitoring, and negotiation grounded in writers' intentions. Findings from a Wizard-of-Oz study with 12 expert users show that WriteFlow scaffolds metacognitive regulation and reflection-in-action by supporting iterative goal refinement, maintaining goal-text alignment during drafting, and prompting evaluation of goal fulfillment. We discuss design implications for AI writing systems that prioritize reflective dialogue, flexible goal structures, and multi-perspective feedback to support intentional and agentic writing.

</details>


### [96] [Shaping Plant-Like Shape-Changing Interfaces as Vertical Charts: Maximizing Readability, Aesthetics, and Naturalness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15902)
*Elodie Bouzekri,Guillaume Riviere*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 该论文探索了植物形态物理图表的设计与实现，通过研究-设计方法比较了四种原型，验证了物理图表在传达环境数据方面的可行性和优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 环境数据可视化在促进环保生活方式方面日益重要，但现有研究主要关注自然启发的计数器、仪表和位图，数据序列的可视化仍待探索。论文旨在设计有效的植物形态图表来填补这一空白。

Method: 采用研究-设计方法，探索设计空间以最大化可读性和美学效果。通过比较四种原型（基于模态和材料维度），在可再生能源预测场景中向用户提问进行评估。

Result: 物理植物形态图表具有良好性能，当材料能感知低技术特性时表现出最佳自然性。折叠形状编码0到最大值的速率时，可在公共场所无需解释即可安装。物理图表在清晰度和可读性上略低于图形图表，但美学装饰性更高。

Conclusion: 物理植物形态图表值得开发，因其具有良好性能和最佳自然性，特别是当材料能传达低技术感知时。折叠形状编码速率的设计使其适合公共场所的无解释安装，为环境数据可视化提供了新途径。

Abstract: Conveying environmental data has grown interest in encouraging the adoption of eco-friendly lifestyles through data-driven strategies. This scope appeals to data visualizations representing the environmental purpose. For example, previous work has already proposed nature-inspired counters, gauges, and bitmaps, but data series remains to be explored. Therefore, could we design and implement effective plant-like charts? This paper brings answers through a research-through-design approach that explores a design space to maximize readability and aesthetics. It then compares four prototypes of charts over modality and material dimensions by asking users about scenarios involving renewable energy forecasts. The results examine whether implementing physical charts is worth it instead of graphical charts and the advantages of using meaningful materials that evocate sustainability and enhance naturalness. The results also reexamine, with physical charts, the previous results on graphical infographics of slightly lower clarity and readability but higher aesthetics of embellishment. In addition, learnability is examined for encoding rates through folded shapes. This paper shows that physical plant-like charts are worthwhile because of promising performance and best-of-breed naturalness when materials allow low-tech aspects' perception and because being installable in public places without explanations if folded shapes encode rates ranging from 0 to a maximum value.

</details>


### [97] [Stochastic wage suppression on gig platforms and how to organize against it](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15962)
*Ana-Andreea Stoica,Celestine Mendler-Duenner,Moritz Hardt*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 该论文研究了数字劳动平台如何利用劳动力成本不确定性来压低工资，并探讨了集体行动作为提高工资和促进福利的杠杆作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数字劳动平台越来越多地用于获取人力输入，从数据标注和AI模型红队测试到网约车和外卖配送。这些市场中的一个核心问题是平台利用低成本劳动力的丰富性来压低工资的能力。本文旨在研究这种剥削模式，并探讨集体行动如何作为提高工资和促进福利的杠杆。

Method: 引入了一种新颖的发布价格采购模型，该模型具有覆盖目标。平台通过向顺序到达的工人发布价格来完成M个任务，每个工人如果价格超过其私人成本则接受任务。首先，在工人成本估计的自然假设下，分析平台的定价策略。然后，研究集体行动的影响，特别是针对性的低成本工人联盟如何通过设定价格底线来影响平台支出。

Result: 1. 在工人成本估计的自然假设下，存在一个简单的定价策略，使平台能够以O(M)的等待时间覆盖所有M个任务，同时仅支付劳动力总成本的O(log(M)/M)部分。这表明平台可以利用工人对劳动力成本的不确定性来有效压低工资。
2. 研究显示，一个有针对性、承诺价格底线的小型低成本工人联盟，能够迫使平台的总支出从M的对数增长变为线性增长。相比之下，同等规模的随机抽样联盟基本无效。
3. 通过合成实验验证了理论结果，展示了集体行动在不同市场机制下的益处。

Conclusion: 数字劳动平台可以利用劳动力成本的不确定性来压低工资，但集体行动，特别是针对性的低成本工人联盟，可以成为提高工资和促进福利的有效杠杆。这种联盟通过设定价格底线，能够显著增加平台支出，从而改善工人待遇。研究结果为理解数字劳动市场的剥削模式和设计更公平的市场机制提供了理论依据。

Abstract: Digital labor platforms are increasingly used to procure human input, ranging from annotating data and red-teaming AI models, to ride-sharing and food delivery. A central concern in such markets is the ability of platforms to suppress wages by exploiting the abundance of low-cost labor. To study this exploitation pattern, we introduce a novel posted-price procurement model with coverage objectives. A platform seeks to complete M tasks by posting prices to sequentially arriving workers, each of whom accepts a task if it exceeds their private cost. First, we show that under natural assumptions on the workers' estimated cost, there exists a simple pricing strategy for the platform to cover all M tasks with wait time O(M), while paying only a O(log(M)/M) fraction of the total cost of labor. This result highlights how platforms can exploit workers' uncertainty about the cost of labor to effectively suppress wages. Then, we study collective action as a lever to increase wages and promote welfare in digital labor markets. In particular, we show how a small coalition of targeted low-cost workers who commit to a price floor forces the platform's total spending from logarithmic to linear in M. In contrast, a randomly sampled coalition of equal size remains largely ineffective. We complement our theory with synthetic experiments, showcasing the benefits of collective action across different market regimes.

</details>


<div id='cs.LO'></div>

# cs.LO [[Back]](#toc)

### [98] [Solving Fuzzy Satisfiability via Mixed-Integer Non-Linear Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15992)
*Pablo F. Castro*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: SATFuL是一个基于MINLP求解器的模糊逻辑SAT求解器，能够处理多种模糊命题逻辑变体，性能与现有求解器相当或更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模糊逻辑的SAT问题虽然具有有趣的应用价值，但相比布尔逻辑受到的关注较少，现有模糊逻辑SAT求解器通常只针对特定版本的模糊逻辑，缺乏通用性。

Method: 使用混合整数非线性规划（MINLP）求解器来检查模糊公式的可满足性，这种方法可以处理所有主要的模糊命题逻辑变体。

Result: 实验表明，SATFuL在Lukasiewicz逻辑上的性能与最先进的模糊求解器相当，在Product逻辑上优于现有求解器。

Conclusion: 该方法具有正确性和完备性，且易于扩展以支持新的模糊运算符，为模糊逻辑SAT求解提供了通用且有效的解决方案。

Abstract: This paper introduces SATFuL, a SAT solver for fuzzy logics. In contrast to the Boolean case, for which numerous SAT solvers exist, the SAT problem for fuzzy logics has attracted less attention, even though these tools have interesting applications. Unlike existing SAT solvers for fuzzy logics, SATFuL uses MINLP (Mixed Integer Non-Linear Programming) solvers to check the satisfiability of fuzzy formulas. This approach offers certain benefits; for instance, our tool can handle all major variations of fuzzy propositional logic, whereas other fuzzy solvers are usually tailored to specific versions of fuzzy logic. We conduct some experiments and demonstrate that the performance of our tool is comparable with state-of-the-art fuzzy solvers for Lukasiewicz logic, and outperforms available solvers for Product logic. The approach is sound and complete and can be easily extended to accommodate new fuzzy operators.

</details>


### [99] [The QBF Gallery 2023](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16153)
*Simone Heisinger,Luca Pulina,Martina Seidl*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: QBF Gallery 2023报告记录了最新的QBF求解器和基准测试集，进行了性能评估分析


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 继续QBF评估活动的传统，记录和调查量化布尔公式求解的最新技术进展，为QBF社区提供研究和基准测试支持

Method: 收集社区提交的新求解器和公式作为基准，创建统一的基准测试集（结合经过良好评估的公式和提交的实例），进行公开可用的求解器性能比较分析

Result: 建立了公开可用的基准测试集，对提交的求解器和公开可用的求解器进行了比较分析，评估了它们的性能和当前能力

Conclusion: 报告了QBF Gallery的现状，讨论了未来版本的想法和方向，以进一步支持QBF社区的研究和基准测试工作

Abstract: The QBF Gallery 2023, the last QBF evaluation event, continues the tradition to survey and document the state of the art in solving quantified Boolean formulas (QBFs). It provides a detailed overview by collecting newly developed solvers and formulas as benchmarks.
  This report documents the solvers and formulas submitted by the community and introduces a new, consolidated benchmark set that combines well-evaluated formulas with the submitted instances. The resulting formula set is made publicly available. With this benchmark set, we conduct a comparative analysis of the submitted solvers and publicly available solvers, assessing their performance and current capabilities. In addition, we report on the present status of the QBF Gallery and discuss ideas and directions for future editions to further support research and benchmarking within the QBF community.

</details>


<div id='cs.CR'></div>

# cs.CR [[Back]](#toc)

### [100] [SoK: Security of Autonomous LLM Agents in Agentic Commerce](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15367)
*Qian'ang Mao,Jiaxin Wang,Ya Liu,Li Zhu,Cong Ma,Jiaqi Yan*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该论文系统化分析了自主LLM智能体在商业金融领域的安全威胁，提出了五维威胁框架和分层防御架构，强调安全需要跨层协调控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着自主LLM智能体（如OpenClaw）推动智能体商业从人工监督辅助转向机器执行者，相关协议（ERC-8004、AP2、x402、ACP、ERC-8183、MPP等）创造了现有安全框架无法很好捕捉的攻击面，需要建立统一的安全框架。

Method: 采用知识系统化（SoK）方法，从学术论文、协议文档、行业报告和事件证据等公开语料库中系统整理，构建五维威胁框架（智能体完整性、交易授权、智能体间信任、市场操纵、监管合规），识别12个跨层攻击向量，提出分层防御架构。

Result: 揭示了安全威胁如何从推理层和工具层传播到托管、结算、市场损害和合规暴露，展示了当前智能体支付协议存在的授权缺口，证明智能体商业安全本质上是需要跨层协调控制的复杂问题。

Conclusion: 保护智能体商业需要跨层协调控制，涵盖LLM安全、协议设计、身份验证、市场结构和监管等方面，提出了研究路线图和基准测试议程来推动安全自主商业的发展。

Abstract: Autonomous large language model (LLM) agents such as OpenClaw are pushing agentic commerce from human-supervised assistance toward machine actors that can negotiate, purchase services, manage digital assets, and execute transactions across on-chain and off-chain environments. Protocols such as the Trustless Agents standard (ERC-8004), Agent Payments Protocol (AP2), the HTTP 402-based payment protocol (x402), Agent Commerce Protocol (ACP), the Agentic Commerce standard (ERC-8183), and Machine Payments Protocol (MPP) enable this transition, but they also create an attack surface that existing security frameworks do not capture well. This Systematization of Knowledge (SoK) develops a unified security framework for autonomous LLM agents in commerce and finance. We organize threats along five dimensions: agent integrity, transaction authorization, inter-agent trust, market manipulation, and regulatory compliance. From a systematically curated public corpus of academic papers, protocol documents, industry reports, and incident evidence, we derive 12 cross-layer attack vectors and show how failures propagate from reasoning and tooling layers into custody, settlement, market harm, and compliance exposure. We then propose a layered defense architecture addressing authorization gaps left by current agent-payment protocols. Overall, our analysis shows that securing agentic commerce is inherently a cross-layer problem that requires coordinated controls across LLM safety, protocol design, identity, market structure, and regulation. We conclude with a research roadmap and a benchmark agenda for secure autonomous commerce.

</details>


### [101] [An Agentic Workflow for Detecting Personally Identifiable Information in Crash Narratives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15369)
*Junyi Ma,Pei Li,Rui Gan,Kai Cheng,Steven T. Parker,Bin Ran*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 开发了一个本地可部署的智能工作流，利用大语言模型检测交通事故报告中的个人身份信息，通过混合提取器和验证器实现高精度PII检测，保护隐私的同时支持大规模安全分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交通事故报告中的事故叙述包含重要的安全分析信息，但由于存在个人身份信息（PII）如姓名、家庭住址和车牌号等，限制了其广泛应用。现有方法无法有效处理稀疏且上下文相关的PII检测问题。

Method: 开发了一个本地可部署的智能工作流，包含混合提取器和验证器。混合提取器将结构化PII（如电话号码、电子邮件）路由到基于规则的模型（Presidio），将上下文相关PII（如姓名、家庭住址、字母数字标识符）路由到领域适应、微调的大语言模型。为处理具有挑战性的类别模糊性，工作流集成了集成LLM提取和智能验证步骤，通过基于证据的推理过滤误检。

Result: 在真实世界事故数据集上评估，智能工作流实现了精确率0.82、召回率0.94、F1分数0.87、准确率0.96的强性能，优于多个基线方法。消融实验表明集成LLM提取和验证器对家庭住址和字母数字标识符的检测有改进效果。

Conclusion: 该工作流在本地运行，支持隐私敏感的操作环境，为可扩展、隐私保护的交通事故数据处理提供了实用且稳健的路径，在保护个人隐私的同时支持更广泛的研究和安全干预。

Abstract: Crash narratives in crash reports provide crucial contextual information for traffic safety analysis. Yet, their broader use is hindered by the presence of personally identifiable information (PII), including names, home addresses, and license plate numbers. Because PII appears sparsely and inconsistently in crash narratives, manual detection is not scalable, and existing rule-based approaches often fail to capture context-dependent PII. This study develops and evaluates a locally deployable, agentic workflow for PII detection in crash narratives by leveraging large language models (LLMs). The workflow contains a Hybrid Extractor and a Verifier. The Hybrid Extractor routes structured PII (e.g., phone numbers and email addresses) to a rule-based model (i.e., Presidio) and context-dependent PII (e.g., names, home addresses, and alphanumeric identifiers) to a domain-adapted, fine-tuned LLM. To address ambiguity in challenging categories, the workflow incorporates ensemble LLM extraction and an agentic verification step that filters false detections through evidence-based reasoning. Evaluated on a real-world crash dataset, the agentic workflow achieves strong performance with a precision of 0.82, a recall of 0.94, an F1 of 0.87, and an accuracy of 0.96, outperforming multiple baseline methods. Moreover, the ablation results suggest that ensemble LLM extraction and Verifier offer improved detection for home addresses and alphanumeric identifiers. The workflow runs locally, supporting privacy-sensitive operational settings where external APIs are restricted. This work offers a practical and robust path for scalable, privacy-preserving crash data processing, enabling broader research and safety interventions while safeguarding individual privacy.

</details>


### [102] [TopFeaRe: Locating Critical State of Adversarial Resilience for Graphs Regarding Topology-Feature Entanglement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15370)
*Xinxin Fan,Wenxiong Chen,Quanliang Jing,Chi Lin,Shaoye Luo,Wenbo Song,Yunfeng Lu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出一种基于复杂动力系统平衡点理论的图对抗防御方法，通过定位图的对抗弹性临界状态来防御拓扑和特征攻击


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有防御方法未能揭示图对抗攻击中拓扑和特征两个维度为何必要以及如何充分融合来共同学习图表示的内在原因

Method: 1) 将图体系映射到复杂动力系统，用系统振荡建模对抗扰动行为；2) 设计二维拓扑-特征纠缠函数表示扰动图的动态变化；3) 利用平衡点理论定位图的对抗弹性临界状态

Result: 在五个常用真实数据集上的多面实验验证了方法的有效性，结果显示该方法在四种代表性图对抗攻击下显著优于最先进的基线方法

Conclusion: 通过复杂动力系统理论为图对抗防御提供了新的视角，能够有效定位图的对抗弹性临界状态，实现优于现有方法的防御性能

Abstract: Graph adversarial attacks are usually produced from the two perspectives of topology/structure and node feature, both of them represent the paramount characteristics learned by today's deep learning models. Although some defense countermeasures are proposed at present, they fails to disclose the intrinsic reasons why these two aspects necessitate and how they are adequately fused to co-learn the graph representation. Towards this question, we in this paper propose an adversarial defense approach through locating the graph's critical state of adversarial resilience, resorting to the equilibrium-point theory in the discipline of complex dynamic system (CDS). In brief, our work has three novelties: i) Adversarial-Attack Modeling, i.e. map a graph regime into CDS, and use the oscillation of dynamic system to model the behavior of adversarial perturbation; ii) 2D Topology-Feature-Entangled Function Design for Perturbed Graph, i.e. project graph topology and node feature as two characteristic spaces, and define two-dimensional entangled perturbation functions to represent the dynamic variance under adversarial attacks; and iii) Location of Critical State of Adversarial Resilience, i.e. utilize the equilibrium-point theory to locate the graph's critical state of attack resilience resorting to the perturbation-reflected 2D function. Finally, multi-facet experiments on five commonly-used realistic datasets validate the effectiveness of our proposed approach, and the results show our approach can significantly outperform the state-of-the-art baselines under four representative graph adversarial attacks.

</details>


### [103] [The Synthetic Media Shift: Tracking the Rise, Virality, and Detectability of AI-Generated Multimodal Misinformation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15372)
*Zacharias Chrysidis,Stefanos-Iordanis Papadopoulos,Symeon Papadopoulos*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: CONVEX数据集包含超过15万条多模态虚假信息帖子，分析显示AI生成内容获得不成比例的高传播度，但主要由被动参与驱动而非主动讨论，且检测模型性能随时间下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着生成式AI发展，真实与合成媒体的界限日益模糊，威胁在线信息完整性。需要大规模数据集来研究多模态虚假信息的传播动态和检测挑战。

Method: 构建CONVEX数据集，包含超过150K多模态帖子（错误标注、编辑和AI生成内容），来自X的社区笔记平台。分析传播性、参与度和共识动态，评估专业检测器和视觉语言模型性能。

Result: AI生成内容获得不成比例的高传播度，但主要由被动参与驱动；虽然初始报告较慢，但一旦被标记后能更快达成社区共识；检测模型性能随时间持续下降，无法跟上生成模型进化速度。

Conclusion: 研究强调在快速演变的数字信息环境中需要持续监控和自适应策略，检测技术必须不断更新以应对不断进化的生成模型挑战。

Abstract: As generative AI advances, the distinction between authentic and synthetic media is increasingly blurred, challenging the integrity of online information. In this study, we present CONVEX, a large-scale dataset of multimodal misinformation involving miscaptioned, edited, and AI-generated visual content, comprising over 150K multimodal posts with associated notes and engagement metrics from X's Community Notes. We analyze how multimodal misinformation evolves in terms of virality, engagement, and consensus dynamics, with a focus on synthetic media. Our results show that while AI-generated content achieves disproportionate virality, its spread is driven primarily by passive engagement rather than active discourse. Despite slower initial reporting, AI-generated content reaches community consensus more quickly once flagged. Moreover, our evaluation of specialized detectors and vision-language models reveals a consistent decline in performance over time in distinguishing synthetic from authentic images as generative models evolve. These findings highlight the need for continuous monitoring and adaptive strategies in the rapidly evolving digital information environment.

</details>


### [104] [LinuxArena: A Control Setting for AI Agents in Live Production Software Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15384)
*Tyler Tracy,Ram Potham,Nick Kuhn,Myles Heller,Anshul Khandelwal,Cody Rushing,Henri Lemoine,Miguel Brandao,Tomas Turlik,Adam Hanson,Josh Hills,Amy Ngo,Ram Rachum,Nik Mitchell,Falko Galperin,Oscar Sykes,Pip Arnott,Samuel Prieto Lima,Carlos Giudice,Matt Goldwater,Daniel Popp,Drew de Wet,Ruben Castaing,Qi Guo,Douw Marx,Benjamin Shaffrey,Justin Shenk,Martin Milbradt,Hannah Meagher,Shaheen Ahmed-Chowdhury,Daniel O'Connell,Chris Canal,Buck Shlegeris,Aryan Bhatt*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: LinuxArena是一个包含20个环境、1671个主要任务和184个侧任务的软件工程控制研究平台，用于评估攻击者在执行正常任务时进行数据窃取和后门植入等安全破坏行为的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前缺乏能够真实模拟生产环境中软件工程任务的多样化控制研究平台，需要构建一个能够同时评估攻击者破坏能力和监控系统检测能力的测试环境。

Method: 创建LinuxArena平台，包含多服务生产环境，设计两类任务：主要任务（合法软件工程工作）和侧任务（安全破坏行为）。通过破坏评估和监控评估两种方式验证平台有效性，并收集人类攻击轨迹数据集LaStraj。

Result: Claude Opus 4.6在1%步进误报率下达到约23%的未检测破坏成功率。人类攻击轨迹LaStraj的规避监控成功率显著高于模型生成的攻击，表明当前攻击策略尚未饱和LinuxArena。

Conclusion: LinuxArena为攻击者和防御者提供了有意义的改进空间，是开发和评估未来控制协议的强大测试平台，有助于推动软件工程安全控制研究的发展。

Abstract: We introduce LinuxArena, a control setting in which agents operate directly on live, multi-service production environments. LinuxArena contains 20 environments, 1,671 main tasks representing legitimate software engineering work, and 184 side tasks representing safety failures such as data exfiltration and backdooring, making it the largest and most diverse control setting for software engineering to date. We validate LinuxArena is useful for control research by running sabotage evaluations, which measure whether attackers can complete side tasks while working on main tasks, and monitor evaluations, which measure a monitor model's ability to detect sabotage attempts. Against a GPT-5-nano trusted monitor at a 1\% step-wise false positive rate, Claude Opus 4.6 achieves roughly a 23% undetected sabotage success rate. We additionally release LaStraj, a dataset of human-crafted attack trajectories that evade monitors at substantially higher rates than any model-generated attacks we elicited, showing that current attack policies do not saturate LinuxArena. These results suggest that LinuxArena has meaningful headroom for both attackers and defenders, making it a strong testbed for developing and evaluating future control protocols.

</details>


### [105] [Graded Symbolic Verification with a Fuzzy Dolev-Yao Attacker Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15402)
*Murat Moran*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种用于协议验证的渐进式侧信道攻击模型，使用分级攻击者视图和T-范数泄漏更新，替代了传统的二元知识模型，能检测累积侧信道泄漏导致的协议漏洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统Dolev-Yao模型使用二元攻击者知识（已知/未知）抽象，无法处理累积侧信道攻击场景，其中重复的噪声观测会逐步提高攻击者知识。需要建模渐进式知识积累过程。

Method: 提出分级攻击者视图μ_K∈[0,1]，使用乘积T-范数进行泄漏更新，在Modified Murphi中实现有限网格显式状态执行。方法通过精确概念格属性约简优化，暴露阈值驱动的安全到失败转换。

Result: 在对称和非对称协议（包括Needham-Schroeder-Lowe）上的执行结果表明，在清晰语义下通过基线模型的协议，一旦启用累积侧信道泄漏就可能失败。

Conclusion: 累积侧信道泄漏显著影响协议安全性，传统二元模型无法捕捉这种渐进式知识积累。提出的分级模型能更准确地评估协议在现实侧信道攻击下的安全性。

Abstract: Classical symbolic protocol verification under Dolev--Yao uses binary attacker knowledge (known/unknown). This abstraction misses cumulative side-channel settings, where repeated noisy observations progressively improve attacker knowledge. We model this process with a graded attacker view \(μ_K\in[0,1]\), product T-norm leak updates, and finite-grid explicit-state execution in Modified Murphi.
  The method is optimised with exact concept-lattice attribute reducts and exposes threshold-driven safe-to-fail transitions that are not represented in corresponding binary runs under the same bounded assumptions. Executed results on symmetric and asymmetric protocols, including Needham--Schroeder--Lowe (NSL), show that baseline models passing under crisp semantics can fail once cumulative side-channel leakage is enabled.

</details>


### [106] [SecureRouter: Encrypted Routing for Efficient Secure Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15499)
*Yukuan Zhang,Mengxin Zheng,Qian Lou*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: SecureRouter：一个端到端加密路由和推理框架，通过加密下的输入自适应模型选择来加速安全Transformer推理，相比先前工作实现1.95倍延迟降低


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于安全多方计算（MPC）的隐私保护推理系统使用单一固定模型处理所有加密输入，忽略了不同输入需要不同模型规模来平衡效率与准确性，导致推理速度慢、成本高，限制了实际部署

Method: 建立统一的加密流水线，集成安全路由器和MPC优化模型池，包含训练阶段和推理阶段组件：1）MPC成本感知的安全路由器，从加密特征预测每个模型的效用和成本；2）MPC优化模型池，其架构和量化方案协同训练以最小化MPC通信和计算开销

Result: 相比先前工作，SecureRouter实现了1.95倍的延迟降低，同时准确率损失可忽略不计，为可扩展和高效的安全AI推理提供了实用路径

Conclusion: SecureRouter通过输入自适应模型选择解决了安全Transformer推理中的主要瓶颈，在保持完全数据和模型机密性的同时显著提升了效率，推动了隐私保护AI推理的实际应用

Abstract: Cryptographically secure neural network inference typically relies on secure computing techniques such as Secure Multi-Party Computation (MPC), enabling cloud servers to process client inputs without decrypting them. Although prior privacy-preserving inference systems co-design network optimizations with MPC, they remain slow and costly, limiting real-world deployment. A major bottleneck is their use of a single, fixed transformer model for all encrypted inputs, ignoring that different inputs require different model sizes to balance efficiency and accuracy. We present SecureRouter, an end-to-end encrypted routing and inference framework that accelerates secure transformer inference through input-adaptive model selection under encryption. SecureRouter establishes a unified encrypted pipeline that integrates a secure router with an MPC-optimized model pool, enabling coordinated routing, inference, and protocol execution while preserving full data and model confidentiality. The framework includes training-phase and inference-phase components: an MPC-cost-aware secure router that predicts per-model utility and cost from encrypted features, and an MPC-optimized model pool whose architectures and quantization schemes are co-trained to minimize MPC communication and computation overhead. Compared to prior work, SecureRouter achieves a latency reduction by 1.95x with negligible accuracy loss, offering a practical path toward scalable and efficient secure AI inference. Our open-source implementation is available at: https://github.com/UCF-ML-Research/SecureRouter

</details>


### [107] [CSLE: A Reinforcement Learning Platform for Autonomous Security Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15590)
*Kim Hammar*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: CSLE是一个用于自主安全管理的强化学习平台，通过结合仿真和仿真系统在现实条件下进行实验，缩小理论与实际性能差距


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前强化学习在安全管理中的应用大多局限于仿真环境，不清楚如何推广到实际操作系统，需要解决这一局限性

Method: CSLE平台包含两个系统：1)仿真系统在虚拟化环境中复制目标系统关键组件，用于收集数据和识别系统模型；2)仿真系统通过模拟系统模型高效学习安全策略，然后在仿真系统中评估和优化策略

Result: 通过四个用例（流控制、复制控制、分段控制和恢复控制）展示了CSLE能够在近似操作系统的环境中实现接近最优的安全管理

Conclusion: CSLE平台通过结合仿真和仿真系统，能够在现实条件下进行自主安全管理实验，缩小理论与实际性能差距，实现接近最优的安全管理

Abstract: Reinforcement learning is a promising approach to autonomous and adaptive security management in networked systems. However, current reinforcement learning solutions for security management are mostly limited to simulation environments and it is unclear how they generalize to operational systems. In this paper, we address this limitation by presenting CSLE: a reinforcement learning platform for autonomous security management that enables experimentation under realistic conditions. Conceptually, CSLE encompasses two systems. First, it includes an emulation system that replicates key components of the target system in a virtualized environment. We use this system to gather measurements and logs, based on which we identify a system model, such as a Markov decision process. Second, it includes a simulation system where security strategies are efficiently learned through simulations of the system model. The learned strategies are then evaluated and refined in the emulation system to close the gap between theoretical and operational performance. We demonstrate CSLE through four use cases: flow control, replication control, segmentation control, and recovery control. Through these use cases, we show that CSLE enables near-optimal security management in an environment that approximates an operational system.

</details>


### [108] [Privacy, Prediction, and Allocation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15596)
*Ben Jacobsen,Nitin Kohli*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该论文研究了在满足差分隐私约束下，资源分配系统中个体层面与单元层面两种目标策略的权衡关系，为隐私保护、分配效率和目标精度之间的平衡提供了理论分析框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前算法预测被广泛用于稀缺资源分配，但存在两个矛盾现象：一方面简单单元层面分配策略可能优于个体层面目标策略；另一方面隐私考虑也反对个体层面目标策略。这导致单元层面目标策略同时因效用和隐私原因被推荐。作者希望系统理解隐私保护与目标策略层级之间的相互作用。

Method: 研究满足差分隐私的援助分配系统，将现有的隐私优化方法与经济学中的援助分配模型相结合。在随机和无分布设置下，分析个体层面和单元层面分配策略的隐私变体，考虑数据可用性的各种约束条件。

Result: 通过分析提供了清晰、可解释的界限，描述了隐私保护、分配效率和目标精度之间的权衡关系。这些界限有助于理解在不同数据可用性约束下，个体层面与单元层面策略在隐私保护环境中的相对性能。

Conclusion: 该研究首次系统探讨了差分隐私约束下资源分配系统的目标策略层级问题，为理解隐私保护、分配效率和目标精度之间的复杂权衡提供了理论框架，有助于指导实际资源分配系统的设计。

Abstract: Algorithmic predictions are increasingly used to inform the allocation of scarce resources. The promise of these methods is that, through machine learning, they can better identify the people who would benefit most from interventions. Recently, however, several works have called this assumption into question by demonstrating the existence of settings where simple, unit-level allocation strategies can meet or even exceed the performance of those based on individual-level targeting. Separately, other works have objected to individual-level targeting on privacy grounds, leading to an unusual situation where a single solution, unit-level targeting, is recommended for reasons of both privacy and utility. Motivated by the desire to fully understand the interplay of privacy and targeting levels, we initiate the study of aid allocation systems that satisfy differential privacy, synthesizing existing works on private optimization with the economic models of aid allocation used in the non-private literature. To this end, we investigate private variants of both individual and unit-level allocation strategies in both stochastic and distribution-free settings under a range of constraints on data availability. Through this analysis, we provide clean, interpretable bounds characterizing the tradeoffs between privacy, efficiency, and targeting precision in allocation.

</details>


### [109] [Too Private to Tell: Practical Token Theft Attacks on Apple Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15637)
*Haoling Zhou,Shixuan Zhao,Chao Wang,Zhiqiang Lin*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 研究发现苹果智能服务（Apple Intelligence）的令牌颁发机制存在漏洞，攻击者可通过跨设备令牌重放攻击（Serpent攻击）窃取受害者设备访问令牌并在不同设备上使用，绕过使用限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 苹果声称其AI服务通过匿名访问令牌机制特别关注用户安全和隐私，但研究者希望验证这一机制的实际安全性，揭示可能的漏洞。

Method: 采用流量分析、逆向工程和与苹果公开文档交叉比对的方法，研究令牌颁发机制，并开发了Serpent攻击——首个针对苹果智能的实用跨设备令牌重放攻击。

Result: 在最新macOS 26 Tahoe上成功实施攻击，证明攻击者即使已耗尽自身配额，也能立即重新获得苹果智能服务访问权限。已向厂商负责任披露并获得苹果确认，分配了CVE并给予赏金。

Conclusion: 匿名化身份本身不能确保AI服务安全；强制执行不可转移性需要与合法用户进行加密绑定。这为内置AI服务提供了普遍教训。

Abstract: Apple Intelligence is a generative AI (GenAI) service provided by Apple on its devices. While offering a similar set of features as other similar GenAI services, Apple Intelligence is claimed to be designed with an extra focus on user security and privacy through a two-stage authentication and authorization design using anonymous access tokens. In this paper, we present our investigation into this token issuance mechanism with a goal to reveal possible vulnerabilities using traffic analysis, reverse engineering, and cross comparison with Apple's public documentation. Specifically, we present the Serpent attack, the first practical cross-device token replay attack against Apple Intelligence that allows the attacker to steal the access tokens from the victim's device and utilise them on a different device, with all usage rate-limited against the victim. We have achieved successful attacks on the latest macOS 26 Tahoe and demonstrated that an attacker, who even has used up its own allowance, can immediately regain access to Apple Intelligence service. We have responsibly disclosed the vulnerabilities to the vendors and received confirmation from Apple with CVE assigned and bounty given. Our results highlight a general lesson for built-in AI services: Anonymising identity does not by itself make the AI service secure; Enforcing non-transferability requires cryptographic binding to the rightful user.

</details>


### [110] [A Protocol-Agnostic Backscatter-Based Security Layer for Ultra-Low-Power SWIPT IoT Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15831)
*Taki Eddine Djidjekh,Alexandru Takacs,Gaël Loubet,Lamoussa Sanogo,Daniela Dragomirescu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出一种轻量级、协议无关的SWIPT安全增强方案，通过整流器驱动反向散射机制实现免电池传感节点的安全认证，无需激活传统RF收发器，降低功耗的同时确保设备识别安全。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物联网应用中SWIPT系统的安全需求，特别是电池受限或免电池节点的安全认证问题。现有方案通常依赖通信协议或需要硬件修改，缺乏轻量级、协议无关的安全增强方案。

Method: 基于反向散射的识别机制，采用整流器驱动的反向散射方案嵌入免电池传感节点中，实现无需激活传统RF收发器的认证。通过重放攻击模拟评估鲁棒性，并在真实WSN中使用LoRaWAN兼容的免电池节点进行实验验证。

Result: 实验证明该方法能实现安全识别、可靠能量收集和数据传输，对节点自主性影响可忽略。在标准LoRaWAN ABP加密上模拟重放攻击，揭示了现有漏洞并验证了所提方案的相关性。

Conclusion: 该方案为SWIPT使能的物联网系统提供了一个实用、节能且可扩展的安全框架，在不改变现有通信协议或不损害功率自主性的前提下增强了设备认证安全性。

Abstract: This paper presents a lightweight, protocol-agnostic security enhancement for Simultaneous Wireless Information and Power Transfer (SWIPT) in Internet of Things (IoT) applications. Building on a backscatter-based identification mechanism, the proposed approach introduces a secure, energy-efficient layer that operates independently of communication protocols and with minimal hardware modification. A rectifier-driven backscattering scheme embedded in battery-free sensing nodes enables authentication without activating conventional RF transceivers, thereby reducing power consumption while ensuring secure device identification. To assess robustness, replay attacks are emulated on standard LoRaWAN Activation By Personalization (ABP) encryption, highlighting vulnerabilities and demonstrating the relevance of the proposed solution. The approach is experimentally validated in a real Wireless Sensor Network (WSN) using LoRaWAN-compatible, battery-free sensing nodes equipped with compact, low-profile antennas, confirming both practicality and scalability for space-constrained IoT deployments. Results show that the method achieves secure identification, reliable energy harvesting, and data transmission with negligible impact on node autonomy. The proposed approach offers a practical, energy-efficient, and scalable security framework for SWIPT-enabled IoT systems, strengthening device authentication without altering existing communication protocols or compromising power autonomy.

</details>


### [111] [Half-Moon Cookie: Private, Similarity-Based Blocklisting with TOCTOU-Attack Resilience](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15641)
*Xinyuan Zhang,Anrin Chakraborti,Michael K. Reiter*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: Half-Moon Cookie是一个私有黑名单框架，允许客户端在不泄露查询或服务器黑名单的情况下检查项目是否接近黑名单中的元素，支持高效的验证机制以防止TOCTOU攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统黑名单技术存在隐私泄露问题：要么需要公开黑名单，要么客户端查询会暴露给服务器。需要一种既能保护客户端隐私又能保护服务器专有黑名单的解决方案。

Method: 提出Half-Moon Cookie框架，将嵌入步骤与黑名单检查分离，使性能取决于两者之和而非乘积。支持两种检查：完整的黑名单检查（较慢）和高效的先前检查结果验证（快速）。

Result: 该框架可实例化为基于相似性的恶意软件检测系统，能够有效识别恶意可执行文件，同时保护客户端输入和底层黑名单不被泄露。

Conclusion: Half-Moon Cookie提供了一种实用的私有黑名单检查方案，解决了传统方法的隐私限制，支持客户端执行初始检查而接收方高效验证，有效防止TOCTOU攻击。

Abstract: Blocklisting is a common technique for preventing the use of known malicious content. However, conventional blocklisting infrastructures require either the blocklist to be public or clients to reveal their queries to the blocklist server. In this work, we introduce a private blocklisting framework, Half-Moon Cookie, by which a client can check an item against a proprietary blocklist held by a server, to determine whether the item is close to any blocklist element in a metric space. Critically, our design separates the embedding step from the blocklist check, so that performance degrades with their sum and not their product. Still, this check might be too costly to perform on the critical path of using the item, and so our design also supports a very efficient check that an item previously passed the blocklist check. In doing so, we support applications where one client can perform the blocklist check on the item before sending it, and recipients can more efficiently confirm the previous result before using the item, thereby avoiding TOCTOU attacks. We demonstrate how Half-Moon Cookie can be instantiated for similarity-based malware detection, enabling effective identification of malicious executables without revealing client inputs or disclosing the underlying blocklist.

</details>


### [112] [DPDSyn: Improving Differentially Private Dataset Synthesis for Model Training by Downstream Task Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15660)
*Mingxuan Jia,Wen Huang,Weixin Zhao,Xingyi Wang,Jian Peng,Zhishuo Zhang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出DPDSyn方法，通过训练满足差分隐私的AI模型来合成数据集，相比现有方法在数据效用和合成效率上均有显著提升


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有差分隐私数据集合成方法通过选择原始数据集的多维低维分布来近似原始分布，但难以选择合适的低维分布，导致合成数据集的数据效用降低

Method: 在原始私有数据集上训练满足差分隐私的AI模型用于下游任务，然后利用该模型合成数据集。模型既满足差分隐私保护要求，又保留了完成下游任务的关键信息

Result: 在四个基准数据集上的实验表明，DPDSyn在准确率上最大提升2.40倍，合成效率最大提升333.73倍，优于八个最先进的基线方法，且在不同数据规模下具有良好的可扩展性

Conclusion: 通过训练差分隐私AI模型来合成数据集的方法能有效平衡隐私保护和数据效用，为差分隐私数据集合成提供了新的有效途径

Abstract: How to synthesize a dataset while achieving differential privacy for AI model training is a meaningful but challenging problem. To address this problem, state-of-the-art methods first select original private dataset's multiple low-dimensional distributions that have the potential to approximate the distribution of original private dataset with high precision, and then synthesize a dataset obeying all selected low-dimensional distributions as the synthetic dataset. However, it is difficult to select suitable low-dimensional distributions, which in turn degrades the data utility of resulting synthetic dataset. To improve differentially private dataset synthesis, we propose to train a differentially private AI model for downstream tasks on the original private dataset and utilize the trained model to synthesize datasets. In particular, on the one hand, the AI model satisfies differential privacy so no matter how to use the model does not disclose private information of original private dataset. On the other hand, the AI model is trained to complete the downstream task so the AI model preserves critical information for completing downstream tasks. We utilize the AI model to synthesize datasets to achieve the goal of improving data utility while preserving privacy. Empirical evaluations on four benchmark datasets demonstrate that our proposed DPDSyn consistently outperforms eight state-of-the-art baselines with a maximum improvement of 2.40x in accuracy and 333.73x in synthesis efficiency. Further experiments also validate that DPDSyn has strong scalability across varying data scales.

</details>


### [113] [DEMUX: Boundary-Aware Multi-Scale Traffic Demixing for Multi-Tab Website Fingerprinting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15677)
*Yali Yuan,Yaosheng Liu,Qianqi Niu,Guang Cheng*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: DEMUX是一个专门用于多标签网站指纹攻击的深度学习框架，通过边界保持聚合、多尺度CNN和两阶段Transformer解决多标签流量混合问题，显著提升现有方法的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度学习方法在单标签假设下表现良好，但当用户同时打开多个标签页时，流量交织导致性能显著下降。多标签场景将网站指纹攻击转化为隐式解混合问题，需要满足三个结构要求：段边界信号完整性、多尺度局部建模和分散片段的相对时间关联。

Method: DEMUX框架包含三个紧密耦合组件：1) 边界保持聚合模块，采用重叠窗口划分和联合包级/突发级特征提取；2) 多尺度并行CNN，通过并行分支捕获异构时间模式；3) 带有旋转位置嵌入的两阶段Transformer编码器，实现稳健的跨窗口片段关联。边界保持聚合模块还可作为即插即用预处理器提升现有基线方法。

Result: 在封闭世界、开放世界、防御增强、动态标签和跨配置设置下的广泛实验表明，DEMUX实现了最先进的性能。在具有挑战性的封闭世界5标签设置中，DEMUX的P@5达到0.943，MAP@5达到0.961，分别比最强基线高出9.2和6.2个百分点，证实了其在复杂多标签解混合场景中的强大鲁棒性。

Conclusion: DEMUX通过满足多标签解混合的三个结构要求，有效解决了现有方法在多标签场景下的性能退化问题。该框架不仅自身性能优越，其边界保持聚合模块还能作为通用预处理器提升现有方法，为网站指纹攻击在多标签环境中的实际应用提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Website fingerprinting (WF) attacks infer the websites visited by users from encrypted traffic in anonymous networks such as Tor. Existing deep learning methods achieve high accuracy under the single-tab assumption but degrade substantially when users open multiple tabs concurrently, producing interleaved traffic that transforms WF into an implicit demixing problem. We identify three structural requirements for effective multi-tab demixing, namely signal integrity at segment boundaries, multi-scale local modeling, and relative temporal association of dispersed fragments, and show that no prior method satisfies all three simultaneously. We propose DEMUX, a designed framework that addresses these requirements through three tightly coupled components. A Boundary Preserving Aggregation Module employs overlapping window partitioning with joint packet-level and burst-level feature extraction. A Multi-Scale Parallel CNN captures heterogeneous temporal patterns via parallel branches. A two-stage Transformer encoder with Rotary Positional Embedding enables robust cross-window fragment association. The Boundary Preserving Aggregation Module additionally serves as a plug-and-play preprocessor that consistently improves existing baselines without architectural modification. Extensive experiments across closed-world, open-world, defense-augmented, dynamic-tab, and cross-configuration settings demonstrate that DEMUX achieves state-of-the-art performance. In the challenging closed-world 5-tab setting, DEMUX attains a P@5 of 0.943 and MAP@5 of 0.961, outperforming the strongest baseline by 9.2 and 6.2 percentage points respectively, confirming its strong robustness in complex multi-tab demixing scenarios.

</details>


### [114] [Into the Gray Zone: Domain Contexts Can Blur LLM Safety Boundaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15717)
*Ki Sen Hung,Xi Yang,Chang Liu,Haoran Li,Kejiang Chen,Changxuan Fan,Tsun On Kwok,Weiming Zhang,Xiaomeng Li,Yangqiu Song*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: Jargon框架通过安全研究上下文和多轮对抗交互，利用上下文敏感对齐漏洞，在主流大语言模型中实现超过93%的攻击成功率，并提出基于策略引导的安全防护机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型对齐中存在帮助性与无害性之间的冲突，当同一知识既可用于合法目的也可用于恶意目的时尤为突出。研究发现上下文敏感对齐存在漏洞：领域特定上下文会选择性放松对领域相关有害知识的防御，而安全研究上下文则触发对所有伤害类别的广泛放松。

Method: 提出Jargon框架，结合安全研究上下文与多轮对抗交互来系统性地利用这一漏洞。通过激活空间分析揭示查询在良性输入和有害输入之间的中间区域（灰色地带）。为缓解此漏洞，设计了策略引导的安全防护机制，通过对齐微调将这种能力内化。

Result: Jargon在包括GPT-5.2、Claude-4.5和Gemini-3在内的七个前沿模型中实现了超过93%的攻击成功率，显著优于现有方法。激活空间分析显示Jargon查询占据良性输入和有害输入之间的中间区域，该区域中拒绝决策变得不可靠。

Conclusion: 上下文敏感对齐存在系统性漏洞，Jargon框架能够有效利用这些漏洞。提出的策略引导安全防护机制能够降低攻击成功率同时保持模型的帮助性，为大语言模型安全对齐提供了新的防御思路。

Abstract: A central goal of LLM alignment is to balance helpfulness with harmlessness, yet these objectives conflict when the same knowledge serves both legitimate and malicious purposes. This tension is amplified by context-sensitive alignment: we observe that domain-specific contexts (e.g., chemistry) selectively relax defenses for domain-relevant harmful knowledge, while safety-research contexts (e.g., jailbreak studies) trigger broader relaxation spanning all harm categories. To systematically exploit this vulnerability, we propose Jargon, a framework combining safety-research contexts with multi-turn adversarial interactions that achieves attack success rates exceeding 93% across seven frontier models, including GPT-5.2, Claude-4.5, and Gemini-3, substantially outperforming existing methods. Activation space analysis reveals that Jargon queries occupy an intermediate region between benign and harmful inputs, a gray zone where refusal decisions become unreliable. To mitigate this vulnerability, we design a policy-guided safeguard that steers models toward helpful yet harmless responses, and internalize this capability through alignment fine-tuning, reducing attack success rates while preserving helpfulness.

</details>


### [115] [Privacy-Preserving LLMs Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15728)
*Xidong Wu,Yukuan Zhang,Yuqiong Ji,Reza Shirkavand,Qian Lou,Shangqian Gao*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: PPRoute是一个保护隐私的LLM路由框架，通过MPC友好操作、多步训练算法和O(1)通信复杂度的Top-k算法，在保证路由质量的同时显著提升加密计算效率


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM路由在平衡模型性能与成本效益的同时，在用户与LLM之间增加了中间层，带来了新的隐私风险。现有隐私保护技术如安全多方计算(MPC)在协议设计和实现方面研究不足，朴素实现通常带来过高的计算开销

Method: 1. 使用MPC友好操作加速编码器推理；2. 采用多步模型训练算法在加密域约束下保持路由质量；3. 提出具有O(1)通信复杂度的未排序Top-k算法，用于模型搜索中的安全排序

Result: 在不同数据集上，PPRoute达到了明文对应方案的性能水平，同时相比朴素MPC实现实现了约20倍的加速

Conclusion: PPRoute框架有效解决了LLM路由中的隐私保护问题，通过优化的MPC技术实现了高效且安全的模型路由，为隐私保护的LLM服务部署提供了可行方案

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) routing has emerged as a critical strategy to balance model performance and cost-efficiency by dynamically selecting services from various model providers. However, LLM routing adds an intermediate layer between users and LLMs, creating new privacy risks to user data. These privacy risks have not been systematically studied. Although cryptographic techniques such as Secure Multi-Party Computation (MPC) enable privacy-preserving computation, their protocol design and implementation remain under-explored, and naïve implementations typically incur prohibitive computational overhead. To address this, we propose a privacy-preserving LLM routing framework (PPRoute). PPRoute includes multiple strategies to speed up encoder inference and nearest neighbor search under the MPC and maintain the quality of LLM routing. First, PPRoute uses MPC-friendly operations to boost the encoder inference. Second, PPRoute uses a multiple-step model training algorithm to maintain routing quality despite the constraints of the encrypted domain. Third, PPRoute proposes an unsorted Top-k algorithm with $O(1)$ communication complexity for secure sorting in model search, significantly reducing communication latency. Across different datasets, PPRoute achieves the performance of plaintext counterparts, while achieving approximately a 20$\times$ speedup over naïve MPC implementations.

</details>


### [116] [Secure Authentication in Wireless IoT: Hamming Code Assisted SRAM PUF as Device Fingerprint](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15810)
*Florian Lehn,Pascal Ahr,Hans D. Schotten*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种基于SRAM PUF的阈值认证方案，通过Hamming码纠错和时序多数投票技术，将认证后误码率控制在1%以下，为受限IIoT设备提供硬件根植的身份认证解决方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为受限工业物联网设备提供硬件根植的可靠身份认证方案，解决SRAM PUF固有不可靠性问题，在资源受限环境下平衡安全性和可靠性需求。

Method: 采用Hamming码纠错和时序多数投票相结合的资源高效方法，通过阈值校准、响应长度优化和稳定技术配置，建立综合设计空间来平衡纠错质量与资源约束。

Result: 成功将认证后误码率控制在1%以下，量化了严格可靠性与安全性约束之间的阈值差距，发现增大响应长度可使可靠性优化逐渐过时，建立了全面的PUF纠错设计空间。

Conclusion: 该研究为SRAM PUF在受限IIoT设备中的实现提供了系统设计框架，通过资源感知的阈值校准和参数优化，可在不违反设计误差限制的前提下平衡计算需求、功耗和实现复杂度。

Abstract: Static Random Access Memory (SRAM) Physically Unclonable Functions (PUFs) make use of intrinsic manufacturing variations in memory cells to derive device-unique responses. Employing such hardware-rooted fingerprints for authentication, this work demonstrates a threshold-based authentication proof of concept for constrained Industrial Internet of Things (IIoT) devices. The proposed scheme can reliably cap the the post-authentication bit error rate (BER) below 1 %. Inherent SRAM PUF unreliability is addressed by a resource-efficient combination of Hamming code (HC) Error Correction (EC) and Temporal Majority Voting (TMV). Increasing HC redundancy or TMV count significantly reduces the BER, albeit with diminishing returns and increasingly prohibitive computational overhead.
