Furthermore, this work quantifies the threshold gap between strict reliability and security constraints. This gap is reframed as a design budget which enables the resource-aware calibration of the acceptance threshold, PUF response length, and stabilization technique, without violating designed-for error limits. Larger responses make reliability optimizations increasingly obsolete. This comparative analysis establishes a comprehensive design space for PUF EC, guiding future implementations in balancing EC quality against resource constraints such as computational demand, power consumption, and implementation complexity.

</details>


### [117] [QUACK! Making the (Rubber) Ducky Talk: A Systematic Study of Keystroke Dynamics for HID Injection Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15845)
*Alessandro Lotto,Francesco Marchiori,Mauro Conti*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该论文首次系统性地研究了基于击键动态的人类与机器输入区分方法，提出了一种轻量级、保护隐私的检测方案，仅使用时序特征即可实现自动化攻击检测，无需访问输入内容或用户画像。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代计算系统默认信任人类输入设备，这为自动化攻击创造了可被利用的攻击面。USB HID仿真攻击（如USB Rubber Ducky）利用这一假设注入任意击键序列，绕过传统防御。现有对策基于简单的击键速度或时序规律启发式方法，容易被基本随机化规避。击键动态分析提供了更稳健的替代方案，但先前工作将其视为行为认证问题（验证输入是否来自特定用户），而非检测自动化注入。连续监控方法需要访问输入内容，引发隐私担忧。

Method: 通过五个研究问题指导，系统地表征了独立于用户身份的击键动态用于人机区分。使用轻量级模型，仅基于时序特征进行操作，无需内容访问或用户画像。分析了攻击者复杂性与规避能力的关系，发现鲁棒性取决于对结构多样化生成策略的暴露程度而非模型复杂性。量化了不同击键序列长度下检测及时性与可靠性之间的权衡。

Result: 研究表明，稳健且保护隐私的检测可以通过轻量级模型实现，仅使用时序特征。攻击者复杂性并不单调转化为改进的规避能力，鲁棒性依赖于对结构多样化生成策略的暴露。确定了早期有效攻击拦截的实用操作点，量化了检测及时性与可靠性之间的权衡关系。

Conclusion: 该工作首次系统地表征了基于击键动态的人机区分，证明了一种轻量级、保护隐私的检测方法可行。研究揭示了攻击者复杂性与检测鲁棒性之间的非单调关系，并提供了实用的检测操作点，为自动化攻击检测提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Modern computing systems inherently trust human input devices, creating an exploitable attack surface for adversarial automation. USB Human Interface Device (HID) emulation attacks, such as those enabled by the USB Rubber Ducky, exploit this assumption to inject arbitrary keystroke sequences while bypassing traditional defenses. Existing countermeasures rely on simple heuristics based on typing speed or timing regularity, which can be easily evaded through basic randomization. Keystroke dynamics analysis offers a more robust alternative by modeling temporal typing behavior. However, prior work frames this problem as behavioral authentication, verifying whether input originates from a specific user rather than detecting automated injection. An alternative approach is continuous monitoring via keylogging integrated with intrusion detection systems, but this requires access to input content, raising significant privacy concerns.
  In this paper, we provide the first systematic characterization of keystroke dynamics for human-vs-machine discrimination, independent of user identity. Guided by five research questions, we show that robust, privacy-preserving detection is achievable using lightweight models operating solely on timing features, eliminating the need for content access or user profiling. Our analysis reveals that attacker sophistication does not monotonically translate into improved evasion. Instead, robustness depends on exposure to structurally diverse generation strategies rather than increased model complexity. Finally, we quantify the trade-off between detection timeliness and reliability across varying keystroke sequence lengths, identifying practical operating points for early and effective attack interception.

</details>


### [118] [Module Lattice Security (Part I): Unconditional Verification of Weber's Conjecture for $k \le 12$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15858)
*Ming-Xing Luo*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 首次为k≤12提供了Weber猜想的无条件证明，无需依赖广义黎曼假设


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Weber猜想在格基密码学中至关重要，涉及主理想问题可解性、整数环上模的自由性以及Ring-LWE和Module-LWE最坏情况到平均情况归约的紧致性。现有验证对k≥9依赖广义黎曼假设，需要无条件证明。

Method: 结合Fukuda-Komatsu计算筛法、分圆Z2-塔的归纳结构以及Herbrand定理

Result: 首次为k≤12提供了Weber猜想的无条件证明，突破了之前依赖广义黎曼假设的限制

Conclusion: 该方法成功为较小参数提供了无条件验证，为格基密码学理论基础提供了更坚实的数学保证

Abstract: Weber's conjecture (1886) governs three aspects of lattice-based cryptography: the solvability of the Principal Ideal Problem, the freeness of modules over rings of integers, and the tightness of worst-case-to-average-case reductions in Ring-LWE (R-LWE) and Module-LWE (MLWE). Existing verifications for $k \ge 9$ rely on Generalized Riemann Hypothesis (GRH). In this paper, we present the first unconditional proof for $k \le 12$. Our method combines the Fukuda-Komatsu computational sieve, inductive structure of the cyclotomic $\mathbb{Z}_2$-tower, and Herbrand's theorem.

</details>


### [119] [Low-Stack HAETAE for Memory-Constrained Microcontrollers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15868)
*Gustavo Banegas,Kim Youngbeom,Seo Seog Chung,Vredendaal Christine Van*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 针对内存受限微控制器（8-16kB SRAM）的HAETAE模块格签名方案低栈实现，通过多项优化技术将签名栈从71-141kB降至5.8-6.0kB，验证栈降至4.7-4.8kB


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在内存受限的微控制器上实现HAETAE签名方案面临栈使用峰值约束，特别是超球采样器、大型瞬态多项式向量和变长签名载荷带来的挑战

Method: 采用三项关键技术：1) 拒绝感知的传递分解，将编码隔离到接受后路径；2) 组件级早期拒绝，当部分范数超过边界时短路响应计算；3) 使用范围非对称数字系统(rANS)的反向顺序流式熵编码，消除完整的提示和高位暂存缓冲区。结合流式矩阵生成、带流式高斯后端的双通超球采样器和行流式验证

Result: 将签名栈从参考实现的71-141kB降至5.8-6.0kB，密钥生成栈降至4.7-5.7kB，验证栈降至4.7-4.8kB。在Nucleo-L4R5ZI上实现，相比参考实现分别减少HAETAE-2/3/5的密钥生成栈75%、86%、8%，签名生成栈92%、95%、24%，验证栈85%、91%、22%。验证在所有安全级别下均适配8kB RAM，比ML-DSA m4fstack快2.34-3.34倍

Conclusion: 提出的优化技术成功解决了HAETAE在内存受限微控制器上的栈使用问题，实现了显著的栈减少，同时保持了可接受的性能开销，验证了在ARM Cortex-M4和RISC-V目标上的可移植性

Abstract: We present a low-stack implementation of the module-lattice signature scheme HAETAE, targeting microcontrollers with 8 kB-16 kB of available SRAM. On such devices, peak stack usage is often the binding constraint, and HAETAE's hyperball-based sampler, large transient polynomial vectors, and variable-length signature payloads (hint and high-bits arrays) pose a particular challenge. To address this we introduce (i) Rejection-aware pass decomposition, which isolates encoding to the post-acceptance path; (ii) Component-level early rejection, which short-circuits the response computation when a partial norm already exceeds the bound; and (iii) Reverse-order streaming entropy coding using range Asymmetric Numeral Systems (rANS), which eliminates full hint and high-bits staging buffers. Combined with streamed matrix generation, a two-pass hyperball sampler with streaming Gaussian backend, and row-streamed verification, these techniques bring Signing stack from 71 kB-141 kB in the reference implementation down to 5.8 kB-6.0 kB, key generation to 4.7 kB-5.7 kB, and verification to 4.7 kB-4.8 kB across all three security levels. Our pure C implementation covers all three security levels (HAETAE-2/3/5), whose optimization paths differ due to the public-key domain (d>0 vs. d=0) and rejection structure. We implement our optimization on a Nucleo-L4R5ZI and compare to the reference pqm4 (for HAETAE-2 and -3) and a recently published memory-optimized implementation (targeting HAETAE-5 only). We reduce HAETAE-2, -3, and -5 stack by respectively 75, 86 and 8 % for key generation, 92, 95 and 24 % for signature generation, and 85, 91 and 22 % for verification. Depending on the parameter set, this impacts performance by at most a factor 1.8 and 3.4 for key and signature generation respectively, while even offering a performance improvement up to 18 % for verification. Verification at all security levels fits within 8 kB of RAM (signature buffer + stack) and is 2.34-3.34x faster than ML-DSA m4fstack at each comparable security level. We additionally validate portability under RIOT-OS on ARM Cortex-M4 and RISC-V targets.

</details>


### [120] [TwoHamsters: Benchmarking Multi-Concept Compositional Unsafety in Text-to-Image Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15967)
*Chaoshuo Zhang,Yibo Liang,Mengke Tian,Chenhao Lin,Zhengyu Zhao,Le Yang,Chong Zhang,Yang Zhang,Chao Shen*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该论文提出了多概念组合不安全（MCCU）这一新漏洞，即多个良性概念组合可能产生不安全语义，并构建了包含17.5k提示的TwoHamsters基准来评估该风险。研究发现当前T2I模型和防御机制在MCCU方面存在严重漏洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前文本到图像（T2I）模型的安全对齐主要关注显性恶意概念，忽略了组合语义的微妙风险。作者识别并形式化了多概念组合不安全（MCCU）这一新漏洞，即个体良性概念的隐式关联可能产生不安全语义。

Method: 1. 形式化MCCU漏洞概念；2. 构建TwoHamsters基准，包含17.5k个专门设计用于探测MCCU漏洞的提示；3. 对10个最先进的T2I模型和16种防御机制进行严格评估。

Result: 评估得出8个关键发现：当前T2I模型和防御机制面临严重的MCCU风险。在TwoHamsters上，FLUX模型的MCCU生成成功率高达99.52%，而LLaVA-Guard的召回率仅为41.06%，凸显了当前管理危险组合生成范式的关键局限性。

Conclusion: 该研究揭示了当前T2I安全对齐方法在应对组合语义风险方面的严重不足，提出了MCCU这一新安全挑战，并提供了TwoHamsters基准来推动该领域的安全研究。

Abstract: Despite the remarkable synthesis capabilities of text-to-image (T2I) models, safeguarding them against content violations remains a persistent challenge. Existing safety alignments primarily focus on explicit malicious concepts, often overlooking the subtle yet critical risks of compositional semantics. To address this oversight, we identify and formalize a novel vulnerability: Multi-Concept Compositional Unsafety (MCCU), where unsafe semantics stem from the implicit associations of individually benign concepts. Based on this formulation, we introduce TwoHamsters, a comprehensive benchmark comprising 17.5k prompts curated to probe MCCU vulnerabilities. Through a rigorous evaluation of 10 state-of-the-art models and 16 defense mechanisms, our analysis yields 8 pivotal insights. In particular, we demonstrate that current T2I models and defense mechanisms face severe MCCU risks: on TwoHamsters, FLUX achieves an MCCU generation success rate of 99.52%, while LLaVA-Guard only attains a recall of 41.06%, highlighting a critical limitation of the current paradigm for managing hazardous compositional generation.

</details>


### [121] [Where Does MEV Really Come From? Revisiting CEXDEX Arbitrage on Ethereum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15973)
*Bence Ladóczk,Miklós Rásonyi,János Tapolcai*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种扩展的离散时间AMM模型，通过引入价格跳跃机制来更准确地估计CEX-DEX套利利润，解决了传统Black-Scholes模型低估套利规模的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究以太坊生态中MEV收入的来源，特别是CEX-DEX套利的重要性。传统理论模型显著低估了这些套利的规模，而实证研究虽然强调了其重要性，但仍受限于各种启发式假设的约束。

Method: 提出了一个扩展的离散时间AMM模型，其中价格过程由扩散成分和具有任意噪声分布的随机跳跃组成。使用一般离散时间SDE，通过函数迭代计算平稳概率分布，并证明错价过程是遍历马尔可夫链。在C++中实现模型，从以太坊区块链收集现货价格和AMM交易数据，并将模型参数拟合到观测价格。

Result: 模型估计与实证观察结果高度匹配，为区块链生态系统中的几个基本问题提供了自然的理论解释。CEX-DEX套利需要与主要流动性池总活动量相当的交易量，产生的利润与MEV相当。

Conclusion: 通过引入价格跳跃机制的新AMM模型能够更准确地估计CEX-DEX套利利润，解决了传统Black-Scholes模型低估套利机会的问题，为理解MEV收入来源提供了更可靠的理论框架。

Abstract: A central question of the Ethereum ecosystem is where Maximal Extractable Value (MEV)revenue originates and to what extent it stems from harming unsuspecting users. It is acceptable if MEV arises from arbitrages between centralised and decentralised exchanges (CEX-DEX). Yet theoretical models have significantly underestimated the scale of these arbitrages, while empirical studies have highlighted their importance - though these remain conservative estimates, constrained by numerous debatable heuristic assumptions. Revisiting the theoretical model, we found that CEX-DEX arbitrages require trading volumes on the order of the total activity of major liquidity pools and yield profits comparable to MEV. Most prior AMM models utilised the Black-Scholes (BS) stochastic differential equation (SDE) - i.e., geometric Brownian motion - and assumed continuous price trajectories where asset prices move in small increments only.We argue that BS underestimates arbitrage profits by ignoring price jumps, which are precisely the points at which arbitrage opportunities tend to arise. To address this gap, we present an extended discrete-time AMM model in which the price process is the sum of a diffusive component and stochastic jumps that can have arbitrary noise distributions. Although mathematically more involved this framework allows us to employ a general discrete-time SDE and compute the stationary probability distribution via function iteration with geometric convergence. We further prove that the resulting mispricing process is an ergodic Markov chain. We implement our model in C++, collect spot prices and AMM exchange data from the Ethereum blockchain and fit the model parameters to the observed prices. The estimates derived from our model closely match empirical observations and provide a natural theoretical explanation for several fundamental questions in the blockchain ecosystem.

</details>


### [122] [MATRIX: Multi-Layer Code Watermarking via Dual-Channel Constrained Parity-Check Encoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16001)
*Yuqing Nie,Chong Wang,Guosheng Xu,Guoai Xu,Chenyu Wang,Haoyu Wang,Kailong Wang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: MATRIX是一个新颖的代码水印框架，通过约束奇偶校验矩阵方程实现双通道水印编码，在保持代码功能性的同时提高水印检测准确性和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代码水印方法存在根本性局限：黑盒方法要么具有可检测的语法模式易受统计分析攻击，要么依赖隐式神经嵌入行为导致可解释性、可审计性和精确控制能力弱；白盒方法缺乏代码感知能力可能损害功能。此外，当前单层水印方案无法满足日益复杂的溯源需求，如多级归属和版本跟踪。

Method: 将水印编码建模为求解约束奇偶校验矩阵方程，采用双通道水印技术（变量命名和语义保持变换），结合BCH纠错码和解决方案空间多样性，增强水印覆盖范围和鲁棒性。

Result: 在多个Code LLM生成的Python代码上评估，MATRIX平均水印检测准确率达99.20%，功能损失极小（0-0.14%），对各种攻击的鲁棒性提高7.70-26.67%，水印适用性比现有方法提高2-6倍。

Conclusion: MATRIX为复杂代码溯源场景提供了有效解决方案，在可检测性、保真度和鲁棒性之间取得了良好平衡，解决了现有水印方法的关键局限性。

Abstract: Code Large Language Models (Code LLMs) have revolutionized software development but raised critical concerns regarding code provenance, copyright protection, and security. Existing code watermarking approaches suffer from two fundamental limitations: black-box methods either exhibit detectable syntactic patterns vulnerable to statistical analysis or rely on implicit neural embedding behaviors that weaken interpretability, auditability, and precise control, while white-box methods lack code-aware capabilities that may compromise functionality. Moreover, current single-layer watermarking schemes fail to address increasingly complex provenance requirements such as multi-level attribution and version tracking.
  We present MATRIX, a novel code watermarking framework that formulates watermark encoding as solving constrained parity-check matrix equations. MATRIX employs dual-channel watermarking through variable naming and semantic-preserving transformations, enhancing watermark coverage across a wider range of code while ensuring mutual backup for robustness. By integrating BCH error-correction codes with solution space diversity, our approach achieves robustness against statistical analysis. Extensive evaluation on Python code generated by multiple Code LLMs demonstrates that MATRIX achieves an average watermark detection accuracy of 99.20% with minimal functionality loss (0-0.14%), improves robustness by 7.70-26.67% against various attacks, and increases watermarking applicability by 2-6x compared with existing methods. These results establish MATRIX as an effective solution for complex code provenance scenarios while balancing among detectability, fidelity, and robustness.

</details>


### [123] [PolicyGapper: Automated Detection of Inconsistencies Between Google Play Data Safety Sections and Privacy Policies Using LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16128)
*Luca Ferrari,Billel Habbati,Meriem Guerar,Mariano Ceccato,Luca Verderame*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: PolicyGapper：基于LLM的自动化工具，用于检测Google Play数据安全部分与隐私政策之间的不一致性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Google Play要求开发者提供数据安全部分披露，但研究表明近80%热门应用存在不完整或误导性声明，且目前缺乏自动化工具验证DSS与隐私政策的一致性

Method: 基于LLM的四阶段方法：爬取、预处理、分析和后处理，无需访问应用二进制文件

Result: 在330个顶级应用中识别出2,689个遗漏披露（2,040个数据收集相关，649个数据共享相关），在10%分层子集上平均精确率0.75，召回率0.77，准确率0.69，F1分数0.76

Conclusion: PolicyGapper能有效检测DSS与隐私政策之间的差异，为应用隐私透明度提供自动化验证工具，支持可复现性

Abstract: Mobile application developers are required to disclose how they collect, use, and share user data in compliance with privacy regulations. To support transparency, major app marketplaces have introduced standardized disclosure mechanisms. In 2022, Google mandated the Data Safety Section (DSS) on Google Play, requiring developers to summarize their data practices. However, compiling accurate DSS disclosures is challenging, as they must remain consistent with the corresponding privacy policy (PP), and no automated tool currently verifies this alignment. Prior studies indicate that nearly 80% of popular apps contain incomplete or misleading DSS declarations. We present PolicyGapper, an LLM-based methodology for automatically detecting discrepancies between DSS disclosures and privacy policies. PolicyGapper operates in four stages: scraping, pre-processing, analysis, and post-processing, without requiring access to application binaries. We evaluate PolicyGapper on a dataset of 330 top-ranked apps spanning all 33 Google Play categories, collected in Q3 2025. The approach identifies 2,689 omitted disclosures, including 2,040 related to data collection and 649 to data sharing. Manual validation on a stratified 10% subset, repeated across three independent runs, yields an average Precision of 0.75, Recall of 0.77, Accuracy of 0.69, and F1-score of 0.76. To support reproducibility, we release a complete replication package, including the dataset, prompts, source code, and results available at https://github.com/Mobile-IoT-Security-Lab/PolicyGapper and https://doi.org/10.5281/zenodo.19628493.

</details>


<div id='cs.PL'></div>

# cs.PL [[Back]](#toc)

### [124] [Verification Modulo Tested Library Contracts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15533)
*Abhishek Uppar,Omar Muhammad,Sumanth Prabhu,Deepak D'Souza,Madhusudan P,Adithya Murali*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: 提出验证模块化测试库合约的问题，通过合成库方法的模块化合约来验证客户端程序，引入上下文合约概念，使用反例引导学习框架解决该问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动化验证使用复杂库的客户端程序，解决传统验证方法在处理大型库时的困难，通过测试库合约来增强验证的可信度。

Method: 提出验证模块化测试库合约问题，形式化为合成库方法的模块化合约；引入上下文合约概念；采用反例引导学习框架，结合约束求解器和测试引擎；使用基于ICE学习算法的通用CHC求解器作为主要合成引擎。

Result: 实现了名为vmtlc的工具，在客户端调用大型库的基准测试中展示了其有效性。

Conclusion: 验证模块化测试库合约是自动化验证使用复杂库客户端程序的有效方法，上下文合约可以比传统模块化合约更简单易推断，反例引导学习框架结合CHC求解器和测试引擎能够有效合成足够的合约和不变式。

Abstract: We consider the problem of \emph{verification modulo tested library
  contracts} as a step towards automating the verification of client programs that use complex libraries. We formulate this problem as the synthesis of modular contracts for the library methods used by the client that are adequate to prove the client correct, and that also pass the scrutiny of a testing engine that tests the library against these contracts. We also consider a new form of method contracts called \emph{contextual contracts} that arise in this setting that hold in the context of the client program, and can often be simpler and easier to infer than classical modular contracts. We provide a counterexample-guided learning framework to solve this problem, in which the synthesizer interacts with a constraint solver as well as the testing engine in order to infer adequate modular/contextual method contracts and inductive invariants for the client. The main synthesis engines we use are generalizing CHC solvers that are realized using ICE learning algorithms. We realize this framework in a tool called \vmtlc and show its efficacy on benchmarks where clients call large libraries.

</details>


### [125] [Synthesizing Backward Error Bounds, Backward](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15633)
*Laura Zielinski,Justin Hsu*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: 提出一个用于数值程序后向误差分析的自动化框架，包括广义后向稳定性定义、范畴论模型Shel和自动化工具eggshel


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 后向稳定性是数值算法的重要性质，但现有自动化工具主要针对前向误差分析，缺乏针对后向误差分析的有效工具

Method: 1) 提出新的广义后向稳定性定义，具有组合性和灵活性；2) 建立范畴Shel，其态射建模稳定数值程序；3) 实现自动化工具eggshel，在Shel的语法子范畴中搜索证明后向稳定性

Result: eggshel工具能够自动合成后向误差界，处理包含变量重用的程序，这些程序之前超出自动化分析范围

Conclusion: 该框架为数值程序后向误差分析提供了形式化、自动化的解决方案，扩展了自动化分析的能力范围

Abstract: Backward stability is a desirable property for a well-designed numerical algorithm: given an input, a backward stable floating-point program produces the exact output for a nearby input. While automated tools for bounding the forward error of a numerical program are well-established, few existing tools target backward error analysis. We present a formal framework that enables sound, automated backward error analysis for a broad class of numerical programs. First, we propose a novel generalization of the definition of backward stability that is both compositional and flexible, satisfied by a wide range of floating-point operations. Second, based on this generalization, we develop the category Shel where morphisms model stable numerical programs, and show that structures in Shel support a rich variety of backward error analyses. Third, we implement a tool, eggshel, that automatically searches within a syntactic subcategory of Shel to prove backward stability for a given program. Our algorithm handles many programs with variable reuse, a known challenge in backward error analysis. We prove soundness of our algorithm and use our tool to synthesize backward error bounds for a suite of programs that were previously beyond the reach of automated analysis.

</details>


### [126] [jMT: Testing Correctness of Java Memory Models (Extended Version)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15978)
*Lukas Panneke,Heike Wehrheim*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: jMT是一个用于构建并发Java程序多执行语义的新工具，通过因果检查从单执行模型构建有效的多执行语义，支持评估Java内存模型提案并测试其与编译方案的符合性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Java内存模型存在缺陷，现有修复方案要么导致意外行为，要么禁止标准编译器优化。由于缺乏自动化工具来验证新的Java内存模型提案，需要开发能够构建多执行语义的工具来评估这些提案。

Method: jMT基于定义良好执行图的单执行模型，通过因果检查构建有效的多执行语义。它支持按程序评估Java内存模型提案，并可用于测试内存模型与现有编译方案和编译器的符合性。

Result: 在169个litmus测试上的评估揭示了现有Java内存模型的一些有趣见解，展示了jMT在分析和评估内存模型方面的有效性。

Conclusion: jMT填补了Java内存模型验证工具的空白，能够系统地评估新提案并测试其与编译优化的兼容性，为内存模型研究提供了重要工具支持。

Abstract: Folklore is often saying "The Java memory model is broken." Therefore, several approaches have proposed repairs, only to find new programs exhibiting unexpected, unintuitive behavior or the model forbidding standard compiler optimizations. The complexity of defining a memory model for concurrent Java lies in the fact that it requires a multi-execution model. Multi-execution models need to inspect the many potential executions of a program in order to find the valid ones. Tools automatically validating novel proposals of Java memory models are, however, largely lacking. To alleviate this problem, we introduce jMT, a novel tool for constructing multi-execution semantics for concurrent Java programs. jMT relies on single-execution models defining well-formed execution graphs, based on which it builds valid multi-execution semantics via causality checking. Thereby, jMT supports evaluating new proposals of Java memory models (JMMs) on a per-program basis. jMT can furthermore be employed for testing the conformance of JMMs to existing compilation schemes and compilers. Our evaluation of jMT on 169 litmus tests reveals a number of interesting insights into existing JMMs.

</details>


<div id='cs.NE'></div>

# cs.NE [[Back]](#toc)

### [127] [Frenetic Cat-inspired Particle Optimization: a Markov state-switching hybrid swarm optimizer with application to cardiac digital twinning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15761)
*Jorge Sánchez,Guadalupe García-Isla,Sandra Perez-Herrero,Beatriz Trenor,Javier Saiz*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: FCPO是一种混合粒子群优化方法，通过马尔可夫切换控制器调度探索和细化算子，在有限评估预算下实现高效优化，在CEC 2022基准测试和心脏数字孪生校准任务中表现出色。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在昂贵黑盒优化场景（如心脏数字孪生）中，设计在有限评估预算下仍能保持有效的优化器至关重要。需要开发能够在探索和细化之间智能切换的高效优化方法。

Method: FCPO（Frenetic Cat-inspired Particle Optimization）是一种混合粒子群方法，将粒子群优化动力学与显式状态马尔可夫切换控制器耦合，在线调度探索和细化算子。包含四个核心组件：(1)状态条件有界运动；(2)精英差异全局跳跃算子用于逃离停滞；(3)基于精英协方差的特征空间引导局部细化；(4)线性种群大小缩减以控制后期计算成本。

Result: 在CEC 2022基准测试中，FCPO在10个测试案例上获得最低平均运行时间（0.183秒），比CMA-ES快2.3倍，比L-SHADE快2.6倍。在D=20的多模态组合函数F10上，FCPO获得最佳平均目标值（9.625×10²±1.275×10³）且运行时间更快（0.602秒 vs CMA-ES的1.126秒）。在结构化景观（F1-F3）和混合函数（F6）上，CMA-ES精度最高，但FCPO显著优于经典粒子群方法并保持有利的精度-运行时间权衡。在心室激活数字孪生校准任务中，FCPO在约40次迭代内达到目标ECG保真度（RMSE<0.1 mV），并产生生理合理的激活图。

Conclusion: FCPO通过马尔可夫切换控制器智能调度探索和细化算子，在有限评估预算下实现了高效的优化性能。该方法在标准基准测试和实际心脏数字孪生校准任务中都表现出色，为昂贵逆问题提供了实用的优化器解决方案。

Abstract: Designing optimizers that remain effective under tight evaluation budgets is critical in expensive black-box settings such as cardiac digital twinning. We propose Frenetic Cat-inspired Particle Optimization (FCPO), a hybrid swarm method that couples particle swarm optimization-like dynamics with an explicit-state Markov switching controller to schedule exploration and refinement operators online. FCPO integrates (i) state-conditioned bounded motion, (ii) an elite-difference global jump operator to escape stagnation, (iii) eigen-space guided local refinement from elite covariance, and (iv) linear population size reduction to control late-stage computational cost. We benchmark FCPO on five representative functions from the Congress on Evolutionary Computation (CEC) 2022 suite (F1, F2, F3, F6 and F10) at dimensions D$\in${10,20} over 30 independent runs, comparing against PSO, CSO, CLPSO, SHADE, L-SHADE and CMA-ES. FCPO achieves the lowest mean runtime across the ten benchmark cases (average 0.183 s), about 2.3x faster than CMA-ES and 2.6x faster than L-SHADE in our Python implementation. On the multimodal composition function F10 at D=20, FCPO attains the best mean objective (9.625x 10^2 $\pm$ 1.275x 10^3) and remains faster than CMA-ES (0.602 s vs. 1.126 s mean runtime). On structured landscapes (F1--F3) and on the hybrid function (F6), CMA-ES remains the most accurate method, while FCPO substantially improves over classical swarms and maintains a favorable accuracy--runtime trade-off. Finally, in a ventricular activation digital twin calibration task, FCPO reaches the target electrocardiogram (ECG) fidelity (RMSE<0.1 mV) within ~ 40 iterations and produces physiologically plausible activation maps with robust convergence across repeated initializations, supporting its use as a practical optimizer for expensive inverse problems.

</details>


<div id='cs.MA'></div>

# cs.MA [[Back]](#toc)

### [128] [InfoChess: A Game of Adversarial Inference and a Laboratory for Quantifiable Information Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15373)
*Kieran A. Murphy*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: InfoChess是一个对称对抗游戏，将竞争性信息获取作为主要目标，通过棋子改变可见性而非捕获，玩家根据对对手国王位置的推理能力评分


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 设计一个专门研究多智能体在部分可观测环境下推理能力的测试平台，消除传统棋类游戏中物质激励的干扰，专注于信息获取的竞争性

Method: 提出InfoChess游戏机制，引入基于对手建模层次结构的启发式智能体，训练强化学习智能体，并利用信息论指标（信念熵、oracle交叉熵、预测对数分数）分析游戏过程

Result: 强化学习智能体优于启发式基线，信息论指标能够解耦认知不确定性、校准不匹配和对抗移动引起的不确定性

Conclusion: InfoChess作为研究部分可观测下多智能体推理的有效测试平台，已发布环境和智能体代码以促进进一步研究

Abstract: We propose InfoChess, a symmetric adversarial game that elevates competitive information acquisition to the primary objective. There is no piece capture, removing material incentives that would otherwise confound the role of information. Instead, pieces are used to alter visibility. Players are scored on their probabilistic inference of the opponent's king location over the duration of the game. To explore the space of strategies for playing InfoChess, we introduce a hierarchy of heuristic agents defined by increasing levels of opponent modeling, and train a reinforcement learning agent that outperforms these baselines. Leveraging the discrete structure of the game, we analyze gameplay through natural information-theoretic characterizations that include belief entropy, oracle cross entropy, and predictive log score under the action-induced observation channel. These measures disentangle epistemic uncertainty, calibration mismatch, and uncertainty induced by adversarial movement. The design of InfoChess renders it a testbed for studying multi-agent inference under partial observability. We release code for the environment and agents, and a public interface to encourage further study.

</details>


### [129] [Scalable Algorithms with Provable Optimality Bounds for the Multiple Watchman Route Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15610)
*Srikar Gouru,Ariel Felner,Jiaoyang Li*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出针对多看守路径问题(MWRP)的优化算法，包括状态空间缩减方法、最优规划器MWRP-CP3、次优算法及其变体，显著提升求解效率和规模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决多看守路径问题(MWRP)中搜索空间过大、计算效率低的问题，旨在开发能够处理更大规模地图的优化算法。

Method: 1) 提出多种状态空间缩减方法，通过剪枝保证在前往其他区域途中可见的地图区域；2) 开发MWRP-CP3最优规划器，结合状态缩减与启发式改进技术；3) 提出具有解质量保证的次优算法，包括针对makespan问题的加权A*变体MxWA*；4) 开发任意时间算法变体及通过分解子问题改进现有次优解的技术。

Result: MWRP-CP3能减少95%以上的搜索空间，在2D网格地图上比现有最优算法快200倍以上；次优算法能解决比MWRP-CP3可处理地图大3倍的地图。

Conclusion: 提出的方法显著提升了多看守路径问题的求解效率和规模，为大规模实际应用提供了有效的解决方案，相关代码已开源。

Abstract: In this paper, we tackle the Multiple Watchman Route Problem (MWRP), which aims to find a set of paths that M watchmen can follow such that every location on the map can be seen by at least one watchman. First, we propose multiple methods to reduce the state space over which a search needs to be conducted by pruning map areas that are guaranteed to be seen en route to other areas. Next, we introduce MWRP-CP3, an efficient optimal planner that combines these methods with techniques that improve the quality and calculation time of existing heuristics. We present several suboptimal algorithms with bounds on solution quality, including MxWA*, a general variant of weighted A* for makespan problems. We also present anytime variations of our suboptimal algorithms, as well as techniques to improve an existing suboptimal solution by solving multiple decomposed sub-problems. We show that MWRP-CP3 can reduce the search space by more than 95% and runs more than 200x faster than existing optimal algorithms on 2D grid maps. We also show that our suboptimal algorithms solve maps 3x larger than those solvable by MWRP-CP3. See mwrp-cp3.github.io for the open source codebase and video demonstrations.

</details>


### [130] [AstroVLM: Expert Multi-agent Collaborative Reasoning for Astronomical Imaging Quality Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16024)
*Yaohui Han,Tianshuo Wang,Zixi Zhao,Zhengchun Zhu,Shuo Ren,Yiru Wang,Rongliang Fu,Tinghuan Chen,Tsung-Yi Ho*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: AstroVLM：用于天文图像质量诊断的多智能体协作视觉语言模型系统


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 天文成像过程复杂，涉及多学科知识和多个子任务，现有视觉语言模型在该领域研究不足。天文成像过程各环节存在复杂的底层相关性，相互影响显著，导致天文图像质量诊断和错误定位极具挑战性。

Method: 提出AstroVLM，一个用于天文图像质量诊断的协作多智能体系统。该系统利用多智能体协作来处理复杂的多过程任务。

Result: 实验结果表明，AstroVLM在真实世界天文成像质量诊断任务上优于所有基线方法。

Conclusion: AstroVLM为语言模型处理复杂多过程任务提供了参考，展示了多智能体系统在解决复杂跨学科问题上的潜力。

Abstract: Vision Language Models (VLMs) have been applied to several specific domains and have shown strong problem-solving capabilities. However, astronomical imaging, a quite complex problem involving multidisciplinary knowledge and several subtasks, has not been adequately studied. Due to the complexity of the astronomical imaging process, both world-class astronomical organizations, such as NASA, and expert enthusiasts devote a great deal of time and effort. This is because the processes in astronomical imaging have complex underlying correlations that significantly influence one another, making the quality diagnosis and error localization of astronomical images challenging. To address this problem, we propose AstroVLM, a collaborative multi-agent system for diagnosing the quality of astronomical images. Experiment results show that AstroVLM outperforms all baselines on real-world astronomical imaging quality diagnosis tasks, providing a reference for language models to handle complicated multi-process tasks.

</details>


### [131] [Veritas-RPM: Provenance-Guided Multi-Agent False Positive Suppression for Remote Patient Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16081)
*Aswini Misro,Vikash Sharma,Shreyank N Gowda*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: Veritas-RPM是一个包含五层处理架构的多智能体系统，用于远程患者监护中的假阳性抑制，通过构建98种假阳性场景的分类法，在530个合成患者时期上进行评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决远程患者监护(RPM)中假阳性警报过多的问题，通过多智能体架构提高警报验证的准确性和效率，减少不必要的临床干预。

Method: 设计五层处理架构：VeritasAgent(真值组装)、SentinelLayer(异常检测)、DirectorAgent(专家路由)、六个领域专家智能体、MetaSentinelAgent(冲突解决和最终决策)。构建包含98种假阳性场景的合成分类法，生成530个合成患者时期，使用真值标签评估性能。

Result: 使用真抑制率(TSR)、假升级率(FER)和不确定率(INDR)三个指标报告性能，所有案例都有已知的真值标签。

Conclusion: Veritas-RPM是一个系统性的多智能体架构，通过分层处理和专家路由机制，旨在有效识别和抑制远程患者监护中的假阳性警报，提高临床决策的可靠性。

Abstract: We present Veritas-RPM, a provenance-guided multi-agent architecture comprising five processing layers: VeritasAgent (ground-truth assembly), SentinelLayer (anomaly detection), DirectorAgent (specialist routing), six domain Specialist Agents, and MetaSentinelAgent (conflict resolution and final decision). We construct a 98-case synthetic taxonomy of false-positive scenarios derived from documented RPM patterns. Synthetic patient epochs (n = 530) were generated directly from taxonomy parameters and processed through the pipeline. Ground-truth labels are known for all cases. Performance is reported as True Suppression Rate (TSR), False Escalation Rate (FER), and Indeterminate Rate (INDR).

</details>


<div id='cs.ET'></div>

# cs.ET [[Back]](#toc)

### [132] [The Relic Condition: When Published Scholarship Becomes Material for Its Own Replacement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16116)
*Lin Deng,Chang-bo Liu*

Main category: cs.ET

TL;DR: 从两位顶尖人文社科学者的已发表文献中提取其学术推理系统，转化为大语言模型的推理约束，创建学者机器人并评估其在学术核心任务中的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索是否能够仅从已发表的学术文献中提取学者的推理系统，并将其转化为可部署的人工智能系统，以执行学术核心功能。研究当出版系统使稳定的推理架构变得可读、可提取且廉价部署时，智力劳动的公共记录如何成为其自身功能替代的原材料。

Method: 采用八层提取方法和九模块技能架构，基于本地封闭语料库分析，从两位国际知名人文社科学者的发表文献中提取其学术推理系统。将这些系统转化为大语言模型的推理约束，创建学者机器人。在博士指导、同行评审、讲座和小组式学术交流等场景中部署。由三位资深学者进行专家评估，包括报告和聘任级别的综合分析。

Result: 所有评审和监督报告均判断输出达到基准水平；聘任级别推荐将两个机器人置于澳大利亚大学系统中的高级讲师或更高水平；恢复的小组评分显示学者A在7.9-8.9/10之间，学者B在8.5-8.9/10之间（多轮辩论条件下）。研究型学位学生调查显示在信息可靠性、理论深度和逻辑严谨性方面获得高评分，尽管所有参与者已是前沿模型用户，但在7点量表上仍出现明显的天花板效应。

Conclusion: 当出版系统使稳定的推理架构变得可读、可提取且廉价部署时，智力劳动的公共记录成为其自身功能替代的原材料（称为"遗物条件"）。由于这一转变的技术门槛在适度工程努力下已被跨越，作者认为建立涵盖披露、同意、补偿和部署限制的保护框架的窗口期是现在，此时部署仍然是可选的而非基础设施性的。

Abstract: We extracted the scholarly reasoning systems of two internationally prominent humanities and social science scholars from their published corpora alone, converted those systems into structured inference-time constraints for a large language model, and tested whether the resulting scholar-bots could perform core academic functions at expert-assessed quality. The distillation pipeline used an eight-layer extraction method and a nine-module skill architecture grounded in local, closed-corpus analysis. The scholar-bots were then deployed across doctoral supervision, peer review, lecturing and panel-style academic exchange. Expert assessment involved three senior academics producing reports and appointment-level syntheses. Across the preserved expert record, all review and supervision reports judged the outputs benchmark-attaining, appointment-level recommendations placed both bots at or above Senior Lecturer level in the Australian university system, and recovered panel scores placed Scholar A between 7.9 and 8.9/10 and Scholar B between 8.5 and 8.9/10 under multi-turn debate conditions. A research-degree-student survey showed high performance ratings across information reliability, theoretical depth and logical rigor, with pronounced ceiling effects on a 7-point scale, despite all participants already being frontier-model users. We term this the Relic condition: when publication systems make stable reasoning architectures legible, extractable and cheaply deployable, the public record of intellectual labor becomes raw material for its own functional replacement. Because the technical threshold for this transition is already crossed at modest engineering effort, we argue that the window for protective frameworks covering disclosure, consent, compensation and deployment restriction is the present, while deployment remains optional rather than infrastructural.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [133] [Contact-Aware Planning and Control of Continuum Robots in Highly Constrained Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15638)
*Aedan Mangan,Kehan Long,Ki Myung Brian Lee,Miheer Potdar,Nikolay Atanasov,Tania K. Morimoto*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种接触感知规划方法，用于连续体机器人在受限解剖环境中的导航，通过评估接触质量、惩罚危险接触、允许良性接触，生成运动轨迹和接触感知雅可比矩阵，实现安全可靠的导航。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续体机器人在血管或腔内解剖等受限脆弱环境中导航时，与周围结构的接触不可避免。虽然可控接触可以辅助运动，但不理想的接触会降低可控性、引发运动学奇点或带来安全风险。因此需要一种能够区分接触质量并相应规划的接触感知方法。

Method: 提出接触感知规划方法：1) 评估接触质量，惩罚危险交互，允许良性接触；2) 生成运动学可行的轨迹；3) 计算接触感知雅可比矩阵，用于硬件实验中的闭环控制；4) 在患者扫描的解剖模型上验证集成系统（规划、控制和机械设计）。

Result: 在三种常见解剖环境中，规划器均能生成有效路径。硬件实验中，连续体机器人成功到达目标并避免危险尖端接触（成功率100%）。三种环境中的平均跟踪误差分别为1.9±0.5mm、1.2±0.1mm和1.7±0.2mm。消融研究表明，惩罚末端连续体段接触提高了可操纵性并防止了硬件故障。

Conclusion: 该工作实现了在高度受限环境中的可靠、接触感知导航。通过区分接触质量、惩罚危险接触、允许良性接触，规划器能够生成安全有效的运动轨迹，结合接触感知雅可比矩阵的闭环控制，为连续体机器人在解剖环境中的临床应用提供了重要支持。

Abstract: Continuum robots are well suited for navigating confined and fragile environments, such as vascular or endoluminal anatomy, where contact with surrounding structures is often unavoidable. While controlled contact can assist motion, unfavorable contact can degrade controllability, induce kinematic singularities, or introduce safety risks. We present a contact-aware planning approach that evaluates contact quality, penalizing hazardous interactions, while permitting benign contact. The planner produces kinematically feasible trajectories and contact-aware Jacobians which can be used for closed-loop control in hardware experiments. We validate the approach by testing the integrated system (planning, control, and mechanical design) on anatomical models from patient scans. The planner generates effective plans for three common anatomical environments, and, in all hardware trials, the continuum robot was able to reach the target while avoiding dangerous tip contact (100% success). Mean tracking errors were 1.9 +/- 0.5 mm, 1.2 +/- 0.1 mm, and 1.7 +/- 0.2 mm across the three different environments. Ablation studies showed that penalizing end-of-continuum-segment (ECS) contact improved manipulability and prevented hardware failures. Overall, this work enables reliable, contact-aware navigation in highly constrained environments.

</details>


### [134] [Iterated Invariant EKF for Quadruped Robot Odometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15449)
*Hilton Marques Souza Santana,João Carlos Virgolino Soares,Sven Goffin,Ylenia Nisticò,Silvère Bonnabel,Claudio Semini,Marco Antonio Meggiolaro*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于迭代不变扩展卡尔曼滤波(IterIEKF)的新型开源腿式机器人状态估计算法，仅使用本体感知测量，利用足部接触时的速度约束和基座速度，在精度和一致性方面优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统卡尔曼滤波算法需要线性系统动态和高斯噪声假设，这在实践中难以满足。虽然IEKF将假设(a)扩展到非线性空间，但测量模型的非线性处理仍有待解决。本文旨在开发一种适用于腿式机器人的鲁棒状态估计算法，克服环境条件变化的影响。

Method: 基于迭代不变扩展卡尔曼滤波(IterIEKF)，提出一种新型状态估计算法。更新步骤仅依赖本体感知测量，利用足部接触时的速度运动学约束和基座速度约束。算法在Lie群上实现，保持了经典卡尔曼滤波的兼容性特性。

Result: 通过大量数值仿真和真实世界数据集评估，IterIEKF在精度和一致性方面均优于vanilla IEKF、基于SO(3)的卡尔曼滤波及其迭代变体。算法对环境条件变化表现出固有的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 提出的基于IterIEKF的腿式机器人状态估计算法有效解决了非线性测量模型问题，仅使用本体感知测量即可实现鲁棒的状态估计，为腿式机器人在复杂环境中的可靠导航提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Kalman filter-based algorithms are fundamental for mobile robots, as they provide a computationally efficient solution to the challenging problem of state estimation. However, they rely on two main assumptions that are difficult to satisfy in practice: (a) the system dynamics must be linear with Gaussian process noise, and (b) the measurement model must also be linear with Gaussian measurement noise. Previous works have extended assumption (a) to nonlinear spaces through the Invariant Extended Kalman Filter (IEKF), showing that it retains properties similar to those of the classical Kalman filter when the system dynamics are group-affine on a Lie group. More recently, the counterpart of assumption (b) for the same nonlinear setting was addressed in [1]. By means of the proposed Iterated Invariant Extended Kalman Filter (IterIEKF), the authors of that work demonstrated that the update step exhibits several compatibility properties of the classical linear Kalman filter. In this work, we introduce a novel open-source state estimation algorithm for legged robots based on the IterIEKF. The update step of the proposed filter relies solely on proprioceptive measurements, exploiting kinematic constraints on foot velocity during contact and base-frame velocity, making it inherently robust to environmental conditions. Through extensive numerical simulations and evaluation on real-world datasets, we demonstrate that the IterIEKF outperforms the vanilla IEKF, the SO(3)-based Kalman Filter, and its iterated variant in terms of both accuracy and consistency.

</details>


### [135] [One-Shot Cross-Geometry Skill Transfer through Part Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15455)
*Skye Thompson,Ondrej Biza,George Konidaris*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于语义部件分解的机器人技能迁移方法，通过生成式形状模型将交互点从演示对象迁移到新对象，实现单次演示的跨几何形状技能泛化


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有技能迁移方法难以适应形状陌生的物体，而组合建模方法显示出更好的迁移效果，因此需要开发能够分解物体语义部件的方法来改进技能迁移能力

Method: 将物体分解为语义部件，利用数据高效的生成式形状模型将交互点从演示对象部件准确迁移到新对象，自主构建优化目标以对齐技能相关物体部件上的点

Result: 该方法比现有工作能泛化到更广泛的物体几何形状，在模拟和真实环境中都能通过单次演示成功迁移多种技能和物体

Conclusion: 通过语义部件分解和生成式形状模型的方法，能够显著提高机器人技能迁移的泛化能力，特别是在处理形状陌生的物体时表现出色

Abstract: Given a demonstration, a robot should be able to generalize a skill to any object it encounters-but existing approaches to skill transfer often fail to adapt to objects with unfamiliar shapes. Motivated by examples of improved transfer from compositional modeling, we propose a method for improving transfer by decomposing objects into their constituent semantic parts. We leverage data-efficient generative shape models to accurately transfer interaction points from the parts of a demonstration object to a novel object. We autonomously construct an objective to optimize the alignment of those points on skill-relevant object parts. Our method generalizes to a wider range of object geometries than existing work, and achieves successful one-shot transfer for a range of skills and objects from a single demonstration, in both simulated and real environments.

</details>


### [136] [NeuroMesh: A Unified Neural Inference Framework for Decentralized Multi-Robot Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15475)
*Yang Zhou,Yash Shetye,Long Quang,Devon Super,Jesse Milzman,Manohari Goarin,Aditya Azad,Devang Sunil Dhake,Jeffery Mao,Carlos Nieto-Granda,Giuseppe Loianno*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: NeuroMesh是一个多领域、跨平台、模块化的去中心化神经推理框架，用于异构机器人团队的标准化学习模型部署


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异构机器人硬件差异、通信限制以及缺乏统一执行栈使得学习型多机器人模型部署面临挑战

Method: 采用双聚合范式（基于归约和广播的信息融合）和并行化架构，将周期时间与端到端延迟解耦，使用高性能C++实现，基于Zenoh进行机器人间通信，支持GPU/CPU混合推理

Result: 在异构空中和地面机器人团队上验证了NeuroMesh在协作感知、去中心化控制和任务分配等任务中的鲁棒性，能够适应不同任务结构和有效载荷大小

Conclusion: NeuroMesh提供了一个统一的多机器人神经推理框架，计划开源以促进社区发展

Abstract: Deploying learned multi-robot models on heterogeneous robots remains challenging due to hardware heterogeneity, communication constraints, and the lack of a unified execution stack. This paper presents NeuroMesh, a multi-domain, cross-platform, and modular decentralized neural inference framework that standardizes observation encoding, message passing, aggregation, and task decoding in a unified pipeline. NeuroMesh combines a dual-aggregation paradigm for reduction- and broadcast-based information fusion with a parallelized architecture that decouples cycle time from end-to-end latency. Our high-performance C++ implementation leverages Zenoh for inter-robot communication and supports hybrid GPU/CPU inference. We validate NeuroMesh on a heterogeneous team of aerial and ground robots across collaborative perception, decentralized control, and task assignment, demonstrating robust operation across diverse task structures and payload sizes. We plan to release NeuroMesh as an open-source framework to the community.

</details>


### [137] [Trajectory Planning for Safe Dual Control with Active Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15507)
*Kaleb Ben Naveed,Manveer Singh,Devansh R. Agrawal,Dimitra Panagou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Dual-gatekeeper框架：在安全约束和任务成本预算下，将鲁棒规划与主动探索相结合，仅在探索能带来可验证改进且不损害安全或违反预算时进行探索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在处理模型不确定性时存在两个主要问题：1）鲁棒规划考虑最坏情况但忽略不确定性减少，导致过于保守；2）大多数双控制方法通过加权探索项来权衡目标与不确定性减少，但缺乏对探索何时有益的正式考虑，且安全保证不一致。

Method: 提出Dual-gatekeeper框架，将鲁棒规划与主动探索集成，在安全约束和任务级成本预算下减少不确定性。核心思想是：仅在探索能提供可验证改进且不损害安全或违反预算时才进行探索。提供了基于不同安全机制的两种实现。

Result: 在具有参数不确定性的四旋翼导航和自动驾驶赛车案例研究中展示了框架性能。系统能够在保证安全和预算可行性的前提下，平衡即时任务性能与长期不确定性减少。

Conclusion: Dual-gatekeeper框架为预算约束的双控制问题提供了原则性解决方案，通过正式的安全和预算可行性保证，实现了鲁棒规划与主动探索的集成，使系统能够智能地平衡任务性能与不确定性减少。

Abstract: Planning safe trajectories under model uncertainty is a fundamental challenge. Robust planning ensures safety by considering worst-case realizations, yet ignores uncertainty reduction and leads to overly conservative behavior. Actively reducing uncertainty on-the-fly during a nominal mission defines the dual control problem. Most approaches address this by adding a weighted exploration term to the cost, tuned to trade off the nominal objective and uncertainty reduction, but without formal consideration of when exploration is beneficial. Moreover, safety is enforced in some methods but not in others. We study a budget-constrained dual control problem, where uncertainty is reduced subject to safety and a mission-level cost budget that limits the allowable degradation in task performance due to exploration. In this work, we propose Dual-gatekeeper, a framework that integrates robust planning with active exploration under formal guarantees of safety and budget feasibility. The key idea is that exploration is pursued only when it provides a verifiable improvement without compromising safety or violating the budget, enabling the system to balance immediate task performance with long-term uncertainty reduction in a principled manner. We provide two implementations of the framework based on different safety mechanisms and demonstrate its performance on quadrotor navigation and autonomous car racing case studies under parametric uncertainty.

</details>


### [138] [ShapeGen: Robotic Data Generation for Category-Level Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15569)
*Yirui Wang,Xiuwei Xu,Angyuan Ma,Bingyao Yu,Jie Zhou,Jiwen Lu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ShapeGen：一种免模拟器、基于3D的形状变化操作数据生成方法，通过形状库构建和功能感知生成两阶段流程，提升策略在真实场景中的类别内形状泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界中部署的操作策略面临日常物体在类别内几何形状的巨大多样性。为了在如此变化下稳健运行，策略需要以类别级别工作，即知道如何与特定类别中的任何物体交互，而不仅仅是训练期间看到的特定物体。这种类别内泛化能力通常需要形状多样化的训练数据来培养，但手动收集这样的数据由于需要密集的人工劳动和大量不同物体而不可行。

Method: ShapeGen将过程分解为两个阶段：1) 形状库构建：训练形状之间的空间扭曲映射，将功能对应的点映射到点，并将3D模型与扭曲映射聚合到即插即用的形状库中；2) 功能感知生成：设计一个利用已建立库的流程，仅需最少的人工标注即可生成物理合理且功能正确的新演示。

Result: 真实世界实验证明了ShapeGen在提升策略类别内形状泛化能力方面的有效性。

Conclusion: ShapeGen提供了一种免模拟器、基于3D的形状变化操作数据生成方法，通过两阶段流程有效解决了手动收集多样化训练数据的不可行性问题，显著提升了操作策略在真实场景中的类别内形状泛化能力。

Abstract: Manipulation policies deployed in uncontrolled real-world scenarios are faced with great in-category geometric diversity of everyday objects. In order to function robustly under such variations, policies need to work in a category-level manner, i.e. knowing how to interact with any object in a certain category, instead of only a specific one seen during training. This in-category generalizability is usually nurtured with shape-diversified training data; however, manually collecting such a corpus of data is infeasible due to the requirement of intense human labor and large collections of divergent objects at hand. In this paper, we propose ShapeGen, a data generation method that aims at generating shape-variated manipulation data in a simulator-free and 3D manner. ShapeGen decomposes the process into two stages: Shape Library curation and Function-Aware Generation. In the first stage, we train spatial warpings between shapes mapping points to points that correspond functionally, and aggregate 3D models along with the warpings into a plug-and-play Shape Library. In the second stage, we design a pipeline that, leveraging established Libraries, requires only minimal human annotation to generate physically plausible and functionally correct novel demonstrations. Experiments in the real world demonstrate the effectiveness of ShapeGen to boost policies' in-category shape generalizability. Project page: https://wangyr22.github.io/ShapeGen/.

</details>


### [139] [GaussianFlow SLAM: Monocular Gaussian Splatting SLAM Guided by GaussianFlow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15612)
*Dong-Uk Seo,Jinwoo Jeon,Eungchang Mason Lee,Hyun Myung*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GaussianFlow SLAM：一种利用光流作为几何感知线索的单目3D高斯溅射SLAM系统，通过高斯流对齐优化场景结构和相机位姿


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高斯溅射作为SLAM系统的地图表示具有吸引力，但应用于单目SLAM时面临挑战，因为缺乏来自单目输入的可靠几何线索，容易陷入局部极小值，导致结构退化和不准确

Method: 提出GaussianFlow SLAM，利用光流作为几何感知线索指导场景结构和相机位姿优化；通过鼓励高斯投影运动（高斯流）与光流对齐，引入一致的结构线索来正则化地图重建和位姿估计；引入基于归一化误差的致密化和剪枝模块来细化非活跃和不稳定的高斯

Result: 在公开数据集上的实验表明，该方法相比最先进算法实现了更优越的渲染质量和跟踪精度

Conclusion: GaussianFlow SLAM通过光流引导的高斯溅射优化，有效解决了单目SLAM中的几何监督不足问题，提高了场景重建和相机跟踪的准确性和鲁棒性

Abstract: Gaussian splatting has recently gained traction as a compelling map representation for SLAM systems, enabling dense and photo-realistic scene modeling. However, its application to monocular SLAM remains challenging due to the lack of reliable geometric cues from monocular input. Without geometric supervision, mapping or tracking could fall in local-minima, resulting in structural degeneracies and inaccuracies. To address this challenge, we propose GaussianFlow SLAM, a monocular 3DGS-SLAM that leverages optical flow as a geometry-aware cue to guide the optimization of both the scene structure and camera poses. By encouraging the projected motion of Gaussians, termed GaussianFlow, to align with the optical flow, our method introduces consistent structural cues to regularize both map reconstruction and pose estimation. Furthermore, we introduce normalized error-based densification and pruning modules to refine inactive and unstable Gaussians, thereby contributing to improved map quality and pose accuracy. Experiments conducted on public datasets demonstrate that our method achieves superior rendering quality and tracking accuracy compared with state-of-the-art algorithms. The source code is available at: https://github.com/url-kaist/gaussianflow-slam.

</details>


### [140] [Factor Graph-Based Shape Estimation for Continuum Robots via Magnus Expansion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15619)
*Lorenzo Ticozzi,Patricio A. Vela,Panagiotis Tsiotras*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种结合低维几何参数化和因子图推理的新方法，用于从稀疏噪声传感器数据重建连续体机械臂形状，实现紧凑状态表示和概率不确定性量化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在参数化方法（紧凑但缺乏概率结构）和Cosserat杆因子图推理（提供概率不确定性量化但状态维度随空间离散化增长）之间存在权衡，需要结合两者优势。

Method: 在因子图框架内估计低维几何可变应变(GVS)参数化的系数，使用基于应变场Magnus展开推导的新型运动学因子，将GVS应变系数与骨干姿态变量连接为约束先验。

Result: 在0.4米长腱驱动连续体机器人仿真中，三种测量配置下平均位置误差均低于2毫米，仅使用位置测量时相比高斯过程回归基线方向误差减少六倍。

Conclusion: 该方法结合了紧凑状态表示和概率不确定性量化的优势，实现了直接适用于模型控制的状态向量，同时保持了因子图推理的模块化、概率处理和计算效率。

Abstract: Reconstructing the shape of continuum manipulators from sparse, noisy sensor data is a challenging task, owing to the infinite-dimensional nature of such systems. Existing approaches broadly trade off between parametric methods that yield compact state representations but lack probabilistic structure, and Cosserat rod inference on factor graphs, which provides principled uncertainty quantification at the cost of a state dimension that grows with the spatial discretization. This letter combines the strength of both paradigms by estimating the coefficients of a low-dimensional Geometric Variable Strain (GVS) parameterization within a factor graph framework. A novel kinematic factor, derived from the Magnus expansion of the strain field, encodes the closed-form rod geometry as a prior constraint linking the GVS strain coefficients to the backbone pose variables. The resulting formulation yields a compact state vector directly amenable to model-based control, while retaining the modularity, probabilistic treatment and computational efficiency of factor graph inference. The proposed method is evaluated in simulation on a 0.4 m long tendon-driven continuum robot under three measurement configurations, achieving mean position errors below 2 mm for all three scenarios and demonstrating a sixfold reduction in orientation error compared to a Gaussian process regression baseline when only position measurements are available.

</details>


### [141] [Fuzzy Logic Theory-based Adaptive Reward Shaping for Robust Reinforcement Learning (FARS)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15772)
*Hürkan Şahin,Van Huyen Dang,Erdi Sayar,Alper Yegenoglu,Erdal Kayacan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于模糊逻辑的奖励塑形方法，将人类直觉融入强化学习奖励设计，通过模糊规则编码专家知识，在无人机竞速任务中实现更稳定的学习和更好的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习在高维状态空间和长时程任务中面临挑战，稀疏或固定奖励会严重减慢探索速度并导致陷入局部最优。需要将人类直觉融入奖励设计以改善这些问题。

Method: 提出基于模糊逻辑的奖励塑形方法，将专家知识编码为自适应且可解释的模糊规则，根据智能体状态动态调整奖励贡献，实现快速运动与精确控制之间的平滑过渡。

Result: 在自主无人机竞速基准测试中，该方法展现出稳定的学习行为和一致的任务性能。在更具挑战性的环境中，相比非模糊奖励公式，收敛速度更快，性能变异性降低，成功率提升约5%。

Conclusion: 模糊逻辑奖励塑形方法通过整合人类直觉和专家知识，有效解决了强化学习在复杂任务中的探索和收敛问题，提高了学习稳定性和任务性能。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) often struggles in real-world tasks with high-dimensional state spaces and long horizons, where sparse or fixed rewards severely slow down exploration and cause agents to get trapped in local optima. This paper presents a fuzzy logic based reward shaping method that integrates human intuition into RL reward design. By encoding expert knowledge into adaptive and interpreable terms, fuzzy rules promote stable learning and reduce sensitivity to hyperparameters. The proposed method leverages these properties to adapt reward contributions based on the agent state, enabling smoother transitions between fast motion and precise control in challenging navigation tasks. Extensive simulation results on autonomous drone racing benchmarks show stable learning behavior and consistent task performance across scenarios of increasing difficulty. The proposed method achieves faster convergence and reduced performance variability across training seeds in more challenging environments, with success rates improving by up to approximately 5 percent compared to non fuzzy reward formulations.

</details>


### [142] [From Seeing to Simulating: Generative High-Fidelity Simulation with Digital Cousins for Generalizable Robot Learning and Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15805)
*Jasper Lu,Zhenhao Shen,Yuanfei Wang,Shugao Liu,Shengqiang Xu,Shawn Xie,Jingkai Xu,Feng Jiang,Jade Yang,Chen Xie,Ruihai Wu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出生成式框架，将真实世界全景图映射到高保真仿真场景，通过语义和几何编辑合成多样化"数字表亲"场景，支持交互式操作任务和长时程导航


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实世界机器人策略学习需要多样化数据增强，但真实数据收集成本高昂，需要物理资产和环境重配置。将真实场景增强到仿真中成为高效学习和评估的实用方法

Method: 建立生成式真实到仿真映射框架，从真实世界全景图生成高保真仿真场景；通过语义和几何编辑合成多样化表亲场景；结合高质量物理引擎和真实资产支持交互操作；采用多房间拼接构建一致的大规模环境用于复杂布局的长时程导航

Result: 实验显示强烈的仿真到真实相关性，验证了平台保真度；大规模数据生成显著提高了对未见场景和物体变化的泛化能力；证明了数字表亲方法对可泛化机器人学习和评估的有效性

Conclusion: 提出的生成式框架通过建立真实到仿真映射并合成多样化场景，为机器人学习提供了高效的数据增强方法，支持交互操作和长时程导航任务，显著提升了泛化能力

Abstract: Learning robust robot policies in real-world environments requires diverse data augmentation, yet scaling real-world data collection is costly due to the need for acquiring physical assets and reconfiguring environments. Therefore, augmenting real-world scenes into simulation has become a practical augmentation for efficient learning and evaluation. We present a generative framework that establishes a generative real-to-sim mapping from real-world panoramas to high-fidelity simulation scenes, and further synthesize diverse cousin scenes via semantic and geometric editing. Combined with high-quality physics engines and realistic assets, the generated scenes support interactive manipulation tasks. Additionally, we incorporate multi-room stitching to construct consistent large-scale environments for long-horizon navigation across complex layouts. Experiments demonstrate a strong sim-to-real correlation validating our platform's fidelity, and show that extensively scaling up data generation leads to significantly better generalization to unseen scene and object variations, demonstrating the effectiveness of Digital Cousins for generalizable robot learning and evaluation.

</details>


### [143] [Limits of Lamarckian Evolution Under Pressure of Morphological Novelty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15854)
*Jed R Muff,Karine Miras,A. E. Eiben*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 拉马克遗传在机器人形态-控制器协同进化中加速优化，但需要亲代-子代形态相似性；当引入形态多样性选择压力时，拉马克系统性能显著下降，揭示基于继承的利用与多样性驱动的探索之间的权衡


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究拉马克遗传在机器人形态-控制器协同进化中的局限性。虽然拉马克遗传通过子代继承亲代学习到的控制器来加速进化，但其有效性依赖于亲代与子代之间的形态相似性。本研究旨在探索当进化过程趋向高形态多样性时，拉马克遗传的表现如何，以及这种多样性压力如何影响其性能优势。

Method: 使用模块化机器人系统，在解决运动任务的背景下比较达尔文进化与拉马克进化。实验设计包括：1）纯任务性能优化；2）多目标选择压力，同时奖励形态新颖性。通过分析亲代-子代相似性变化，评估拉马克遗传在不同选择压力下的表现。

Result: 1. 在纯任务性能优化中，拉马克进化显著优于达尔文进化；2. 引入形态多样性选择压力后，两种系统性能均下降，但拉马克系统下降幅度更大；3. 促进多样性会降低亲代-子代相似性，从而削弱继承亲代学习控制器的益处；4. 揭示了基于继承的利用与多样性驱动的探索之间存在根本性权衡。

Conclusion: 拉马克遗传在机器人形态-控制器协同进化中具有加速优势，但其有效性高度依赖于亲代-子代形态相似性。当进化过程强调形态多样性时，这种相似性降低，导致拉马克遗传的优势减弱。研究揭示了进化算法中利用与探索之间的基本权衡，为设计更有效的协同进化系统提供了重要见解。

Abstract: Lamarckian inheritance has been shown to be a powerful accelerator in systems where the joint evolution of robot morphologies and controllers is enhanced with individual learning. Its defining advantage lies in the offspring inheriting controllers learned by their parents. The efficacy of this option, however, relies on morphological similarity between parent and offspring. In this study, we examine how Lamarckian inheritance performs when the search process is driven toward high morphological variance, potentially straining the requirement for parent-offspring similarity. Using a system of modular robots that can evolve and learn to solve a locomotion task, we compare Darwinian and Lamarckian evolution to determine how they respond to shifting from pure task-based selection to a multi-objective pressure that also rewards morphological novelty. Our results confirm that Lamarckian evolution outperforms Darwinian evolution when optimizing task-performance alone. However, introducing selection pressure for morphological diversity causes a substantial performance drop, which is much greater in the Lamarckian system. Further analyses show that promoting diversity reduces parent-offspring similarity, which in turn reduces the benefits of inheriting controllers learned by parents. These results reveal the limits of Lamarckian evolution by exposing a fundamental trade-off between inheritance-based exploitation and diversity-driven exploration.

</details>


### [144] [Environment-Adaptive Solid-State LiDAR-Inertial Odometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15864)
*Zhi Zhang,Chalermchon Satirapod,Bingtao Ma,Changjun Gu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种环境自适应的固态激光雷达-惯性SLAM方法，通过局部法向量约束和退化感知地图维护来提升极端环境下的定位精度和地图一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 固态激光雷达-惯性SLAM在速度和鲁棒性方面具有优势，但在极端环境中面临几何退化和不可靠观测的挑战，导致优化病态和地图不一致，需要提升在退化环境下的定位精度和地图质量。

Method: 提出环境自适应的固态激光雷达-惯性里程计，集成局部法向量约束和退化感知地图维护。局部法向量约束提升状态估计稳定性，抑制退化场景下的定位漂移；设计退化引导的地图更新策略提升地图精度。

Result: 实验结果表明，该方法在极端和感知退化环境中实现了优越的建图精度和鲁棒性，相比基线方法平均RMSE降低达12.8%。

Conclusion: 通过局部法向量约束和退化感知地图维护，提出的环境自适应固态激光雷达-惯性SLAM方法有效解决了极端环境下的几何退化和地图一致性问题，显著提升了定位精度和系统鲁棒性。

Abstract: Solid-state LiDAR-inertial SLAM has attracted significant attention due to its advantages in speed and robustness. However, achieving accurate mapping in extreme environments remains challenging due to severe geometric degeneracy and unreliable observations, which often lead to ill-conditioned optimization and map inconsistencies. To address these challenges, we propose an environment-adaptive solid-state LiDAR-inertial odometry that integrates local normal-vector constraints with degeneracy-aware map maintenance to enhance localization accuracy. Specifically, we introduce local normal-vector constraints to improve the stability of state estimation, effectively suppressing localization drift in degenerate scenarios. Furthermore, we design a degeneration-guided map update strategy to improve map precision. Benefiting from the refined map representation, localization accuracy is further enhanced in subsequent estimation. Experimental results demonstrate that the proposed method achieves superior mapping accuracy and robustness in extreme and perceptually degraded environments, with an average RMSE reduction of up to 12.8% compared to the baseline method.

</details>


### [145] [DTEA: A Dual-Topology Elastic Actuator Enabling Real-Time Switching Between Series and Parallel Compliance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15865)
*Vishal Ramesh,Aman Singh,Shishir Kolathaya*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种新型双拓扑弹性驱动器(DTEA)，可在运行期间实时在串联弹性驱动器(SEA)和并联弹性驱动器(PEA)拓扑之间动态切换，并验证了其可行性和性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 串联和并联弹性驱动器各有优势但相互排斥，现有驱动器无法在运行期间实现这两种拓扑之间的实时切换，因此需要开发能够动态切换拓扑的新型驱动器。

Method: 设计了双拓扑弹性驱动器(DTEA)原型，开发了拓扑切换机制，通过实验评估切换机制的鲁棒性和时序特性，并测试了两种模式下的静态刚度和抗干扰性能。

Result: 原型成功完成324次负载下的拓扑切换无损坏，切换时间小于33.33ms；PEA模式比SEA模式刚度高1.53倍(8.54 vs 5.57 Nm/rad)；SEA模式的平均峰值偏转是PEA模式的2.26倍(5.2° vs 2.3°)，平均稳定时间是其3.45倍(1380ms vs 400ms)。

Conclusion: DTEA成功实现了运行期间SEA和PEA拓扑的动态切换，两种模式表现出与传统驱动器一致的特征，验证了该设计的可行性和功能性。

Abstract: Series and parallel elastic actuators offer complementary but mutually exclusive advantages, yet no existing actuator enables real-time transition between these topologies during operation. This paper presents a novel actuator design called the Dual-Topology Elastic Actuator (DTEA), which enables dynamic switching between SEA and PEA topologies during operation. A proof-of-concept prototype of the DTEA is developed to demonstrate the feasibility of the topology-switching mechanism. Experiments are conducted to evaluate the robustness and timing of the switching mechanism under operational conditions. The actuator successfully performed 324 topology-switching cycles under load without damage, demonstrating the robustness of the mechanism. The measured switching time between SEA and PEA modes is under 33.33 ms. Additional experiments are conducted to characterize the static stiffness and disturbance rejection performance in both SEA and PEA modes. Static stiffness tests show that the PEA mode is 1.53x stiffer than the SEA mode, with KSEA = 5.57 +/- 0.02 Nm/rad and KPEA = 8.54 +/- 0.02 Nm/rad. Disturbance rejection experiments show that the mean peak deflection in SEA mode is 2.26x larger than in PEA mode (5.2 deg vs. 2.3 deg), while the mean settling time is 3.45x longer (1380 ms vs. 400 ms). The observed behaviors are consistent with the known characteristics of conventional SEA and PEA actuators, validating the functionality of both modes in the DTEA actuator.

</details>


### [146] [Robust Fleet Sizing for Multi-UAV Inspection Missions under Synchronized Replacement Demand](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15890)
*Vishal Ramesh,Antony Thomas*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对多无人机巡检任务，提出考虑同步电池耗尽风险的机队规模确定方法，确保任务级可靠性而非单次请求阻塞率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机队规模确定方法假设稳态条件或独立请求，忽略了高效路由导致无人机工作负载相似，从而引发同步电池耗尽和替换需求集中爆发的问题，即使平均容量足够也可能耗尽备用无人机池。

Method: 推导出闭式充分机队规模规则 k = m(ceil(R) + 1)，其中m为活跃无人机数量，R为恢复时间与活跃时间之比。该规则添加m个备用缓冲，吸收恢复周期边界处最坏情况同步需求。通过蒙特卡洛模拟在五种场景下验证。

Result: Erlang-B方法在R=3.39时任务成功率降至69.9%，95%的备用耗尽事件集中在需求最高的5分钟窗口内。而提出的规则在所有测试条件下保持99.8%成功率（Wilson 95%下限99.3%），在最苛刻场景下仅需额外4架无人机。

Conclusion: 考虑同步电池耗尽的结构性失效模式对于确保多无人机巡检任务可靠性至关重要。提出的机队规模规则通过添加m个备用缓冲有效应对最坏情况需求，显著提升任务成功率，且成本增加有限。

Abstract: Multi-UAV inspection missions require spare drones to replace active drones during recharging cycles. Existing fleet-sizing approaches often assume steady-state operating conditions that do not apply to finite-horizon missions, or they treat replacement requests as statistically independent events. The latter provides per-request blocking guarantees that fail to translate to mission-level reliability when demands cluster. This paper identifies a structural failure mode where efficient routing assigns similar workloads to each UAV, leading to synchronized battery depletion and replacement bursts that exhaust the spare pool even when average capacity is sufficient.
  We derive a closed-form sufficient fleet-sizing rule, k = m(ceil(R) + 1), where m is the number of active UAVs and R is the recovery-to-active time ratio. This additive buffer of m spares absorbs worst-case synchronized demand at recovery-cycle boundaries and ensures mission-level reliability even when all UAVs deplete simultaneously. Monte Carlo validation across five scenarios (m in [2, 10], R in [0.87, 3.39], 1000 trials each) shows that Erlang-B sizing with a per-request blocking target epsilon = 0.01 drops to 69.9% mission success at R = 3.39, with 95% of spare exhaustion events concentrated in the top-decile 5-minute demand windows. In contrast, the proposed rule maintains 99.8% success (Wilson 95% lower bound 99.3%) across all tested conditions, including wind variability up to CV = 0.30, while requiring only four additional drones in the most demanding scenario.

</details>


### [147] [A Reconfigurable Pneumatic Joint Enabling Localized Selective Stiffening and Shape Locking in Vine-Inspired Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15907)
*Ayodele James Oyejide,Ustaz A. Yaqub,Samir Erturk,Eray A. Baran,Fabio Stroppa*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出可重构气动关节架构，解决藤蔓机器人轴向刚度低、负载能力差和转向后形状保持问题，实现局部刚度可调而不影响连续生长


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 藤蔓机器人通过尖端外翻实现大工作空间覆盖，但在自由空间中部署受到轴向刚度低、负载能力差以及转向期间和转向后无法保持形状的根本限制

Method: 提出可重构气动关节架构，由对称分布的气动腔室组成，加压时局部增加弯曲刚度；集成到具有肌腱驱动转向的软体生长机器人中，开发紧凑基站用于空中外翻

Result: 系统表征和实验验证显示适中的外翻压力需求，局部刚化和转向性能与层压机制相当；机器人实现弯曲时改进的形状保持、负载下减少的重力偏转、级联收缩，以及在自由空间中可靠运输高达202克的有效载荷

Conclusion: 可重构气动关节机制为结构自适应藤蔓机器人建立了实用途径，适用于物体分类和不受约束环境中的自适应探索等操作导向任务

Abstract: Vine-inspired robots achieve large workspace coverage through tip eversion, enabling safe navigation in confined and cluttered environments. However, their deployment in free space is fundamentally limited by low axial stiffness, poor load-bearing capacity, and the inability to retain shape during and after steering. In this work, we propose a reconfigurable pneumatic joint (RPJ) architecture that introduces discrete, pressure-tunable stiffness along the robot body without compromising continuous growth. Each RPJ module comprises symmetrically distributed pneumatic chambers that locally increase bending stiffness when pressurized, enabling decoupling between global compliance and localized rigidity. We integrate the RPJs into a soft growing robot with tendon-driven steering and develop a compact base station for mid-air eversion. System characterization and experimental validation demonstrate moderate pressure requirements for eversion, as well as comparable localized stiffening and steering performance to layer-jamming mechanisms. Demonstrations further show that the proposed robot achieves improved shape retention during bending, reduced gravitational deflection under load, cascading retraction, and reliable payload transport up to 202 g in free space. The RPJ mechanism establishes a practical pathway toward structurally adaptive vine robots for manipulation-oriented tasks such as object sorting and adaptive exploration in unconstrained environments.

</details>


### [148] [DENALI: A Dataset Enabling Non-Line-of-Sight Spatial Reasoning with Low-Cost LiDARs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16201)
*Nikhil Behari,Diego Rivero,Luke Apostolides,Suman Ghosh,Paul Pu Liang,Ramesh Raskar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出DENALI数据集，首次大规模采集低成本LiDAR的时空直方图数据，用于数据驱动的非视距感知研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 消费级LiDAR内部记录包含直接和多跳光返回的完整时间分辨直方图，这些多跳返回编码了丰富的非视距线索，但硬件限制使得传统方法难以进行NLOS重建。

Method: 创建DENALI数据集，采集72,000个隐藏物体场景的时间分辨LiDAR直方图，涵盖不同物体形状、位置、光照条件和空间分辨率。

Result: 证明消费级LiDAR能够实现准确的数据驱动非视距感知，识别了限制性能的关键场景和建模因素，以及当前仿真到真实迁移存在的保真度差距。

Conclusion: 为未来使用消费级LiDAR实现可扩展的非视距视觉研究奠定了基础，指出了仿真保真度差距是需要解决的关键问题。

Abstract: Consumer LiDARs in mobile devices and robots typically output a single depth value per pixel. Yet internally, they record full time-resolved histograms containing direct and multi-bounce light returns; these multi-bounce returns encode rich non-line-of-sight (NLOS) cues that can enable perception of hidden objects in a scene. However, severe hardware limitations of consumer LiDARs make NLOS reconstruction with conventional methods difficult. In this work, we motivate a complementary direction: enabling NLOS perception with low-cost LiDARs through data-driven inference. We present DENALI, the first large-scale real-world dataset of space-time histograms from low-cost LiDARs capturing hidden objects. We capture time-resolved LiDAR histograms for 72,000 hidden-object scenes across diverse object shapes, positions, lighting conditions, and spatial resolutions. Using our dataset, we show that consumer LiDARs can enable accurate, data-driven NLOS perception. We further identify key scene and modeling factors that limit performance, as well as simulation-fidelity gaps that hinder current sim-to-real transfer, motivating future work toward scalable NLOS vision with consumer LiDARs.

</details>


### [149] [Semantic Area Graph Reasoning for Multi-Robot Language-Guided Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16263)
*Ruiyang Wang,Hao-Lun Hsu,Jiwoo Kim,Miroslav Pajic*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SAGR是一个分层框架，利用大型语言模型通过语义区域图协调多机器人探索和语义搜索，在保持探索竞争力的同时显著提高语义目标搜索效率


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统多机器人协调策略依赖于边界覆盖或信息增益，无法融入高层任务意图（如搜索特定房间类型的关联对象）。需要一种能够结合语义推理的协调方法

Method: 提出语义区域图推理（SAGR）分层框架：从语义占据地图增量构建语义区域图，编码房间实例、连通性、边界可用性和机器人状态；LLM基于空间结构和任务上下文进行高层语义房间分配；确定性边界规划和局部导航处理几何执行

Result: 在Habitat-Matterport3D数据集的100个场景中，SAGR保持与最先进探索方法的竞争力，同时持续提高语义目标搜索效率，在大型环境中提升达18.8%

Conclusion: 结构化语义抽象作为LLM推理与多机器人协调之间的有效接口，在复杂室内环境中具有重要价值

Abstract: Coordinating multi-robot systems (MRS) to search in unknown environments is particularly challenging for tasks that require semantic reasoning beyond geometric exploration. Classical coordination strategies rely on frontier coverage or information gain and cannot incorporate high-level task intent, such as searching for objects associated with specific room types. We propose \textit{Semantic Area Graph Reasoning} (SAGR), a hierarchical framework that enables Large Language Models (LLMs) to coordinate multi-robot exploration and semantic search through a structured semantic-topological abstraction of the environment. SAGR incrementally constructs a semantic area graph from a semantic occupancy map, encoding room instances, connectivity, frontier availability, and robot states into a compact task-relevant representation for LLM reasoning. The LLM performs high-level semantic room assignment based on spatial structure and task context, while deterministic frontier planning and local navigation handle geometric execution within assigned rooms. Experiments on the Habitat-Matterport3D dataset across 100 scenarios show that SAGR remains competitive with state-of-the-art exploration methods while consistently improving semantic target search efficiency, with up to 18.8\% in large environments. These results highlight the value of structured semantic abstractions as an effective interface between LLM-based reasoning and multi-robot coordination in complex indoor environments.

</details>


<div id='cs.DB'></div>

# cs.DB [[Back]](#toc)

### [150] [SAGE: Selective Attention-Guided Extraction for Token-Efficient](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15583)
*Xinzhi Wang,Peter Baile Chen,Gerardo Vitagliano,Matthew Russo,Jun Chen,Michael Cafarella,Samuel Madden,Chunwei Liu*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: SAGE是一种无需训练、即插即用的上下文缩减框架，利用轻量级本地LLM的注意力信号生成查询特定的相关性热图，在保持准确性的同时减少90%的tokens。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文窗口LLM虽然能直接从完整文档回答问题，但传递整个文档成本高、速度慢，可能降低答案质量并增加数据泄露风险。传统固定位置启发式方法和标准RAG在处理异构问题时，由于文档结构多变和查询-块语义相似性弱，常常需要任务和领域特定的嵌入检索器调优。

Method: 提出SAGE（选择性注意力引导提取）框架：1）使用轻量级本地LLM进行单次预填充，将语言模型注意力信号转换为可配置粒度的查询特定相关性热图；2）引入差分注意力策略更好地隔离问题相关证据；3）在用户定义的token预算下选择最高得分的单元，仅将此缩减后的上下文传递给下游LLM生成答案。

Result: SAGE在多个长文档QA基准测试中超越了传统缩减技术，特别是在QuALITY-hard基准上，在仅使用10%上下文预算的情况下获得了前4名的排名。实现了90%的token减少同时保持竞争力准确性，无需模型微调或复杂校准。

Conclusion: SAGE提供了一种有效的训练免费上下文缩减解决方案，通过利用LLM自身的注意力机制来识别查询相关证据，显著降低了长文档问答的计算成本和潜在风险，同时保持了回答质量。

Abstract: Large language models with long context windows can answer complex questions directly from full-length academic, technical, and policy documents, but passing entire documents is often costly, slow, and can degrade answer quality while increasing the risk of unnecessary data leakage. This paper targets the common setting of answering many heterogeneous questions over long document(s), where fixed position heuristics and standard retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) can fail due to document structure variability and weak query-chunk semantic similarity, which often requires task- and domain-specific tuning of embedding retrievers. We propose {Selective Attention-Guided Extraction} (\ourmethod), a training-free, plug-and-play context reduction framework that uses a lightweight local LLM to perform a single prefilling pass and convert language model attention signals into a query-specific relevance heatmap at configurable granularities. \ourmethod\ further introduces \emph{differential attention} strategies to better isolate question-relevant evidence, then selects the top-scoring units under a user-defined token budget and forwards only this reduced context to a downstream LLM for answer generation. \ourmethod\ surpasses traditional reduction techniques across multiple long-document QA benchmarks, notably securing a top-4 rank on QuALITY-hard while constrained to a 10\% context budget. This enables a 90\% reduction in tokens with competitive accuracy, without the need for model fine-tuning or complex calibration.

</details>


### [151] [EvoRAG: Making Knowledge Graph-based RAG Automatically Evolve through Feedback-driven Backpropagation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15676)
*Zhenbo Fu,Yuanzhe Zhang,Qiange Wang,Hao Yuan,Yuehao Xu,Enze Yi,Yanfeng Zhang,Ge Yu*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: EvoRAG是一个自演化的知识图谱增强检索生成框架，通过响应反馈持续优化知识图谱，提升推理准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有KG-RAG框架在真实场景中表现不佳，因为预捕获的知识依赖关系未针对下游任务或其演化需求进行定制。这些框架难以适应任务特定要求，缺乏在生成过程中过滤低贡献知识的机制。响应反馈提供了有效的监督信号，但将响应级反馈链接到三元组级贡献评估和知识更新是一个关键挑战。

Method: 提出EvoRAG框架，引入反馈驱动的反向传播机制：1) 将反馈归因到检索路径，通过测量其对响应的效用；2) 将此效用传播回单个三元组；3) 支持细粒度知识图谱优化，实现更自适应和准确的推理。建立反馈、LLM和图数据的闭环系统。

Result: 实验结果显示，EvoRAG相比最先进的KG-RAG框架，推理准确率提高了7.34%。

Conclusion: EvoRAG通过建立反馈驱动的知识图谱演化机制，解决了现有KG-RAG框架在真实场景中的适应性问题，实现了持续的性能提升和鲁棒性增强。

Abstract: Knowledge Graph-based Retrieval-Augmented Generation (KG-RAG) has emerged as a promising paradigm for enhancing LLM reasoning by retrieving multi-hop paths from KGs. However, existing KG-RAG frameworks often underperform in real-world scenarios because the pre-captured knowledge dependencies are not tailored to the downstream task or its evolving requirements. These frameworks struggle to adapt to task-specific requirements and lack mechanisms to filter low-contribution knowledge during generation. We observe that feedback on generated responses offers effective supervision for improving KG quality, as it directly reflects user expectations and provides insights into the correctness and usefulness of the output. However, a key challenge lies in effectively linking response-level feedback to triplet-level contribution evaluation and knowledge updates in the KG.
  In this work, we propose EvoRAG, a self-evolving KG-RAG framework that leverages the feedback over generated responses to continuously refine the KG and enhance reasoning accuracy. EvoRAG introduces a feedback-driven backpropagation mechanism that attributes feedback to retrieved paths by measuring their utility for response and propagates this utility back to individual triplets, supporting fine-grained KG refinements towards more adaptive and accurate reasoning. Through EvoRAG, we establish a closed loop that couples feedback, LLM, and graph data, continuously enhancing the performance and robustness in real-world scenarios. Experimental results show that EvoRAG improves reasoning accuracy by $7.34\%$ over state-of-the-art KG-RAG frameworks. The source code has been made available at https://github.com/iDC-NEU/EvoRAG.

</details>


### [152] [Exploring Agentic Visual Analytics: A Co-Evolutionary Framework of Roles and Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15813)
*Tianqi Luo,Leixian Shen,Yuyu Luo*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文对55个智能体视觉分析系统进行了全面调查，提出了一个协同进化框架，分析智能体自主性提升与人类角色转变之间的关系，并建立了角色-工作流分类法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体视觉分析系统近年来快速发展，但设计格局尚未被充分理解。需要解决两个核心问题：自主智能体如何重塑传统视觉分析流程，以及随着智能体自主性增加，人类参与应如何适应。

Method: 对55个主要智能体视觉分析系统进行全面调查，引入协同进化框架，建立角色-工作流分类法，将四个关键智能体角色（规划者、创建者、评审者、上下文管理者）映射到视觉分析流程阶段，并分析三个基础轴上的权衡。

Result: 提出了协同进化框架和角色-工作流分类法，揭示了智能体自主性、角色和视觉分析工作流之间的权衡关系，并整合为可操作的设计指南。创建了基于Web的交互式浏览器展示框架、语料库和设计指南。

Conclusion: 智能体视觉分析系统正在改变人机交互方式，需要协同考虑智能体自主性与人类角色转变。提出的框架和分类法为系统设计提供了理论基础，设计指南和未来研究方向将推动该领域发展。

Abstract: Agentic visual analytics (VA) represents an emerging class of systems in which large language model (LLM)-driven agents autonomously plan, execute, evaluate, and iterate across the full visual analytics pipeline. By shifting users from low-level tool operations to high-level analytical goals expressed through natural language, these systems are fundamentally transforming how humans interact with data. However, the rapid proliferation of such systems in recent years has outpaced our understanding of their design landscape. Two intertwined problems remain open: how do autonomous agents reshape the traditional VA pipeline, and how must human involvement adapt as agent autonomy increases? To address these questions, this paper presents a comprehensive survey of 55 primary agentic VA systems and introduces a co-evolutionary framework. This framework is essential because it jointly analyzes the progression of agent autonomy alongside the necessary shift in human roles from manual operators to strategic supervisors. Within this framework, we define a role-workflow taxonomy that aligns four key agentic roles (PLANNER, CREATOR, REVIEWER, and CONTEXT MANAGER) and maps them onto established VA pipeline stages. Our analysis uncovers recurring trade-offs along three foundational axes: autonomy levels, agentic roles, and the VA workflow. We consolidate these findings into actionable design guidelines and outline future research directions for agentic visual analytics. A web-based interactive browser of our co-evolutionary framework, including the corpus and design guidelines, is available at agenticva.github.io/AgenticVA/.

</details>


### [153] [Compliance in Databases: A Study of Structural Policies and Query Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15861)
*Ahana Pradhan,Srinivas Karthik,Imtiyazuddin Shaik,Srinivas Vivek*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 论文研究了内容型访问控制策略对数据库查询优化和性能的影响，提出了结构化框架和策略语法，并通过实验验证策略结构对优化器行为和性能的决定性影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 隐私法规和内部治理需求推动了对细粒度、上下文敏感的访问控制的需求，其中基于内容的访问控制日益重要。虽然简单的基于内容的谓词通常开销可忽略，但复杂的策略会与查询优化产生微妙交互，导致显著且难以理解的性能变化。

Method: 提出了一个结构化框架和表达性策略语法，用于建模基于内容的合规策略并分析其对数据库查询规划和执行的影响。在此基础上，用结构化策略工作负载增强分析基准，实现对查询-策略组合工作负载下执行机制和优化策略的受控评估。

Result: 实验结果表明，策略结构对优化器行为和端到端性能具有决定性影响，强调了需要设计策略感知的数据库和优化器。

Conclusion: 基于内容的访问控制策略的结构特性会显著影响数据库查询优化和性能，需要数据库系统设计时考虑策略感知的优化机制。

Abstract: Growing privacy regulations and internal governance mandates are driving demand for fine-grained, context-sensitive access control in data management systems. Among competing approaches, content-based access control -- where access decisions depend on the data values referenced by a query -- is becoming particularly prominent, and is supported directly in modern database engines. While simple content-based predicates often incur negligible overhead, increasingly rich policies can interact in subtle ways with query optimization, leading to significant and poorly understood performance variability. This paper investigates this gap by introducing a structural framework and expressive policy grammar for modelling content-based compliance policies and analysing their impact on query planning and execution in database systems. Building on this framework, we augment an analytical benchmark with structured policy workloads, enabling controlled evaluation of enforcement mechanisms and optimization strategies under combined query - policy workloads. Our experimental results show that policy structure has a decisive impact on optimizer behaviour and end-to-end performance, underscoring the need for policy-aware database and optimizer design.

</details>
